# 植物

**plant**
英/美 /plɑːnt/
n. 植物；工厂（另义）
v. 种植；安放
[义] A living thing that grows in earth or water, usually with leaves, and cannot move by itself. 在土或水中生长、通常有叶、不能自行移动的生命体（indoor plants 室内植物，flowering plants 开花植物，plant a tree 植树）。可数/不可数。动词 plant 指栽种或固定放置。名词 plant 亦指「工厂」，依语境区分。
[例] ① She watered the plants on the balcony every morning. 她每天早晨给阳台上的植物浇水。 ② Volunteers planted hundreds of trees along the riverbank. 志愿者在河岸种植了数百棵树。
[搭] indoor plants 室内植物, flowering plants 开花植物, plant a tree 植树, plant seeds 播种, power plant 发电厂（另义）
[联] tree 树, flower 花, grass 草, grow 生长, garden 花园, crop 作物, vegetation 植被, animal 动物
[记] 可数/不可数。plant 作「工厂」时（a car plant 汽车厂）与「植物」同形，须看语境

**vegetation**
英/美 /ˌvedʒəˈteɪʃn/
n. 植被；草木
[义] Plants in general, especially those growing in a particular area. 植物总称，尤指某一地区的草木（dense vegetation 茂密植被，tropical vegetation 热带植被，loss of vegetation 植被丧失）。不可数。
[例] ① The trail disappeared into thick vegetation. 小径没入茂密的草木之中。 ② Fire destroyed much of the coastal vegetation. 大火烧毁了大部分沿海植被。
[搭] dense vegetation 茂密植被, tropical vegetation 热带植被, loss of vegetation 植被丧失, native vegetation 本地植被
[联] plant 植物, forest 森林, flora 植物群, foliage 叶丛, jungle 丛林, habitat 栖息地, wildlife 野生动植物
[记] 不可数。比 plants 更偏生态、地理描述；口语说 plants 更自然

**flora**
英/美 /ˈflɔːrə/
n. 植物群；植物区系
[义] All the plants of a particular region or period. 特定地区或时期的所有植物（local flora 当地植物群，native flora 本地植物区系，flora and fauna 动植物群）。不可数/集合名词。与 fauna（动物区系）成对。
[例] ① The guide pointed out rare species in the island's flora. 向导指出该岛植物区系中的稀有物种。 ② Climate change is altering alpine flora across Europe. 气候变化正在改变欧洲高山植物区系。
[搭] local flora 当地植物群, native flora 本地植物区系, flora and fauna 动植物群, alpine flora 高山植物区系
[联] fauna 动物群, vegetation 植被, species 物种, ecosystem 生态系统, wildlife 野生动植物, habitat 栖息地, botanical 植物学的
[记] 不可数/集合名词。fauna 在动物章；flora 指植物区系，科学/旅游文本常见

**foliage**
英/美 /ˈfəʊliɪdʒ/
n. 叶丛
[义] The leaves of a plant or tree, considered as a group. 植物或树木的叶子总称（dense foliage 密叶, autumn foliage 秋叶, green foliage 绿叶）。不可数。比 leaves 更偏整体、书面。
[例] ① Sunlight filtered through the green foliage above the path. 阳光透过小径上方的绿叶洒下来。 ② Tourists drive north each fall to see the autumn foliage. 游客每年秋天驱车北上观赏秋叶。
[搭] dense foliage 密叶, autumn foliage 秋叶, green foliage 绿叶, tropical foliage 热带叶丛
[联] leaf 叶子, tree 树, forest 森林, shade 荫凉, branch 树枝, plant 植物, vegetation 植被
[记] 不可数。比 leaves 更书面、偏整体观感；旅游广告 autumn foliage 极常见

**botanical**
英/美 /bəˈtænɪkl/
adj. 植物学的；来自植物的
[义] Relating to plants or the scientific study of plants. 与植物或植物学相关的（botanical garden 植物园, botanical name 学名, botanical illustration 植物图鉴）。只作形容词。
[例] ① The city opened a new botanical garden near the river. 该市在河边新建了一座植物园。 ② She consulted a botanical guide to identify the wildflowers. 她查阅植物图鉴辨认野花。
[形] botany /ˈbɒtəni/ 植物学（不可数）, botanist 植物学家
[搭] botanical garden 植物园, botanical name 学名, botanical illustration 植物图鉴, botanical research 植物学研究
[联] plant 植物, flora 植物群, species 物种, garden 花园, science 科学, herb 草本植物, flower 花
[记] 只作形容词。botanical garden 日常说法；勿与 biology（生物学）混为一谈

**herb**
英/美 /hɜːb/
n. 草本植物；药草；香草
[义] A plant whose leaves, seeds, or flowers are used for flavour, medicine, or scent. 叶、籽或花可用于调味、入药或取香的植物（fresh herbs 新鲜香草, medicinal herbs 药草, herb garden 香草园）。可数。亦指「非木本的草本植物」。与 spice（多为种子、树皮）略分：herb 多指叶类。
[例] ① She snipped fresh herbs from the window box for the soup. 她从窗台花箱剪下新鲜香草入汤。 ② Traditional medicine relies on many local herbs. 传统医学依赖多种本地药草。
[搭] fresh herbs 新鲜香草, medicinal herbs 药草, herb garden 香草园, dried herbs 干香草, herb tea 草药茶
[联] spice 香料, plant 植物, garden 花园, basil 罗勒, mint 薄荷, rosemary 迷迭香, shrub 灌木
[记] 可数：an herb（美式常发 /ɜːrb/）。herb 亦指「草本植物」与 tree 相对；烹饪语境多指香草

**shrub**
英/美 /ʃrʌb/
n. 灌木
[义] A small bush with several woody stems growing from the ground. 从地面长出多根木质茎的小型灌木（flowering shrubs 开花灌木, evergreen shrub 常绿灌木）。可数。
[例] ① They planted low shrubs along the fence for privacy. 他们沿篱笆种植矮灌木以遮挡视线。 ② The garden is full of roses and other flowering shrubs. 花园里种满了玫瑰和其他开花灌木。
[搭] flowering shrubs 开花灌木, evergreen shrub 常绿灌木, shrub border 灌木带, prune shrubs 修剪灌木
[联] bush 灌木丛, tree 树, hedge 树篱, garden 花园, woody 木质的, plant 植物, prune 修剪
[记] 可数：a shrub。shrub 比 bush 略正式；landscaping 文本常用

**bush**
英/美 /bʊʃ/
n. 灌木；灌木丛
[义] A plant with many small branches growing close to the ground; wild uncultivated land covered with such plants. 贴近地面、多枝的小灌木；亦指未开垦的灌丛地（rose bush 玫瑰丛, bush fire 灌木林火〔澳〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Birds nested in the thick bush near the pond. 鸟在池塘边的密灌丛中筑巢。 ② He disappeared into the bush and was not seen for hours. 他钻进灌丛，数小时不见踪影。
[搭] rose bush 玫瑰丛, bush fire 灌木林火, beat around the bush 拐弯抹角（习语）, bush country 灌丛地带
[联] shrub 灌木, tree 树, branch 树枝, wild 野生的, hedge 树篱, thicket 灌木丛, garden 花园
[记] 可数/不可数。习语 beat around the bush 意为「绕圈子不说正题」。the bush 在澳新常指偏远灌丛地带

**vine**
英/美 /vaɪn/
n. 藤本植物；葡萄藤
[义] A climbing or trailing plant with long stems; especially a grapevine. 茎长、攀缘或匍匐的植物；尤指葡萄藤（grape vine 葡萄藤, ivy vine 常春藤, vine leaves 葡萄叶）。可数。
[例] ① Grape vines covered the hillside terraces. 葡萄藤覆盖了山坡梯田。 ② The vine climbed up the old stone wall. 藤本沿旧石墙向上攀爬。
[搭] grape vine 葡萄藤, climbing vine 攀缘藤, vine leaves 葡萄叶, vine yard 葡萄园（vineyard）
[联] grape 葡萄, climb 攀爬, plant 植物, orchard 果园, wine 葡萄酒, ivy 常春藤, trellis 棚架
[记] 可数：a vine。vineyard（葡萄园）由 vine + yard 构成；vine 在葡萄酒产区文本极常见

**weed**
英/美 /wiːd/
n. 杂草
v. 除杂草
[义] A wild plant growing where it is not wanted, especially among crops or in a garden. 在不希望出现处生长的野生植物，尤指混在作物或花园里的（pull weeds 拔草, weed killer 除草剂）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She spent the afternoon pulling weeds from the vegetable beds. 她花了一下午从菜床拔草。 ② The lawn was overrun with weeds after the rainy season. 雨季过后草坪杂草丛生。
[搭] pull weeds 拔草, weed killer 除草剂, weed the garden 给花园除杂草, invasive weed 入侵杂草
[联] grass 草, garden 花园, plant 植物, herbicide 除草剂, crop 作物, wild 野生的, flower 花
[记] 可数/动词。weed out 喻指「剔除、淘汰」（weed out errors 剔除错误）

**tree**
英/美 /triː/
n. 树；树木
[义] A tall plant with a wooden trunk, branches, and leaves. 有木质树干、树枝和叶的高大植物（fruit tree 果树, pine tree 松树, family tree 家谱〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① An old oak tree shaded the picnic tables in the park. 公园里一张老橡树荫蔽着野餐桌。 ② They planted a tree for each newborn in the village. 村里每有新生儿就种一棵树。
[搭] fruit tree 果树, pine tree 松树, tree trunk 树干, climb a tree 爬树, family tree 家谱
[联] forest 森林, leaf 叶子, branch 树枝, bark 树皮, wood 木材, plant 植物, shade 荫凉
[记] 可数：a tree。习语 can't see the forest for the trees 见林不见树（喻因细节忽略大局）

**flower**
英/美 /ˈflaʊə(r)/
n. 花；花朵
v. 开花
[义] The coloured part of a plant from which seeds develop; to produce flowers. 植物上发育为种子的彩色部分；开花（wild flowers 野花, cut flowers 切花, flower shop 花店）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Bright flowers lined the path to the cottage. 通往小屋的小径两侧开满鲜艳的花。 ② The cherry trees flower in early spring. 樱桃树早春开花。
[搭] wild flowers 野花, cut flowers 切花, flower shop 花店, flower garden 花园, in flower 开花中
[联] petal 花瓣, bloom 开花, blossom 花朵, plant 植物, garden 花园, bee 蜜蜂, pollen 花粉
[记] 可数/动词。flower 亦可喻「精华、盛期」（the flower of youth 青春年华）

**grass**
英/美 /ɡrɑːs/
n. 草；草地
[义] A common plant with narrow green leaves that covers lawns and fields. 叶窄而绿、覆盖草坪与田野的常见植物（cut the grass 割草, tall grass 高草, grassland 草原）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Children rolled down the soft grass on the hill. 孩子们在山上柔软的草地上打滚。 ② The path was hidden by knee-high grass. 小径被及膝高的草遮住了。
[搭] cut the grass 割草, tall grass 高草, grass lawn 草皮, blade of grass 一根草叶, grassland 草原
[联] lawn 草坪, meadow 草地, weed 杂草, field 田野, green 绿色的, cattle 牛, graze 啃食
[记] 不可数/可数。the grass is always greener 喻「这山望着那山高」。grass 俚语可指大麻，依语境区分

**moss**
英/美 /mɒs/
n. 苔藓；青苔
[义] A small green or yellow plant without flowers that grows in damp places on rocks, trees, or soil. 无花的小型绿或黄植物，生于潮湿处的岩石、树干或土壤（moss-covered 苔藓覆盖的, rolling stone gathers no moss 滚石不生苔〔谚〕）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Moss grew thick on the north side of the old wall. 老墙朝北的一面长满了厚苔。 ② The forest floor was soft with moss and fallen leaves. 林地被苔藓和落叶铺得松软。
[搭] moss-covered 苔藓覆盖的, green moss 绿苔, moss on rocks 岩石上的苔藓, peat moss 泥炭藓
[联] damp 潮湿的, forest 森林, stone 石头, fern 蕨类, lichen 地衣, wet 湿的, shade 荫凉
[记] 不可数/可数。谚语 A rolling stone gathers no moss 喻「漂泊不定难积累」

**fern**
英/美 /fɜːn/
n. 蕨类植物；蕨
[义] A plant with feathery green leaves and no flowers, reproducing by spores. 羽状绿叶、无花、以孢子繁殖的植物（tree fern 树蕨, fern frond 蕨叶）。可数。
[例] ① Ferns thrived in the shady corner of the garden. 蕨类在花园荫蔽角落长得茂盛。 ② She placed a potted fern on the bookshelf. 她在书架上摆了一盆蕨。
[搭] tree fern 树蕨, fern frond 蕨叶, potted fern 盆栽蕨, fern grove
[联] plant 植物, leaf 叶子, shade 荫凉, forest 森林, moss 苔藓, spore 孢子, tropical 热带的
[记] 可数：a fern。fern 常作室内观叶植物；与 palm 不同，无木质主干

**algae**
英/美 /ˈældʒiː/
n. 藻类；海藻
（单数 alga /ˈælɡə/）
[义] Simple plants or plant-like organisms that live in water or damp places, including seaweed. 生活在水或潮湿环境的简单植物或类植物生物，含海藻（algae bloom 藻华, algae growth 藻类生长）。复数形式；单数 alga 较少单独使用。
[例] ① Warm weather caused an algae bloom in the lake. 暖天导致湖中藻华爆发。 ② The pool walls were green with algae. 泳池壁长满绿藻。
[搭] algae bloom 藻华, algae growth 藻类生长, green algae 绿藻, algae in the pool 池内藻类
[联] water 水, pond 池塘, seaweed 海藻, plant 植物, bloom 爆发, ecosystem 生态系统, pool 泳池
[记] 通常作复数 algae；单数 alga 学术语境偶见。algae bloom 为环保与新闻高频搭配

**mushroom**
英/美 /ˈmʃrʊm/
n. 蘑菇；伞菌
v. 迅速增长；采蘑菇
[义] A fungus with a stem and cap, some kinds edible and others poisonous. 有茎有盖的真菌，有的可食、有的有毒（wild mushrooms 野蘑菇, mushroom soup 蘑菇汤, poisonous mushroom 毒蘑菇）。可数/动词。生物学上非植物，但日常与植物并提。
[例] ① Only experts should pick wild mushrooms in the forest. 只有专家才应在林中采野菇。 ② New cafés mushroomed across the neighbourhood last year. 去年街区里咖啡馆雨后春笋般增多。
[搭] wild mushrooms 野蘑菇, mushroom soup 蘑菇汤, poisonous mushroom 毒蘑菇, mushroom cloud 蘑菇云（喻）
[联] fungus 真菌, forest 森林, edible 可食用的, poisonous 有毒的, toadstool 毒伞（旧称）, pick 采摘, soup 汤
[记] 可数/动词。动词 mushroom 喻「迅速扩散」。毒蘑菇识别错误可致命，写作须强调 caution

**crop**
英/美 /krɒp/
n. 作物；庄稼；收成
v. 种植；剪裁
[义] A plant grown in large amounts for food or other use; the amount harvested. 为食用或其他用途大面积种植的作物；收成（food crops 粮食作物, crop rotation 轮作, main crop 主粮）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Farmers rotated crops to keep the soil healthy. 农民轮作以保持土壤健康。 ② This year's wheat crop was smaller than expected. 今年小麦收成低于预期。
[搭] food crops 粮食作物, crop rotation 轮作, main crop 主粮, cash crop 经济作物, crop failure 歉收
[联] harvest 收获, farm 农场, wheat 小麦, rice 稻, field 田野, sow 播种, yield 产量
[记] 可数/动词。crop 作动词亦指「裁剪（照片、头发）」——多义。作物物种名在本章，食材烹饪义在食物章

**root**
英/美 /ruːt/
n. 根；根源
v. 生根；翻找
[义] The part of a plant that grows underground and takes in water and nutrients. 植物地下生长、吸收水分与养分的部分（tree roots 树根, root system 根系, deep roots 深根）。可数/动词。动词 root 亦指「翻找、支持」——多义。
[例] ① The storm uprooted several trees along the street. 暴风雨把沿街的数棵树连根拔起。 ② Carrots and beets are root vegetables. 胡萝卜和甜菜是根菜类蔬菜。
[搭] tree roots 树根, root system 根系, deep roots 深根, take root 生根/扎根, root vegetable 根菜
[联] stem 茎, leaf 叶, plant 植物, soil 土壤, grow 生长, anchor 固定, branch 枝
[记] 可数/动词。take root 可喻「观念扎根」。root for sb. 美式口语「为某人加油」

**stem**
英/美 /stem/
n. 茎；梗
v. 阻止；起源于
[义] The main long part of a plant that supports leaves and flowers. 支撑叶与花的主长干（flower stem 花茎, stem cell 干细胞〔另义〕）。可数/动词。动词 stem from 指「源于」。
[例] ① She trimmed the stems before putting the roses in water. 她把玫瑰插水前先剪了梗。 ② Bamboo stems are hollow and surprisingly strong. 竹茎中空却异常坚韧。
[搭] flower stem 花茎, stem from 源于, stem the flow 遏制（喻）, long stem 长梗
[联] root 根, leaf 叶, branch 枝, flower 花, plant 植物, stalk 梗, trunk 树干
[记] 可数/动词。stem cell 为生物/医学义，与「花梗」同形。stem from 书面常用

**trunk**
英/美 /trʌŋk/
n. 树干；躯干；行李箱
[义] The thick main stem of a tree. 树的粗壮主茎（tree trunk 树干, trunk diameter 胸径）。可数。亦指躯干、箱 trunk——多义；动物章已述 elephant trunk。
[例] ① Children carved their initials into the old tree trunk. 孩子在老树干上刻了姓名缩写。 ② The elephant wrapped its trunk around the branch. 象用长鼻卷住树枝。
[搭] tree trunk 树干, trunk diameter 胸径, hollow trunk 空心树干, thick trunk 粗干
[联] tree 树, bark 树皮, branch 枝, root 根, wood 木材, elephant 象, forest 森林
[记] 可数。tree trunk 与 car trunk（后备箱）、elephant trunk（象鼻）同形异义

**branch**
英/美 /brɑːntʃ/
n. 树枝；分支
v. 分叉
[义] A part that grows out from the trunk or main stem of a tree or bush. 从树干或主茎长出的部分（tree branch 树枝, olive branch 橄榄枝〔喻和平〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① A bird sang from a high branch above the stream. 鸟在溪上方的高枝上鸣叫。 ② The path branched off to the left after the bridge. 过桥后小路向左岔开。
[搭] tree branch 树枝, olive branch 橄榄枝, branch off 分叉, broken branch 断枝
[联] tree 树, trunk 树干, twig 小枝, leaf 叶, nest 巢, forest 森林, limb 大枝
[记] 可数/动词。branch 亦指「分店、分支」——多义。olive branch 喻「和平姿态」

**twig**
英/美 /twɪɡ/
n. 小枝；细枝
[义] A small thin branch of a tree or bush. 树或灌木的细小枝条（snap a twig 折断细枝, bare twigs 光秃细枝）。可数。
[例] ① She tied the label to the plant with a twig and string. 她用细枝和绳把标签系在植株上。 ② Birds collected twigs to build their nest. 鸟收集细枝筑巢。
[搭] snap a twig 折断细枝, bare twigs 光秃细枝, dry twig 枯枝, twig and leaf 枝叶
[联] branch 树枝, nest 巢, bird 鸟, tree 树, leaf 叶, bark 树皮, small 小的
[记] 可数：a twig。比 branch 更小更细；文学中常写 stepping on a twig 暴露行踪

**leaf**
英/美 /liːf/
n. 叶子
（复数 leaves /liːvz/）
[义] A flat green part of a plant that grows from a stem and uses sunlight to make food. 从茎上长出、利用阳光制造养分的扁平绿色部分（green leaves 绿叶, fall leaves 秋叶, turn over a new leaf 翻开新的一页〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① Leaves turned gold and red before they fell. 叶子先变金变红，然后飘落。 ② She pressed a maple leaf in her notebook. 她把一片枫叶夹在笔记本里。
[搭] green leaves 绿叶, fall leaves 秋叶, leaf fall 落叶, turn over a new leaf 改过自新, tea leaves 茶叶
[联] tree 树, branch 枝, foliage 叶丛, stem 茎, autumn 秋天, green 绿色的, photosynthesis 光合作用
[记] 复数 leaves /liːvz/，非 leafs。tea leaves 亦指「茶叶」；leaf 与 leave（离开）勿混

**petal**
英/美 /ˈpetl/
n. 花瓣
[义] Each of the coloured parts of a flower that surround the centre. 环绕花心、通常带色的每一片（rose petals 玫瑰瓣, scatter petals 撒花瓣）。可数。
[例] ① Rose petals lay scattered on the wedding aisle. 玫瑰花瓣撒满婚礼通道。 ② The flower has five soft pink petals. 这朵花有五片柔软的粉色花瓣。
[搭] rose petals 玫瑰瓣, scatter petals 撒花瓣, petal fall 落瓣, soft petals 柔瓣
[联] flower 花, blossom 花朵, bloom 开花, stem 花梗, pollen 花粉, bee 蜜蜂, colour 颜色
[记] 可数：a petal。通常用复数 petals；婚礼、诗歌中意象常见

**bud**
英/美 /bʌd/
n. 芽；花蕾
v. 发芽
[义] A small swelling on a plant from which a flower, leaf, or shoot develops. 植物上将发育为花、叶或新枝的小突起（flower bud 花蕾, in bud 含苞, nip sth. in the bud 防患于未然〔习语〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The rose buds opened after two warm days. 两个暖日后玫瑰蕾绽放了。 ② Trees are still in bud here in early April. 这里四月初树仍在发芽期。
[搭] flower bud 花蕾, in bud 含苞, nip in the bud 防患于未然, taste buds 味蕾（另义）
[联] bloom 开花, blossom 花朵, leaf 叶, spring 春天, flower 花, sprout 发芽, tree 树
[记] 可数/动词。nip sth. in the bud 喻「扼杀在萌芽状态」。taste buds 为「味蕾」，不同义

**blossom**
英/美 /ˈblɒsəm/
n. 花朵；花期
v. 开花；发展
[义] The flowers on a tree or bush, especially fruit trees; to produce flowers. 树或灌木上的花，尤指果树；开花（cherry blossom 樱花, in full blossom 盛开, blossom into 发展为〔喻〕）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① Cherry blossom drew crowds to the park each spring. 每年春天樱花把人群吸引到公园。 ② Their friendship blossomed during the summer trip. 他们的友谊在夏日旅行中开花结果。
[搭] cherry blossom 樱花, in full blossom 盛开, peach blossom 桃花, blossom into 发展为
[联] bloom 开花, flower 花, petal 花瓣, spring 春天, tree 树, fruit 果实, bud 花蕾
[记] 不可数/动词。blossom 多指果树/木本的花；flower 更通用。动词 blossom into 喻「逐渐发展成熟」

**seed**
英/美 /siːd/
n. 种子
v. 播种（另义：seeded 种子选手〔体育〕）
[义] A small part of a plant from which a new plant can grow. 可长出新植株的植物微小部分（plant seeds 播种, sunflower seeds 葵花籽, go to seed 结籽/走下坡路〔习语〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She saved seeds from last year's tomatoes to plant again. 她留下去年番茄的种子再种。 ② Birds ate the seeds she had scattered on the snow. 鸟吃掉了她撒在雪上的种子。
[搭] plant seeds 播种, sunflower seeds 葵花籽, seed packet 种子袋, go to seed 结籽/走样
[联] sow 播种, sprout 发芽, germinate 萌发, flower 花, fruit 果实, grow 生长, crop 作物
[记] 可数/动词。seeded player 为体育义「种子选手」。go to seed 喻人/事物「走样、衰退」

**fruit**
英/美 /fruːt/
n. 果实；水果
v. 结果实
[义] The part of a plant that contains seeds, often sweet and eaten as food. 含种子的植物部分，常甜美可食（bear fruit 结果/见效, fresh fruit 新鲜水果, fruit tree 果树）。可数/不可数/动词。本章取植物学/物种义；餐桌用法亦在食物章交叉。
[例] ① The apple tree bore fruit for the first time this year. 这棵苹果树今年首次结果。 ② Hard work eventually bore fruit in her career. 努力最终在她的事业上见了成效。
[搭] bear fruit 结果/见效, fresh fruit 新鲜水果, fruit tree 果树, dried fruit 干果, citrus fruit 柑橘类
[联] seed 种子, flower 花, tree 树, harvest 收获, ripen 成熟, orchard 果园, vegetable 蔬菜
[记] 可数/不可数。fruit 作集合名词；语法上 fruit 单复数同形居多。bear fruit 可喻「努力见效」

**bark**
英/美 /bɑːk/
n. 树皮
v. 狗叫；大声喊
[义] The hard outer covering of a tree. 树的外部硬皮（tree bark 树皮, birch bark 桦树皮）。不可数/动词。动词 bark 指犬吠——与动物章 dog bark 同形；名词此处专指树皮。
[例] ① The birch bark peeled away in thin curls. 桦树皮卷成薄片剥落。 ② Carvings on the bark showed the tree was centuries old. 树皮上的刻痕表明此树已有数百年。
[搭] tree bark 树皮, birch bark 桦树皮, bark carving 树皮刻, under the bark 皮下（喻）
[联] tree 树, trunk 树干, wood 木材, branch 枝, forest 森林, peel 剥落, rough 粗糙的
[记] 不可数（指材料）。bark 与 dog bark（吠）同形异义；cinnamon 来自 bark（肉桂皮）

**thorn**
英/美 /θɔːn/
n. 刺；荆棘
[义] A sharp pointed growth on the stem of a plant such as a rose. 玫瑰等植物茎上的尖刺（rose thorn 玫瑰刺, thorn in one's side 眼中钉〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① She pricked her finger on a rose thorn. 她被玫瑰刺扎了手指。 ② The path was blocked by thorns and brambles. 小路被荆棘挡住。
[搭] rose thorn 玫瑰刺, thorn bush 荆棘丛, thorn in one's side 眼中钉, sharp thorn 尖刺
[联] rose 玫瑰, bush 灌木, prick 刺, sharp 尖的, bramble 黑莓丛, stem 茎, painful 痛的
[记] 可数：a thorn。thorn 与 spine（仙人掌等）近义；thorn 多指玫瑰类

**stalk**
英/美 /stɔːk/
n. 茎；梗
v. 潜近跟踪
[义] The main stem of a plant that supports leaves or flowers; a long narrow part joining a leaf or flower to the main stem. 支撑叶或花的主茎；连接叶/花与主茎的细长部分（corn stalk 玉米秆, flower stalk 花梗）。可数/动词。动词 stalk 指「跟踪」——多义。
[例] ① Corn stalks rustled in the wind after harvest. 收获后玉米秆在风中沙沙作响。 ② Celery is eaten for its crisp stalks. 芹菜因其脆茎而食用。
[搭] corn stalk 玉米秆, flower stalk 花梗, celery stalk 芹菜茎, leaf stalk 叶柄
[联] stem 茎, leaf 叶, root 根, plant 植物, corn 玉米, celery 芹菜, grow 生长
[记] 可数/动词。stalk 动词义「跟踪」与植物义同形。stalk 与 stem 常可互换，stem 更通用

**bulb**
英/美 /bʌlb/
n. 鳞茎；球茎；灯泡
[义] A round underground part of some plants from which the stem and leaves grow. 某些植物地下的圆形部分，茎叶由此长出（tulip bulb 郁金香球茎, plant bulbs 种球）。可数。亦指电灯泡——多义。
[例] ① She planted tulip bulbs before the first frost. 她在初霜前种下郁金香球茎。 ② Daffodils grow from bulbs that multiply each year. 水仙从每年增殖的球茎长出。
[搭] tulip bulb 郁金香球茎, plant bulbs 种球, spring bulbs 春季球根, light bulb 灯泡（另义）
[联] tulip 郁金香, daffodil 水仙, flower 花, spring 春天, plant 种植, root 根, garden 花园
[记] 可数。bulb 与 light bulb（灯泡）同形；garden center 秋日常卖 spring bulbs

**grow**
英/美 /ɡrəʊ/
v. 生长；种植；变得
[义] To increase in size and develop; to cause plants to develop; to become gradually. 体积增大并发育；种植；逐渐变得（plants grow 植物生长, grow vegetables 种蔬菜, grow taller 长高）。不规则动词。
[形] grow / grew / grown / growing
[例] ① Tomatoes grow best in full sun. 番茄在全日照下长得最好。 ② They grow herbs on the kitchen windowsill. 他们在厨房窗台上种香草。
[搭] plants grow 植物生长, grow vegetables 种蔬菜, grow taller 长高, grow out of 长大不穿/不再
[联] plant 种植, seed 种子, garden 花园, bloom 开花, crop 作物, soil 土壤, harvest 收获
[记] 不规则：grow / grew / grown。grow 作系动词（grow old 变老）与「生长/种植」并存

**bloom**
英/美 /bluːm/
n. 花朵；花期
v. 开花
[义] A flower, or the state of flowering; to produce flowers. 花或开花状态；开花（in bloom 盛开, full bloom 盛花期, bloom into 发展为〔喻〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The lilacs are in bloom for only a few weeks each year. 丁香每年只开几周。 ② Cactus blooms can be surprisingly large and bright. 仙人掌的花可以又大又艳，出人意料。
[搭] in bloom 盛开, full bloom 盛花期, come into bloom 进入花期, bloom of youth 青春盛期（喻）
[联] blossom 花朵, flower 花, bud 花蕾, spring 春天, petal 花瓣, garden 花园, fade 凋谢
[记] 可数/动词。bloom 与 blossom 近义；bloom 可用于仙人掌等非典型「花树」。algae bloom 为「藻华」，不同搭配

**wilt**
英/美 /wɪlt/
v. 枯萎；蔫
n. 萎蔫病
[义] To become limp and dry because of heat, lack of water, or disease. 因热、缺水或病害而变软干枯（wilted lettuce 蔫生菜, wilting flowers 萎花）。动词/可数名词。
[例] ① The cut flowers wilted within a day without water. 切花不浇水一天就蔫了。 ② Heat made the lettuce wilt in the sun. 高温让生菜在阳光下打蔫。
[搭] wilted lettuce 蔫生菜, wilting flowers 萎花, wilt in the heat 热蔫, wilt disease 萎蔫病
[联] dry 干的, wither 枯萎, water 浇水, heat 热, limp 无力的, fresh 新鲜的, droop 耷拉
[记] 动词/名词。wilt 比 wither 更偏「缺水打蔫」；wilted greens 烹饪中指「焯至软塌」

**sprout**
英/美 /spraʊt/
n. 新芽；芽苗
v. 发芽；冒出
[义] A new part growing on a plant; to begin to grow. 植物上新长出的部分；开始生长（bean sprouts 豆芽, sprouts on potatoes 土豆芽）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Green shoots sprouted from the bare branches in March. 三月光秃的枝上冒出绿芽。 ② Bean sprouts add crunch to the stir-fry. 豆芽给炒菜增加脆感。
[搭] bean sprouts 豆芽, sprouts on potatoes 土豆芽, sprout up 冒出, Brussels sprouts 抱子甘蓝
[联] seed 种子, germinate 萌发, grow 生长, bud 芽, shoot 新枝, spring 春天, leaf 叶
[记] 可数/动词。Brussels sprouts 为「抱子甘蓝」菜名；sprout up 可喻「大量涌现」

**germinate**
英/美 /ˈdʒɜːmɪneɪt/
v. 萌发；发芽
[义] To begin to grow from a seed or spore. 从种子或孢子开始生长（seeds germinate 种子萌发, germination rate 发芽率）。动词。
[例] ① The seeds germinated within a week in the warm soil. 种子在暖土中一周内萌发。 ② Not every idea germinates into a successful project. 并非每个想法都能发展为成功项目。
[形] germination /ˌdʒɜːmɪˈneɪʃn/ 萌发（不可数）
[搭] seeds germinate 种子萌发, germination rate 发芽率, germinate quickly 快速萌发
[联] seed 种子, sprout 发芽, grow 生长, soil 土壤, plant 种植, root 根, idea 想法（喻）
[记] 及物/不及物。名词 germination；科学/园艺文本常用，口语多说 sprout

**sow**
英/美 /səʊ/
v. 播种；散布
[义] To plant seeds in the ground. 把种子种入地里（sow seeds 播种, sow wheat 播麦, sow doubt 散布疑虑〔喻〕）。不规则动词。
[形] sow / sowed / sown或sowed / sowing
[例] ① Farmers sowed wheat after the last frost. 农民在末霜后播麦。 ② She sowed lettuce and radishes in the raised beds. 她在高床里播了生菜和小萝卜。
[搭] sow seeds 播种, sow wheat 播麦, sow the fields 耕地播种, reap what you sow 种瓜得瓜（谚）
[联] seed 种子, harvest 收获, crop 作物, field 田野, plant 种植, farm 农场, reap 收割
[记] 不规则：sow / sowed / sown。谚语 You reap what you sow 喻「自作自受」。sow 与 sew（缝）同音异义

**harvest**
英/美 /ˈhɑːvɪst/
n. 收获；收成
v. 收割
[义] The time when crops are gathered, or the act of gathering them. 作物被收集的时节或行为（harvest season 收获季, bumper harvest 丰收, harvest moon 秋月）。可数/不可数/动词。
[例] ① The grape harvest begins in late September. 葡萄采收九月底开始。 ② They harvested corn before the storm arrived. 他们在暴风雨来临前收完玉米。
[搭] harvest season 收获季, bumper harvest 丰收, harvest moon 秋月, bring in the harvest 收庄稼
[联] crop 作物, sow 播种, farm 农场, field 田野, ripe 成熟的, yield 产量, autumn 秋天
[记] 可数/动词。harvest 亦可指「采集（器官、数据）」——比喻义。本章侧重农作物

**ripen**
英/美 /ˈraɪpən/
v. 成熟
[义] To become ripe; to make ripe. 变熟；使成熟（ripen on the vine 藤上熟, ripening fruit 将熟果实）。动词。
[例] ① Tomatoes ripen faster in warm sunny weather. 番茄在暖晴天气下熟得更快。 ② Bananas continue to ripen after they are picked. 香蕉采后仍会继续熟。
[形] ripe /raɪp/ 成熟的, ripeness 成熟度
[搭] ripen on the vine 藤上熟, ripening fruit 将熟果实, ripen into 成熟为（喻）
[联] ripe 成熟的, fruit 果实, harvest 收获, crop 作物, green 未熟的, sweet 甜的, pick 采摘
[记] 及物/不及物。ripe 形容词；ripen 强调过程。ripen into 可喻「逐渐发展成」

**perennial**
英/美 /pəˈreniəl/
n. 多年生植物
adj. 多年的；反复出现的
[义] A plant that lives for more than two years; lasting for a long time or returning each year. 存活超过两年的植物；持久的、年年复现的（perennial flowers 多年生花卉, perennial problem 老问题〔喻〕）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Lavender is a hardy perennial in this climate. 薰衣草在该气候下是耐寒多年生植物。 ② Perennials return each spring without replanting. 多年生植物每年春天无需重种即复生。
[搭] perennial flowers 多年生花卉, perennial plant 多年生植物, hardy perennial 耐寒多年生, perennial problem 老问题
[联] annual 一年生, evergreen 常绿, garden 花园, flower 花, plant 植物, bloom 开花, hardy 耐寒的
[记] 可数/形容词。per-（贯穿）+ ann-（年）→ 跨年而生。与 annual（一年生）相对

**annual**
英/美 /ˈænjuəl/
n. 一年生植物
adj. 每年的；一年生的
[义] A plant that completes its life cycle in one year; happening once every year. 一年内完成生命周期的植物；每年一次的（annual flowers 一年生花卉, annual report 年报〔另义〕）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Marigolds are cheerful annuals for summer borders. 万寿菊是夏季花境里明快的一年生植物。 ② She replants annuals each spring for fresh colour. 她每年春天重种一年生花卉以换新色。
[搭] annual flowers 一年生花卉, annual plant 一年生植物, plant annuals 种一年生植物
[联] perennial 多年生, biennial 二年生, garden 花园, seed 种子, bloom 开花, season 季节, yearly 每年的
[记] 可数/形容词。ann-（年）词根；annual 亦指「年度的」——多义。与 perennial 成对记忆

**evergreen**
英/美 /ˈevəɡriːn/
n. 常绿植物
adj. 常绿的
[义] A plant that has green leaves all year; having leaves in all seasons. 全年有绿叶的植物；四季有叶的（evergreen trees 常绿树, evergreen content 常青内容〔喻〕）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Pine and fir are evergreens common in northern forests. 松和冷杉是北方森林常见常绿树。 ② The hedge was planted with evergreen shrubs for winter colour. 树篱种常绿灌木以保持冬日绿意。
[搭] evergreen trees 常绿树, evergreen shrub 常绿灌木, evergreen forest 常绿林
[联] deciduous 落叶的, pine 松, conifer 针叶树, winter 冬天, leaf 叶, forest 森林, green 绿的
[记] 可数/形容词。ever + green → 始终绿。evergreen 亦指「历久不衰的内容/歌曲」

**deciduous**
英/美 /dɪˈsɪdʒuəs/
adj. 落叶的
[义] (Of a tree or shrub) losing its leaves every year. （树或灌木）每年落叶的（deciduous forest 落叶林, deciduous teeth 乳牙〔另义〕）。只作形容词。
[例] ① Maple is a deciduous tree famous for autumn colour. 枫是以为人熟知的秋色落叶树。 ② The path was buried under fallen leaves from deciduous trees. 小路被落叶树的落叶覆盖。
[搭] deciduous forest 落叶林, deciduous tree 落叶树, deciduous woodland 落叶林地
[联] evergreen 常绿的, leaf 叶, autumn 秋天, forest 森林, maple 枫, oak 橡, seasonal 季节性的
[记] 只作形容词。de-（落下）+ cad-（落）相关；deciduous teeth 指「乳牙」，不同义

**garden**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːdn/
n. 花园；菜园
v. 从事园艺
[义] A piece of ground for growing flowers, vegetables, or plants. 种植花卉、蔬菜或其他植物的一块地（back garden 后院, vegetable garden 菜园, botanical garden 植物园）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Roses filled the front garden with colour each June. 每年六月玫瑰让前院色彩缤纷。 ② She gardens on weekends to relax from office work. 她周末做园艺以从办公室工作中放松。
[搭] back garden 后院, vegetable garden 菜园, botanical garden 植物园, garden tools 园艺工具, garden party 花园派对
[联] plant 种植, flower 花, lawn 草坪, gardener 园丁, hedge 树篱, herb 香草, yard 院子
[记] 可数/动词。garden 在英式英语常指 whole yard；美式 front yard / backyard 更常说

**lawn**
英/美 /lɔːn/
n. 草坪
[义] An area of grass kept short and tidy, especially around a house. 修剪短而整齐的草地，尤指房屋周围（mow the lawn 割草坪, lawn mower 割草机, front lawn 前院草坪）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① He mowed the lawn before the barbecue. 烧烤前他先割了草坪。 ② Children played soccer on the school lawn. 孩子在学校草坪上踢足球。
[搭] mow the lawn 割草坪, lawn mower 割草机, front lawn 前院草坪, lawn care 草坪养护
[联] grass 草, garden 花园, yard 院子, green 绿色的, mow 割, hedge 树篱, sprinkler 洒水器
[记] 可数/不可数。mow the lawn 为美国家庭高频家务；lawn 亦可指「细布」，不同义

**orchard**
英/美 /ˈɔːtʃəd/
n. 果园
[义] A piece of land planted with fruit trees. 种植果树的土地（apple orchard 苹果园, peach orchard 桃园）。可数。
[例] ① They walked through the apple orchard in blossom. 他们穿过正开花的苹果园。 ② The orchard supplies fruit to local markets each autumn. 果园每年秋天向本地市场供果。
[搭] apple orchard 苹果园, peach orchard 桃园, orchard owner 果园主, fruit orchard 果园
[联] fruit 果实, tree 树, harvest 收获, apple 苹果, farm 农场, blossom 开花, crop 作物
[记] 可数：an orchard。orchard 专指果树园；vineyard 指葡萄园

**greenhouse**
英/美 /ˈɡriːnhaʊs/
n. 温室；暖房
[义] A glass building used for growing plants that need warmth or protection. 用于栽培需保温或防护植物的玻璃建筑（greenhouse gases 温室气体〔另义〕, greenhouse effect 温室效应）。可数。
[例] ① Tomatoes ripen early in the heated greenhouse. 番茄在加热温室里熟得早。 ② She started seedlings in the greenhouse before spring planting. 她在春季种植前于温室育秧。
[搭] heated greenhouse 加热温室, greenhouse gases 温室气体, greenhouse effect 温室效应, greenhouse plants 温室植物
[联] plant 种植, garden 花园, warm 温暖的, glass 玻璃, seedling 幼苗, climate 气候, grow 生长
[记] 可数：a greenhouse。greenhouse gases/effect 为气候议题高频义，与「花房」同形

**nursery**
英/美 /ˈnɜːsəri/
n. 苗圃；幼儿室
[义] A place where young plants and trees are grown for sale or transplanting. 培育幼株与幼树以供出售或移栽的场所（plant nursery 植物苗圃, tree nursery 树苗圃）。可数。亦指「托儿所」——多义。
[例] ① We bought lavender starts from a local nursery. 我们从本地苗圃买了薰衣草苗。 ② The nursery stocks native trees for reforestation projects. 苗圃为重新造林项目储备本地树种。
[搭] plant nursery 植物苗圃, tree nursery 树苗圃, garden nursery 园艺苗圃, nursery stock 苗木
[联] plant 种植, seedling 幼苗, garden 花园, tree 树, grow 生长, transplant 移栽, sapling 幼树
[记] 可数。nursery 作「托儿所」与「苗圃」同形；plant nursery 可消歧

**cultivate**
英/美 /ˈkʌltɪveɪt/
v. 栽培；培养
[义] To prepare and use land for crops or gardening; to develop a quality or relationship. 整地并种植作物或园艺；培养（品质/关系）（cultivate land 耕地, cultivate roses 栽培玫瑰, cultivate friendships 培养友谊〔喻〕）。动词。
[例] ① Farmers have cultivated this valley for centuries. 农民在此山谷耕作已有数百年。 ② She cultivated herbs in pots on the balcony. 她在阳台花盆里种香草。
[形] cultivation /ˌkʌltɪˈveɪʃn/ 栽培（不可数）, cultivated 栽培的/有教养的
[搭] cultivate land 耕地, cultivate crops 种庄稼, cultivate roses 栽培玫瑰, soil cultivation 土壤耕作
[联] grow 生长, farm 农场, crop 作物, garden 花园, plant 种植, soil 土壤, harvest 收获
[记] 及物动词。cult-（耕作）词根；cultivated 可指「有教养的」——比喻义

**prune**
英/美 /pruːn/
v. 修剪；删节
n. 西梅干（另义）
[义] To cut off dead or overgrown branches from a tree or bush. 剪去树或灌木的枯枝或过长枝条（prune roses 修玫瑰, prune trees 修树）。动词/可数名词。名词 prune 指「西梅干」——同形异义。
[例] ① She pruned the apple trees in late winter. 她在冬末修剪苹果树。 ② Regular pruning keeps hedges dense and neat. 定期修剪使树篱密而整齐。
[搭] prune roses 修玫瑰, prune trees 修树, prune back 剪短, heavy pruning 重剪
[联] trim 修剪, branch 枝, hedge 树篱, shrub 灌木, garden 花园, tree 树, cut 剪
[记] 动词/名词。prune 作「西梅干」为食物义；园艺义及物。prune back 指「剪回促新枝」

**compost**
英/美 /ˈkɒmpɒst/
n. 堆肥
v. 制成堆肥
[义] Decayed organic matter used to fertilize soil. 用于肥土的腐殖有机质（kitchen compost 厨余堆肥, compost heap 堆肥堆, add compost 加堆肥）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① She added compost to the beds before planting. 她在种植前给菜床加了堆肥。 ② Vegetable scraps can be composted instead of thrown away. 菜叶厨余可堆肥而非丢弃。
[搭] kitchen compost 厨余堆肥, compost heap 堆肥堆, add compost 加堆肥, make compost 制堆肥
[联] soil 土壤, fertilizer 肥料, garden 花园, organic 有机的, recycle 回收, decay 腐烂, mulch 覆盖物
[记] 不可数/动词。compost bin 为家庭园艺常见；与 fertilizer 互补：compost 偏有机自制

**fertilizer**
英/美 /ˈfɜːtəlaɪzə(r)/
n. 肥料
[义] A substance added to soil to help plants grow. 加入土壤助植物生长的物质（organic fertilizer 有机肥, chemical fertilizer 化肥, apply fertilizer 施肥）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Too much fertilizer can burn young plants. 肥料过多会灼伤幼株。 ② They switched to organic fertilizer on the vegetable plot. 他们在菜地上改用了有机肥。
[搭] organic fertilizer 有机肥, chemical fertilizer 化肥, apply fertilizer 施肥, liquid fertilizer 液肥
[联] soil 土壤, compost 堆肥, grow 生长, crop 作物, plant 植物, nutrient 养分, garden 花园
[记] 不可数/可数。英式拼写 fertiliser；fertilize 为动词「施肥/使受精」

**hedge**
英/美 /hedʒ/
n. 树篱；对冲（金融）
v. 用树篱围住；规避
[义] A row of bushes or small trees planted close together along the edge of a garden or field. 沿花园或田地边缘密植的灌木或小树行（garden hedge 花园树篱, hedge trimming 修篱）。可数/动词。金融 hedge 为另义。
[例] ① A tall hedge blocked the view from the road. 高树篱挡住了路人的视线。 ② They trimmed the hedge into a neat shape. 他们把树篱修成整齐形状。
[搭] garden hedge 花园树篱, hedge trimming 修篱, hedge row 树篱行, behind the hedge 篱后
[联] shrub 灌木, bush 灌木, fence 围栏, garden 花园, prune 修剪, boundary 边界, green 绿的
[记] 可数/动词。hedge 金融义「对冲」与植物义同形；living hedge 指「活树篱」

**transplant**
英/美 /trænsˈplɑːnt/
v. 移栽；移植
n. 移植；移植器官
[义] To move a plant to another place; to move an organ into a body. 将植物移到另一处；移植器官（transplant seedlings 移栽秧苗, heart transplant 心脏移植〔另义〕）。动词/可数名词。
[例] ① Wait until frost passes before you transplant tomatoes outdoors. 等霜期过后再把番茄移栽到室外。 ② The oak sapling was transplanted to the school courtyard. 橡树幼株被移栽到学校庭院。
[搭] transplant seedlings 移栽秧苗, transplant into the garden 移入花园, organ transplant 器官移植（另义）
[联] plant 种植, seedling 幼苗, garden 花园, root 根, grow 生长, soil 土壤, nursery 苗圃
[记] 及物动词。trans-（转移）+ plant；医学 transplant 与园艺义同词

**forest**
英/美 /ˈfɒrɪst/
n. 森林；林立
[义] A large area covered chiefly with trees and undergrowth. 主要由树木与下层植被覆盖的大片区域（rainforest 雨林, dense forest 密林, forest fire 林火）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① They hiked through the forest for three hours. 他们在森林中徒步了三小时。 ② Logging threatens old-growth forest in the region. 伐木威胁该地区的原始林。
[搭] rainforest 雨林, dense forest 密林, forest fire 林火, forest floor 林地, old-growth forest 原始林
[联] tree 树, woodland 林地, jungle 丛林, wildlife 野生动植物, habitat 栖息地, logging 伐木, vegetation 植被
[记] 可数/不可数。can't see the forest for the trees 见林不见树。forest 比 woods 规模更大、更正式

**jungle**
英/美 /ˈdʒʌŋɡl/
n. 丛林；弱肉强食的环境
[义] An area of tropical forest where trees and plants grow very thickly. 热带树木与植物极密生的区域（Amazon jungle 亚马逊丛林, concrete jungle 水泥丛林〔喻〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Monkeys called from the jungle canopy above the river. 猴在河上空的丛林冠层鸣叫。 ② The trail was overgrown and felt like a jungle. 小径杂草丛生，像丛林一样。
[搭] Amazon jungle 亚马逊丛林, jungle canopy 丛林冠层, concrete jungle 水泥丛林, dense jungle 密丛林
[联] forest 森林, tropical 热带的, rainforest 雨林, vine 藤, wildlife 野生动植物, thick 密的, vegetation 植被
[记] 可数/不可数。jungle 强调热带、密、野；rainforest 更偏生态/科学用语

**meadow**
英/美 /ˈmedəʊ/
n. 草地；牧场
[义] A field of grass and wild flowers, often used for hay. 长草与野花的田野，常用来打干草（alpine meadow 高山草甸, flower meadow 花田）。可数。
[例] ① Cows grazed peacefully in the sunny meadow. 牛在洒满阳光的草地上安静吃草。 ② Butterflies fluttered over the wildflower meadow. 蝴蝶在野花草甸上飞舞。
[搭] alpine meadow 高山草甸, flower meadow 花田, meadow grass 草甸草, water meadow 浸水草甸
[联] grass 草, field 田野, flower 花, pasture 牧场, cattle 牛, butterfly 蝴蝶, peaceful 宁静的
[记] 可数：a meadow。meadow 比 lawn 更野、更自然；常出现在田园文学中

**rainforest**
英/美 /ˈreɪnfɒrɪst/
n. 雨林
[义] A dense forest in tropical areas with heavy rainfall. 热带高降雨区的密林（tropical rainforest 热带雨林, rainforest canopy 雨林冠层, save the rainforest 拯救雨林）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The Amazon rainforest produces much of the world's oxygen. 亚马逊雨林产生全球大量氧气。 ② Tourists visited a rainforest reserve in Costa Rica. 游客参观了哥斯达黎加的一处雨林保护区。
[搭] tropical rainforest 热带雨林, rainforest canopy 雨林冠层, rainforest reserve 雨林保护区, deforestation 毁林
[联] jungle 丛林, forest 森林, tropical 热带的, biodiversity 生物多样性, habitat 栖息地, species 物种, logging 伐木
[记] 可数/不可数。rain + forest 复合词；环保与地理文本高频

**oak**
英/美 /əʊk/
n. 橡树；橡木
[义] A large tree that produces acorns and has hard wood; the wood itself. 产橡实、木材坚硬的大树；其木材（oak tree 橡树, oak forest 橡树林, solid oak 纯橡木）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① An ancient oak shaded the village square. 一棵古橡荫蔽着村广场。 ② The table was made of solid oak. 这张桌子是纯橡木做的。
[搭] oak tree 橡树, oak forest 橡树林, solid oak 纯橡木, oak leaf 橡树叶, acorn 橡实
[联] tree 树, forest 森林, wood 木材, acorn 橡实, deciduous 落叶的, maple 枫, leaf 叶
[记] 可数/不可数（木材）。oak 在英美文化象征力量与耐久；acorn 为橡实

**pine**
英/美 /paɪn/
n. 松树；松木
v.  pine for 渴望
[义] An evergreen tree with needle-like leaves and cones; its wood. 叶如针、结球果的常绿树；其木材（pine tree 松树, pine forest 松林, pine cone 松果）。可数/动词。动词 pine for 指「渴望」——多义。
[例] ① The scent of pine filled the mountain trail. 山路上弥漫着松香。 ② Pine needles carpeted the forest floor. 松针铺满林地。
[搭] pine tree 松树, pine forest 松林, pine cone 松果, pine needle 松针, pine wood 松木
[联] evergreen 常绿, conifer 针叶树, forest 森林, tree 树, cone 球果, needle 针叶, wood 木材
[记] 可数/动词。pine for 与树木义同形；pine cone 为松果，圣诞装饰常见

**maple**
英/美 /ˈmeɪpl/
n. 枫树；枫木
[义] A tree with lobed leaves, often bright in autumn; its wood or syrup. 叶掌状分裂、秋日常艳丽的树；其木材或糖浆（maple syrup 枫糖浆, maple leaf 枫叶, sugar maple 糖枫）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Maple leaves turned crimson along the lake road. 沿湖公路的枫叶变成深红。 ② Canada uses the maple leaf as a national symbol. 加拿大以枫叶为国家象征。
[搭] maple syrup 枫糖浆, maple leaf 枫叶, sugar maple 糖枫, maple tree 枫树
[联] tree 树, leaf 叶, autumn 秋天, deciduous 落叶的, oak 橡, syrup 糖浆, Canada 加拿大
[记] 可数/不可数。maple syrup 为北美文化符号；deciduous 典型代表树种

**palm**
英/美 /pɑːm/
n. 棕榈树；手掌
[义] A tropical tree with a straight trunk and large leaves at the top; the inner surface of the hand. 树干挺直、顶生大叶的热带树；手掌内侧（palm tree 棕榈, coconut palm 椰棕, palm oil 棕榈油）。可数。手掌义在 body 章；此处取植物义。
[例] ① Palm trees lined the beach road. 棕榈排列在滨海公路旁。 ② Palm oil is used in many processed foods. 棕榈油用于多种加工食品。
[搭] palm tree 棕榈, coconut palm 椰棕, palm oil 棕榈油, palm frond 棕榈叶
[联] tropical 热带的, tree 树, coconut 椰子, beach 海滩, frond 叶, oil 油, island 岛屿
[记] 可数。palm 手掌义与植物义同形；palm oil 为环境/经济议题常见词

**bamboo**
英/美 /ˌbæmˈbuː/
n. 竹；竹子
[义] A tall tropical plant with hollow woody stems, used for building and crafts. 茎中空木质的高大热带植物，用于建筑与工艺（bamboo forest 竹林, bamboo shoots 竹笋, bamboo cane 竹杖）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Pandas feed almost entirely on bamboo. 熊猫几乎完全以竹为食。 ② Bamboo grows quickly and is highly renewable. 竹生长快、可再生性高。
[搭] bamboo forest 竹林, bamboo shoots 竹笋, bamboo cane 竹杖, bamboo grove
[联] grass 草（分类上为禾本）, forest 林, panda 熊猫, shoot 笋, wood 材, tropical 热带的, stem 茎
[记] 不可数/可数。 bamboo 在分类上属禾本科，日常与 tree 并提。shoots 作食物在食物章可交叉

**rose**
英/美 /rəʊz/
n. 玫瑰；蔷薇
[义] A flower with thorny stems and fragrant petals, often red or pink. 茎有刺、瓣常香，多为红或粉的花（rose garden 玫瑰园, rose bush 玫瑰丛, rose petals 玫瑰瓣）。可数。
[例] ① She planted red roses along the garden path. 她在花园小径旁种了红玫瑰。 ② The rose is often called the queen of flowers. 玫瑰常被称为花中之后。
[搭] rose garden 玫瑰园, rose bush 玫瑰丛, rose petals 玫瑰瓣, rose thorn 玫瑰刺, bed of roses 称心如意（习语）
[联] flower 花, petal 花瓣, thorn 刺, bloom 开花, fragrance 芬芳, tulip 郁金香, garden 花园
[记] 可数：a rose。rise（上升）与 rose（过去式/玫瑰）同音异义须语境区分

**tulip**
英/美 /ˈtjuːlɪp/
n. 郁金香
[义] A spring flower with a cup-shaped bloom on a long stem, grown from a bulb. 春季花，长梗上杯状花，由球茎生长（tulip bulb 郁金香球茎, tulip field 郁金香花田）。可数。
[例] ① Tulips opened in bright rows at the park entrance. 郁金香在公园入口成排绽放。 ② Dutch tulip fields draw tourists each April. 荷兰郁金香花田每年四月吸引游客。
[搭] tulip bulb 郁金香球茎, tulip field 郁金香花田, spring tulips 春郁金香, plant tulips 种郁金香
[联] bulb 球茎, flower 花, spring 春天, garden 花园, petal 花瓣, Netherlands 荷兰, bloom 开花
[记] 可数：a tulip。郁金香与荷兰历史、球茎贸易文化紧密相关

**sunflower**
英/美 /ˈsʌnflaʊə(r)/
n. 向日葵
[义] A tall plant with a large yellow flower that turns to face the sun; its seeds. 高大、开大黄花并常朝向阳光的植物；其籽（sunflower seeds 葵花籽, sunflower oil 葵花籽油, sunflower field 向日葵田）。可数。
[例] ① Sunflowers towered over the fence in late summer. 夏末向日葵高过篱笆。 ② Birds fed on sunflower seeds left in the garden. 鸟啄食留在花园里的葵花籽。
[搭] sunflower seeds 葵花籽, sunflower oil 葵花籽油, sunflower field 向日葵田, giant sunflower 巨型向日葵
[联] seed 种子, flower 花, yellow 黄的, oil 油, field 田, summer 夏天, tall 高的
[记] 可数：a sunflower。sun + flower → 向日；葵花籽为常见零食

**daisy**
英/美 /ˈdeɪzi/
n. 雏菊
[义] A small white flower with a yellow centre, common in grass. 常见于草地、白瓣黄心的小花（daisy chain 雏菊花链, fresh as a daisy 精神饱满〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① Daisies dotted the lawn after the spring rains. 春雨过后草坪上点缀着雏菊。 ② She wove a daisy chain for her daughter. 她为女儿编了雏菊花链。
[搭] daisy chain 雏菊花链, fresh as a daisy 精神饱满, pick daisies 摘雏菊
[联] flower 花, grass 草, lawn 草坪, white 白的, petal 花瓣, spring 春天, meadow 草地
[记] 可数：a daisy。习语 fresh as a daisy 指「精神很好」。ups-a-daisy 为哄小孩用语

**orchid**
英/美 /ˈɔːkɪd/
n. 兰花
[义] A plant with showy, often exotic flowers, popular as a houseplant or in botanical gardens. 花艳丽、常带异域风情，宜作室内或植物园观赏（orchid collection 兰花收藏, wild orchid 野生兰）。可数。
[例] ① She received an orchid as a housewarming gift. 她收到一盆兰花作乔迁礼。 ② Wild orchids are protected in many national parks. 许多国家公园保护野生兰花。
[搭] orchid collection 兰花收藏, wild orchid 野生兰, orchid show 兰花展, potted orchid 盆栽兰
[联] flower 花, tropical 热带的, petal 花瓣, rare 稀有的, greenhouse 温室, exotic 异国情调的, garden 花园
[记] 可数：an orchid。orchid 常作礼品花；品种极多，日常不细分学名

**wheat**
英/美 /wiːt/
n. 小麦
[义] A cereal plant whose grain is ground into flour for bread and pasta. 谷物植物，其籽磨粉做面包和面食（wheat field 麦田, wheat flour 小麦粉, grow wheat 种麦）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Golden wheat swayed before the summer harvest. 夏收前金麦摇曳。 ② Bread in this region is made from local wheat. 本地区面包用本地小麦制作。
[搭] wheat field 麦田, wheat flour 小麦粉, grow wheat 种麦, whole wheat 全麦, wheat crop 小麦收成
[联] grain 谷物, crop 作物, harvest 收获, flour 面粉, bread 面包, field 田, farm 农场
[记] 不可数/可数。wheat 本章取物种/作物义；面粉、面包用法在食物章。whole wheat 指「全麦」

**rice**
英/美 /raɪs/
n. 稻；大米
[义] A cereal plant grown in warm wet places; the grains used as food. 种于暖湿地区的禾本作物；其谷粒作粮食（rice paddy 稻田, rice field 稻田, wild rice 野稻）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Rice paddies stretched to the horizon in the valley. 山谷里稻田延至天际。 ② Farmers flood the fields when planting rice. 农民种稻时给田灌水。
[搭] rice paddy 稻田, rice field 稻田, wild rice 野稻, rice crop 稻作, grow rice 种稻
[联] grain 谷物, crop 作物, harvest 收获, paddy 稻田, field 田, staple 主食, water 水
[记] 不可数/可数。rice 作植物义在 paddy/field 语境；餐桌义 rice 不可数。亚洲农业核心作物

**corn**
英/美 /kɔːn/
n. 玉米；谷物（英式）
[义] (AmE) A tall plant with yellow kernels on a cob; (BrE) cereal crops in general. （美式）结穗黄粒的高大作物；（英式）谷物总称（corn field 玉米田, sweet corn 甜玉米, corn stalk 玉米秆）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Corn grew higher than a person by August. 到八月玉米长得比人还高。 ② They drove past miles of corn fields in Iowa. 他们驾车经过爱荷华数英里玉米田。
[搭] corn field 玉米田, sweet corn 甜玉米, corn stalk 玉米秆, corn crop 玉米收成, ear of corn 玉米穗
[联] crop 作物, stalk 秆, harvest 收获, maize 玉米（英式专名）, field 田, grain 谷物, farm 农场
[记] 美式 corn = 英式 maize；英式 corn 可泛指谷物，须看语境。本章取 maize 义

**cotton**
英/美 /ˈkɒtn/
n. 棉；棉花
[义] A plant grown in warm countries for the soft white fibres around its seeds; the fibres or cloth. 暖地栽培、种籽外包软白纤维的植物；纤维或棉布（cotton field 棉田, cotton plant 棉株, cotton wool 药棉）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① White cotton bolls opened in the autumn sun. 白色棉铃在秋阳下绽开。 ② The region depended on cotton for its economy. 该地区经济依赖棉花。
[搭] cotton field 棉田, cotton plant 棉株, cotton wool 药棉, cotton crop 棉花收成, pick cotton 采棉
[联] crop 作物, fibre 纤维, fabric 织物, field 田, harvest 收获, plant 植物, textile 纺织
[记] 不可数/可数。cotton 亦可指「棉布」；cotton on to 英式口语「领会」——不同义

**photosynthesis**
英/美 /ˌfəʊtəʊˈsɪnθəsɪs/
n. 光合作用
[义] The process by which green plants use sunlight to make food from carbon dioxide and water. 绿色植物利用阳光将二氧化碳与水合成养料的过程。不可数。
[例] ① Chlorophyll captures light energy for photosynthesis. 叶绿素捕获光能以供光合作用。 ② Without photosynthesis, most life on Earth could not exist. 没有光合作用，地球上大多数生命无法存在。
[搭] process of photosynthesis 光合作用过程, photosynthesis in plants 植物光合作用, rate of photosynthesis 光合速率
[联] chlorophyll 叶绿素, leaf 叶, sunlight 阳光, plant 植物, carbon dioxide 二氧化碳, oxygen 氧气, biology 生物学
[记] 不可数。photo-（光）+ synthesis（合成）；中学科学必考词

**chlorophyll**
英/美 /ˈklɒrəfɪl/
n. 叶绿素
[义] The green substance in plants that absorbs light for photosynthesis. 植物中吸收光线、供光合作用的绿色物质。不可数。
[例] ① Leaves look green because chlorophyll reflects green light. 叶呈绿色是因为叶绿素反射绿光。 ② Algae contain chlorophyll like land plants. 藻类与陆地植物一样含叶绿素。
[搭] chlorophyll in leaves 叶中叶绿素, contain chlorophyll 含叶绿素, green chlorophyll 绿色叶绿素
[联] photosynthesis 光合作用, leaf 叶, green 绿的, plant 植物, algae 藻类, pigment 色素, sunlight 阳光
[记] 不可数。chloro-（绿）+ phyll（叶）；拼写注意双 l 结尾

**pollen**
英/美 /ˈpɒlən/
n. 花粉
[义] A fine powder produced by flowers that fertilizes other flowers of the same kind. 花产生的细粉，使同种花受精（pollen count 花粉指数, pollen allergy 花粉过敏, bee pollen 蜂花粉）。不可数。
[例] ① Bees collect pollen and nectar from blossoms. 蜜蜂从花朵采集花粉与花蜜。 ② High pollen counts make her allergies worse in spring. 春季花粉指数高时她的过敏加重。
[搭] pollen count 花粉指数, pollen allergy 花粉过敏, bee pollen 蜂花粉, pollen grain 花粉粒
[联] flower 花, bee 蜜蜂, blossom 花朵, allergy 过敏, spring 春天, pollinate 授粉, seed 种子
[记] 不可数。pollinate / pollinator 词条在动物章；pollen 为植物生物学核心名词

**beehive**
英/美 /ˈbiːhaɪv/
n. 蜂箱；蜂巢
[义] A box or structure where bees are kept; the place where a colony of bees lives. 养蜂用的箱或结构；蜂群居住处（manage a beehive 管理蜂箱， wild beehive 野蜂巢， beehive boxes 蜂箱）。可数。
[例] ① The orchard owner kept beehives to pollinate the apple trees. 果园主养蜂箱为苹果树授粉。 ② Honey dripped from a crack in the old beehive. 老蜂巢的裂缝渗出蜂蜜。
[搭] manage a beehive 管理蜂箱, wild beehive 野蜂巢, beehive boxes 蜂箱, check the beehive 检查蜂箱
[联] bee 蜜蜂, honey 蜂蜜, pollen 花粉, pollinate 授粉, hive 蜂巢, orchard 果园, colony 群体
[记] 可数：a beehive。hive 可单用；beehive 更具体指蜂箱或蜂群居所

**seedling**
英/美 /ˈsiːdlɪŋ/
n. 幼苗；秧苗
[义] A young plant grown from a seed. 由种子长出的幼株（tree seedlings 树苗, plant seedlings 秧苗, seedling stage 幼苗期）。可数。
[例] ① She watered the tomato seedlings every morning. 她每天早晨给番茄秧浇水。 ② Volunteers planted pine seedlings on the bare hillside. 志愿者在光秃山坡上种松苗。
[搭] tree seedlings 树苗, plant seedlings 秧苗, seedling stage 幼苗期, raise seedlings 育秧
[联] seed 种子, sprout 发芽, nursery 苗圃, transplant 移栽, grow 生长, sapling 幼树, garden 花园
[记] 可数：a seedling。seed + -ling（小）→ 小种子长出的苗

**sapling**
英/美 /ˈsæplɪŋ/
n. 幼树；树苗
[义] A young tree. 年幼的树（plant saplings 植幼树, oak sapling 橡树苗）。可数。
[例] ① Each student planted a sapling in the school grove. 每位学生在学校园种了一棵幼树。 ② Deer sometimes damage saplings in new forests. 鹿有时会损害新林中的幼树。
[搭] plant saplings 植幼树, oak sapling 橡树苗, young sapling 幼树, sapling growth 幼树生长
[联] tree 树, seedling 幼苗, forest 森林, plant 种植, grow 生长, nursery 苗圃, trunk 树干
[记] 可数：a sapling。比 seedling 更指木本幼树；reforestation 文本常见

**woodland**
英/美 /ˈwʊdlənd/
n. 林地；小树林
[义] Land covered with trees, smaller than a forest. 有树覆盖的土地，规模小于 forest（ancient woodland 古 woodland, woodland path 林间小径, woodland creatures 林地生物）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Bluebells carpeted the woodland floor in May. 五月蓝铃花铺满林地。 ② They picnicked at the edge of the woodland. 他们在林地边缘野餐。
[搭] ancient woodland 古林地, woodland path 林间小径, woodland area 林地区, mixed woodland 混交林
[联] forest 森林, tree 树, wildlife 野生动植物, path 小径, shade 荫, wildflower 野花, habitat 栖息地
[记] 不可数/可数。wood + land；比 forest 规模小、更田园；英式 countryside 描述常见

**wildflower**
英/美 /ˈwaɪldflaʊə(r)/
n. 野花
[义] A flower that grows in natural conditions without being planted. 在自然条件下生长、非人工栽培的花（wildflower meadow 野花草甸, native wildflowers 本地野花）。可数/复合名词。
[例] ① Wildflowers bloomed along the hiking trail after the rains. 雨后徒步道旁野花盛开。 ② The guide identified native wildflowers for the group. 向导为团队辨认本地野花。
[搭] wildflower meadow 野花草甸, native wildflowers 本地野花, pick wildflowers 采野花, wildflower seed mix 野花混种
[联] flower 花, meadow 草地, grass 草, native 本地的, bloom 开花, garden 花园, prairie 草原
[记] 可数。wild + flower 复合；环保倡导「勿采野花」leave no trace

**ivy**
英/美 /ˈaɪvi/
n. 常春藤
[义] A climbing evergreen plant with dark shiny leaves, often on walls. 攀缘常绿、叶深绿有光泽，常见于墙面（ivy-covered wall 常春藤覆墙, poison ivy 毒葛〔另种〕）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Ivy climbed the stone walls of the old library. 常春藤爬上旧图书馆石墙。 ② She trimmed the ivy away from the windows. 她把窗边的常春藤剪开。
[搭] ivy-covered wall 常春藤覆墙, climbing ivy 攀缘常春藤, English ivy 英国常春藤, ivy league 常春藤盟校（另义）
[联] vine 藤, climb 攀爬, evergreen 常绿, wall 墙, leaf 叶, plant 植物, shade 荫
[记] 不可数/可数。Ivy League 指美国名校联盟，与植物义关联历史。poison ivy 为毒葛，触之起疹

**cactus**
英/美 /ˈkæktəs/
n. 仙人掌
（复数 cacti /ˈkæktaɪ/ 或 cactuses）
[义] A plant with thick fleshy stems and spines, adapted to dry places. 茎厚肉质、有刺、适应干燥环境的植物（desert cactus 沙漠仙人掌, cactus garden 仙人掌园）。可数。
[例] ① A cactus survived months without rain on the windowsill. 一盆仙人掌在窗台上数月无雨仍存活。 ② Cacti store water in their thick stems. 仙人掌在粗茎中储水。
[搭] desert cactus 沙漠仙人掌, cactus garden 仙人掌园, cactus spine 仙人掌刺, potted cactus 盆栽仙人掌
[联] desert 沙漠, spine 刺, succulent 多肉, dry 干的, drought 干旱, stem 茎, bloom 开花
[记] 复数 cacti 或 cactuses。desert 生态典型植物；与 succulent 常并提

**succulent**
英/美 /ˈsʌkjələnt/
n. 多肉植物
adj. 多汁的
[义] A plant with thick leaves or stems that store water; full of juice. 叶或茎肥厚储水的植物；多汁的（succulent plants 多肉, succulent garden 多肉园, succulent leaves 多肉叶）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① She collects succulents in ceramic pots on the balcony. 她在阳台陶盆里收集多肉。 ② Succulents need little water and plenty of light. 多肉需水少、需光足。
[搭] succulent plants 多肉植物, succulent garden 多肉园, succulent leaves 多肉叶, potted succulent 盆栽多肉
[联] cactus 仙人掌, drought 干旱, stem 茎, leaf 叶, water 储水, desert 沙漠, garden 花园
[记] 可数/形容词。succulent 作形容词亦可形容「多汁的」（succulent steak），依语境区分

**lily**
英/美 /ˈlɪli/
n. 百合；百合花
[义] A large often white flower with six petals, growing from a bulb; a symbol of purity in many cultures. 常开大花、六瓣，由鳞茎生长；多文化中象征纯洁（water lily 睡莲, lily pad 荷叶, Easter lily 复活节百合）。可数。
[例] ① White lilies filled the church for the ceremony. 教堂里摆满白百合用于仪式。 ② Water lilies floated on the pond surface. 睡莲浮在池塘水面。
[搭] water lily 睡莲, lily pad 荷叶, Easter lily 复活节百合, lily bulb 百合鳞茎
[联] flower 花, petal 花瓣, bulb 鳞茎, pond 池塘, white 白的, fragrance 芬芳, garden 花园
[记] 可数：a lily。gild the lily 喻「画蛇添足」。water lily 为睡莲，水生

**lavender**
英/美 /ˈlævəndə(r)/
n. 薰衣草
adj. 淡紫色的
[义] A plant with purple flowers and a sweet scent, used in gardens and oils. 开紫花、香气甜，用于园艺与精油（lavender field 薰衣草田, lavender oil 薰衣草油, lavender scent 薰衣草香）。不可数/可数/形容词。
[例] ① Lavender rows stretched across the Provence hillside. 普罗旺斯山坡上薰衣草成行。 ② She dried lavender bundles for the linen closet. 她扎束晾干薰衣草放衣柜。
[搭] lavender field 薰衣草田, lavender oil 薰衣草油, lavender scent 薰衣草香, lavender bush 薰衣草丛
[联] herb 香草, purple 紫的, fragrance 芬芳, garden 花园, flower 花, field 田, scent 香气
[记] 不可数/可数/形容词。lavender 亦作淡紫色；南法旅游与园艺常见

**daffodil**
英/美 /ˈdæfədɪl/
n. 水仙；黄水仙
[义] A yellow spring flower with a trumpet-shaped centre, growing from a bulb. 春季黄花，花心如喇叭，由球茎生长（daffodil bulb 水仙球茎, field of daffodils 水仙花田）。可数。
[例] ① Daffodils announced spring along the riverbank. 河岸水仙报春。 ② Wordsworth's poem celebrates daffodils by the lake. 华兹华斯的诗歌咏湖畔水仙。
[搭] daffodil bulb 水仙球茎, field of daffodils 水仙花田, spring daffodils 春水仙, golden daffodils 金水仙
[联] bulb 球茎, spring 春天, flower 花, yellow 黄的, tulip 郁金香, garden 花园, bloom 开花
[记] 可数：a daffodil。英国文化符号；与 Wordsworth 名诗关联

**willow**
英/美 /ˈwɪləʊ/
n. 柳树
[义] A tree with long thin branches that hang down, often near water. 枝细长下垂的树，常近水（weeping willow 垂柳, willow tree 柳树, willow branch 柳枝）。可数。
[例] ① A weeping willow shaded the pond in the park. 公园里垂柳荫蔽池塘。 ② They wove baskets from flexible willow branches. 他们用柔韧柳枝编篮。
[搭] weeping willow 垂柳, willow tree 柳树, willow branch 柳枝, willow bark 柳树皮
[联] tree 树, branch 枝, water 水, pond 池塘, bend 垂弯, flexible 柔韧的, leaf 叶
[记] 可数：a willow。weeping willow 为垂柳；willow 常与 water、melancholy 意象相连

**birch**
英/美 /bɜːtʃ/
n. 桦树；桦木
[义] A tree with smooth bark that peels in thin layers; its wood. 树皮光滑、薄层剥落的树；其木材（birch tree 桦树, birch bark 桦树皮, silver birch 白桦）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Silver birch gleamed against the dark pines. 白桦在暗松间闪亮。 ② Canoes were once made from birch bark. 独木舟曾用桦树皮制作。
[搭] birch tree 桦树, birch bark 桦树皮, silver birch 白桦, birch forest 桦林
[联] tree 树, bark 树皮, forest 森林, wood 木材, leaf 叶, pine 松, paper 纸（桦皮纸）
[记] 可数/不可数。北欧与白桦林文化关联；birch 亦指「桦条鞭」，不同义

**cedar**
英/美 /ˈsiːdə(r)/
n. 雪松；香柏
[义] An evergreen tree with fragrant wood; the wood itself. 木材芳香的常绿树；其木材（cedar tree 雪松, cedar wood 香柏木, cedar chest 雪松箱）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Cedar trees towered above the mountain trail. 雪松耸立在山道上方。 ② The closet was lined with cedar to repel moths. 衣柜衬香柏木防蛀。
[搭] cedar tree 雪松, cedar wood 香柏木, cedar chest 雪松箱, cedar forest 雪松林
[联] evergreen 常绿, tree 树, wood 木材, pine 松, forest 森林, fragrance 芬芳, chest 箱
[记] 可数/不可数。cedar 木材防虫；黎巴嫩雪松为历史与文化符号

**wither**
英/美 /ˈwɪðə(r)/
v. 枯萎；凋谢
[义] To dry up and die; to lose freshness or strength. 干枯死亡；失去新鲜或活力（withered leaves 枯叶, wither away 枯萎/衰落）。动词。
[例] ① Without rain, the crops withered in the field. 无雨，庄稼在田里枯死。 ② Cut roses wither within days unless kept in water. 切玫瑰不泡水几天就蔫枯。
[搭] withered leaves 枯叶, wither away 枯萎/衰落, wither on the vine 未熟即枯（喻）
[联] wilt 蔫, dry 干, die 死, fade 凋谢, decay 腐烂, drought 干旱, leaf 叶
[记] 不及物/及物。wither 比 wilt 更偏「彻底枯死」；wither away 可喻组织、希望「萎缩」

**peony**
英/美 /ˈpiːəni/
n. 牡丹；芍药
[义] A large fragrant garden flower with many soft petals, blooming in late spring. 晚春开放、瓣多层、常香的大型园艺花卉（peony bush 牡丹丛, peony season 牡丹季, tree peony 芍药）。可数。
[例] ① Peonies opened in heavy pink clusters along the fence. 牡丹沿篱笆绽成厚重的粉色花簇。 ② She cut a few peonies for the dining table. 她剪了几枝牡丹摆餐桌。
[搭] peony bush 牡丹丛, peony season 牡丹季, tree peony 芍药, cut peonies 剪牡丹
[联] flower 花, garden 花园, petal 花瓣, bloom 开花, spring 春天, fragrance 芬芳, rose 玫瑰
[记] 可数：a peony。复数 peonies；勿与 penny（便士）混淆

**chrysanthemum**
英/美 /krɪˈsænθəməm/
n. 菊花
[义] An autumn flower with many narrow petals, popular in gardens and as a cut flower. 秋季开放、瓣细长，常见于园艺与切花（chrysanthemum show 菊展, potted chrysanthemum 盆栽菊）。可数。
[例] ① Chrysanthemums filled the market stalls in October. 十月市场货摊上摆满菊花。 ② She entered her best chrysanthemums in the village show. 她把最好的菊花送去参加乡村花展。
[搭] chrysanthemum show 菊展, potted chrysanthemum 盆栽菊, autumn chrysanthemums 秋菊, cut chrysanthemums 切花菊
[联] flower 花, autumn 秋天, garden 花园, petal 花瓣, bloom 开花, daisy 雏菊, yellow 黄的
[记] 可数：a chrysanthemum。口语常简称 mums；东亚文化中菊花象征高洁，西方亦作悼念用花

**carnation**
英/美 /kɑːˈneɪʃn/
n. 康乃馨；麝香石竹
[义] A sweet-smelling garden flower with ruffled petals, often pink or red, used in bouquets. 瓣有皱边、常粉红或红、用于花束的芳香花卉（red carnation 红康乃馨, Mother's Day carnations 母亲节康乃馨）。可数。
[例] ① He bought a bunch of carnations for his mother. 他给母亲买了一束康乃馨。 ② Carnations last longer than many cut flowers in a vase. 康乃馨在花瓶里比许多切花更耐放。
[搭] red carnation 红康乃馨, Mother's Day carnations 母亲节康乃馨, cut carnations 切花康乃馨, carnation bouquet 康乃馨花束
[联] flower 花, petal 花瓣, bouquet 花束, fragrance 芬芳, rose 玫瑰, gift 礼物, pink 粉的
[记] 可数：a carnation。母亲节与教师节常见用花；亦可指「肉色/粉红色」

**violet**
英/美 /ˈvaɪələt/
n. 紫罗兰；堇菜
[义] A small purple or white wildflower; the colour purple. 小型紫或白野花；紫色（wild violets 野紫罗兰, African violet 非洲堇〔室内〕）。可数/不可数（颜色）。形容词 violet 指紫色。
[例] ① Wild violets dotted the shady bank of the stream. 野紫罗兰点缀在溪岸荫处。 ② She kept an African violet on the kitchen windowsill. 她在厨房窗台上养了一盆非洲堇。
[搭] wild violets 野紫罗兰, African violet 非洲堇, violet flower 紫罗兰花, shy as a violet 羞怯如紫罗兰（喻）
[联] flower 花, purple 紫的, wildflower 野花, shade 荫, petal 花瓣, spring 春天, colour 颜色
[记] 可数/颜色不可数。shy as a violet 喻「极害羞」。与 violent 拼写不同

**jasmine**
英/美 /ˈdʒæzmɪn/
n. 茉莉；素馨
[义] A climbing or bush plant with small white or yellow fragrant flowers. 攀缘或灌木状、开小白或黄花且芳香的植物（jasmine tea 茉莉花茶, jasmine vine 茉莉藤, night-blooming jasmine 夜香木）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Jasmine scent drifted from the garden gate at dusk. 黄昏时茉莉香从花园门飘出。 ② She planted jasmine beside the porch trellis. 她在门廊棚架旁种了茉莉。
[搭] jasmine tea 茉莉花茶, jasmine vine 茉莉藤, jasmine flower 茉莉花, night-blooming jasmine 夜香木
[联] vine 藤, fragrance 芬芳, flower 花, tea 茶, climb 攀爬, white 白的, garden 花园
[记] 不可数/可数。jasmine tea 为常见饮品；南亚与东亚文化中茉莉象征纯洁、欢迎

**hibiscus**
英/美 /hɪˈbɪskəs/
n. 木槿；芙蓉
[义] A tropical plant with large colourful trumpet-shaped flowers. 热带植物，开大朵艳丽喇叭形花（hibiscus flower 木槿花, hibiscus tea 洛神花茶, tropical hibiscus 热带木槿）。可数。
[例] ① Red hibiscus flowers lined the hotel walkway. 红色木槿花排列在酒店走道旁。 ② She drank hibiscus tea iced on hot afternoons. 炎热午后她喝冰洛神花茶。
[搭] hibiscus flower 木槿花, hibiscus tea 洛神花茶, tropical hibiscus 热带木槿, hibiscus bush 木槿丛
[联] flower 花, tropical 热带的, petal 花瓣, tea 茶, red 红的, garden 花园, shrub 灌木
[记] 可数：a hibiscus。夏威夷州花；hibiscus tea 亦称 roselle tea

**marigold**
英/美 /ˈmærɪɡəʊld/
n. 万寿菊
[义] A bright orange or yellow garden flower that blooms for a long season. 亮橙或黄色、花期长的园艺花卉（French marigold 法国万寿菊, marigold border 万寿菊边饰）。可数。
[例] ① Marigolds added cheerful colour to the vegetable beds. 万寿菊给菜床添了明快色彩。 ② Gardeners plant marigolds to deter some pests. 园丁种万寿菊以驱部分害虫。
[搭] French marigold 法国万寿菊, marigold border 万寿菊边饰, plant marigolds 种万寿菊, golden marigolds 金万寿菊
[联] flower 花, annual 一年生, garden 花园, orange 橙的, yellow 黄的, pest 害虫, bloom 开花
[记] 可数：a marigold。annual 词条已述；与 gold 联想可助记

**hydrangea**
英/美 /haɪˈdreɪndʒə/
n. 绣球花
[义] A bush with large round clusters of pink, blue, or white flowers. 开大圆簇粉、蓝或白花的灌木（hydrangea bush 绣球丛, blue hydrangeas 蓝绣球, hydrangea season 绣球季）。可数。
[例] ① Hydrangeas turned blue in the acidic soil near the pine trees. 松树旁酸性土里的绣球变蓝。 ② She dried hydrangea heads for winter arrangements. 她把绣球花头晾干作冬日插花。
[搭] hydrangea bush 绣球丛, blue hydrangeas 蓝绣球, hydrangea season 绣球季, dried hydrangeas 干绣球
[联] shrub 灌木, flower 花, garden 花园, pink 粉的, blue 蓝的, bloom 开花, cluster 花簇
[记] 可数：a hydrangea。土壤 pH 可影响花色；日韩园艺与婚礼常见

**magnolia**
英/美 /mæɡˈnəʊliə/
n. 木兰；玉兰花
[义] A tree or bush with large fragrant flowers, often white or pink, blooming in spring. 春开大花、常白或粉、有香气的树或灌木（magnolia tree 木兰树, magnolia blossom 玉兰花, southern magnolia 南方木兰）。可数。
[例] ① Magnolia blossoms opened before the leaves appeared. 玉兰先花后叶。 ② The street was named for the magnolia trees planted in 1920. 这条街因 1920 年所植木兰而得名。
[搭] magnolia tree 木兰树, magnolia blossom 玉兰花, southern magnolia 南方木兰, magnolia petals 木兰瓣
[联] tree 树, flower 花, blossom 花朵, spring 春天, fragrance 芬芳, petal 花瓣, white 白的
[记] 可数：a magnolia。美国南方象征；花瓣常厚而蜡质

**pansy**
英/美 /ˈpænzi/
n. 三色堇；蝴蝶花
[义] A small garden flower with velvety petals in purple, yellow, or white, often with dark markings. 瓣如天鹅绒、紫黄白相间、常带深色斑纹的小型园艺花（winter pansies 冬三色堇, pansy bed 三色堇花坛）。可数。
[例] ① Pansies survived light frosts in the window boxes. 三色堇在窗台花箱里经住了轻霜。 ② She planted pansies for colour through the cool months. 她种三色堇以在凉季保持色彩。
[搭] winter pansies 冬三色堇, pansy bed 三色堇花坛, plant pansies 种三色堇, velvety pansies 绒瓣三色堇
[联] flower 花, garden 花园, petal 花瓣, purple 紫的, winter 冬天, annual 一年生, colour 颜色
[记] 可数：a pansy。英式 insult pansy 指「懦弱者」，与花义语境完全不同

**cherry**
英/美 /ˈtʃeri/
n. 樱桃；樱桃树
[义] A small round stone fruit; the tree that bears it. 小型核果；结果之树（cherry tree 樱桃树, cherry blossom 樱花, cherry orchard 樱桃园）。可数。本章取物种/树义；果酱、派等用法在食物章。
[例] ① Cherry blossoms drew crowds to the park each April. 每年四月樱花把人群吸引到公园。 ② They picked cherries from the trees behind the house. 他们从屋后树上摘樱桃。
[搭] cherry tree 樱桃树, cherry blossom 樱花, cherry orchard 樱桃园, wild cherry 野樱桃
[联] tree 树, blossom 花朵, fruit 果实, orchard 果园, spring 春天, pink 粉的, flower 花
[记] 可数。cherry blossom 为日本文化符号；cherry-pick 喻「精挑细选」，不同义

**elm**
英/美 /elm/
n. 榆树
[义] A large tree with spreading branches and serrated leaves; its wood. 枝开展、叶有锯齿的大树；其木材（elm tree 榆树, Dutch elm disease 荷兰榆病, elm wood 榆木）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Elms once lined many streets in New England towns. 新英格兰城镇许多街道曾以榆树成行。 ② Dutch elm disease wiped out millions of trees. 荷兰榆病使数百万株树死亡。
[搭] elm tree 榆树, Dutch elm disease 荷兰榆病, elm wood 榆木, street elms 街道榆树
[联] tree 树, leaf 叶, forest 森林, disease 病害, wood 木材, branch 枝, deciduous 落叶的
[记] 可数/不可数。Dutch elm disease 为 20 世纪欧美标志性生态事件

**poplar**
英/美 /ˈpɒplə(r)/
n. 杨树
[义] A fast-growing tall tree with narrow leaves that flutter in the wind. 生长快、窄叶在风中颤动的乔木（poplar tree 杨树, poplar grove 杨树林, Lombardy poplar 伦巴第杨）。可数。
[例] ① Poplars rustled along the irrigation canal. 杨树沿灌溉渠沙沙作响。 ② Farmers planted poplars as windbreaks around the fields. 农民在田周种杨作防风林。
[搭] poplar tree 杨树, poplar grove 杨树林, Lombardy poplar 伦巴第杨, white poplar 白杨
[联] tree 树, leaf 叶, wind 风, fast-growing 速生的, forest 森林, grove 小树林, timber 木材
[记] 可数：a poplar。Lombardy poplar 为典型行道树；与 popular（流行的）勿混

**spruce**
英/美 /spruːs/
n. 云杉
v.  spruce up 打扮整洁
[义] An evergreen tree with needle-like leaves and cones; to make neat and smart. 针叶常绿、结球果的树；使整洁漂亮（spruce forest 云杉林, Norway spruce 挪威云杉, spruce up 打扮）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Spruce trees covered the mountain slopes above the village. 云杉覆盖村庄上方山坡。 ② She spruced up the porch with potted evergreens. 她用盆栽常绿植物把门廊打扮整洁。
[搭] spruce forest 云杉林, Norway spruce 挪威云杉, spruce up 打扮整洁, spruce tree 云杉
[联] pine 松, fir 冷杉, conifer 针叶树, evergreen 常绿, forest 森林, needle 针叶, cone 球果
[记] 可数/动词。spruce up 与树木义同形；Christmas trees 常用 Norway spruce

**redwood**
英/美 /ˈredwʊd/
n. 红杉；红木
[义] A very tall evergreen tree of the western US, or the reddish wood from it. 美国西部极高常绿针叶树，或其红褐色木材（coastal redwood 海岸红杉, redwood forest 红杉林, giant redwood 巨红杉）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Coastal redwoods are among the tallest trees on Earth. 海岸红杉是地球上最高的树之一。 ② Fog sustains the redwood forests of northern California. 雾维系北加州红杉林。
[搭] coastal redwood 海岸红杉, redwood forest 红杉林, giant redwood 巨红杉, redwood grove 红杉林
[联] tree 树, forest 森林, conifer 针叶树, evergreen 常绿, sequoia 巨杉, tall 高的, California 加州
[记] 可数/不可数。coastal redwood（Sequoia sempervirens）与 giant sequoia（巨杉）不同种

**fir**
英/美 /fɜː(r)/
n. 冷杉；枞
[义] An evergreen tree with flat needle-like leaves and upright cones. 叶扁平如针、球果直立的常绿树（fir tree 冷杉, Douglas fir 道格拉斯冷杉, fir forest 冷杉林）。可数。
[例] ① Fir branches scented the cabin throughout December. 十二月里冷杉枝让木屋充满香气。 ② Douglas firs tower above the forests of the Pacific Northwest. 道格拉斯冷杉耸立太平洋西北部的林冠之上。
[搭] fir tree 冷杉, Douglas fir 道格拉斯冷杉, fir forest 冷杉林, fir cone 冷杉球果
[联] pine 松, spruce 云杉, conifer 针叶树, evergreen 常绿, Christmas 圣诞, forest 森林, needle 针叶
[记] 可数：a fir。fir 与 fur（毛皮）发音相近；圣诞装饰常用 fir branches

**conifer**
英/美 /ˈkɒnɪfə(r)/
n. 针叶树
[义] A tree that produces cones and usually has needle-shaped or scale-like leaves. 产球果、通常叶呈针状或鳞状的树（conifer forest 针叶林, evergreen conifers 常绿针叶树）。可数。
[例] ① Conifers dominate the higher elevations of the range. 针叶树在该山脉较高海拔占主导。 ② The trail wound through dark conifer woods. 小径蜿蜒穿过幽暗针叶林。
[搭] conifer forest 针叶林, evergreen conifers 常绿针叶树, conifer species 针叶树种, native conifers 本地针叶树
[联] pine 松, fir 冷杉, spruce 云杉, evergreen 常绿, cone 球果, needle 针叶, forest 森林
[记] 可数：a conifer。coni-（锥/球果）+ fer（携带）→ 「带球果的树」

**grove**
英/美 /ɡrəʊv/
n. 小树林；果树林
[义] A small group of trees, especially fruit trees or trees planted for shade. 一小群树，尤指果树或为遮荫而植者（olive grove 橄榄园, orange grove 柑橘园, bamboo grove 竹林）。可数。
[例] ① They picnicked in an olive grove overlooking the valley. 他们在俯瞰山谷的橄榄园中野餐。 ② A lemon grove surrounded the old farmhouse. 老农舍四周是柠檬园。
[搭] olive grove 橄榄园, orange grove 柑橘园, bamboo grove 竹林, lemon grove 柠檬园
[联] orchard 果园, tree 树, forest 森林, woodland 林地, plantation 种植园, shade 荫, fruit 果实
[记] 可数：a grove。grove 比 forest 小、常人工；与 grave（坟墓）勿混

**vineyard**
英/美 /ˈvɪnjəd/
n. 葡萄园
[义] A plantation of grapevines, especially for wine. 种植葡萄藤的园地，尤指酿酒用（work in a vineyard 在葡萄园劳作, vineyard row 葡萄园行, family vineyard 家族葡萄园）。可数。
[例] ① Rows of vines stretched across the hillside vineyard. 葡萄藤成行铺展在山坡葡萄园。 ② Tourists toured the vineyard before the wine tasting. 游客在品酒前参观了葡萄园。
[搭] work in a vineyard 在葡萄园劳作, vineyard row 葡萄园行, family vineyard 家族葡萄园, hillside vineyard 山坡葡萄园
[联] vine 藤, grape 葡萄, wine 葡萄酒, harvest 收获, orchard 果园, row 行, plantation 种植园
[记] 可数：a vineyard。vine + yard；葡萄酒产区旅游高频词

**plantation**
英/美 /plænˈteɪʃn/
n. 种植园；人工林
[义] A large area where crops such as tea, coffee, rubber, or trees are grown. 大面积栽培茶、咖啡、橡胶或树木等的土地（tea plantation 茶园, rubber plantation 橡胶园, tree plantation 人工林）。可数。
[例] ① Tea plantations cover the hills in parts of Sri Lanka. 斯里兰卡部分地区丘陵为茶园覆盖。 ② The old plantation house overlooked rows of sugar cane. 旧种植园宅邸俯瞰一行行甘蔗。
[搭] tea plantation 茶园, rubber plantation 橡胶园, tree plantation 人工林, plantation crop 种植园作物
[联] crop 作物, farm 农场, harvest 收获, field 田, grove 小树林, colonial 殖民的, labour 劳工
[记] 可数：a plantation。历史语境常涉殖民与奴隶制；现代亦指林业人工林

**mulch**
英/美 /mʌltʃ/
n. 覆盖物
v. 覆盖根部
[义] Material such as straw or bark spread over soil to protect plants and retain moisture. 覆于土表以保护植株、保水的材料，如稻草、树皮（wood chip mulch 木屑覆盖, mulch the beds 给苗床覆盖）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① She spread mulch around the roses to keep roots cool. 她在玫瑰周围铺覆盖物以保根凉爽。 ② A thick mulch reduced weeds in the vegetable beds. 厚覆盖物减少了菜床杂草。
[搭] wood chip mulch 木屑覆盖, mulch the beds 给床覆盖, organic mulch 有机覆盖物, spread mulch 铺覆盖物
[联] compost 堆肥, soil 土壤, garden 花园, weed 杂草, moisture 水分, bark 树皮, protect 保护
[记] 不可数/动词。mulch 与 compost 不同：mulch 多覆于表土，compost 混入土中

**graft**
英/美 /ɡrɑːft/
n. 嫁接；接穗
v. 嫁接
[义] To join a shoot from one plant onto another so they grow together; the joined part. 将一植株的枝接到另一株上使其愈合生长；接合部（graft a branch 嫁接枝条, skin graft 皮肤移植〔医学另义〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The orchard worker grafted scion wood onto rootstock. 果园工人把接穗嫁接到砧木上。 ② Apple varieties are often preserved through grafting. 许多苹果品种靠嫁接保留。
[搭] graft a branch 嫁接枝条, graft onto rootstock 嫁接到砧木, successful graft 嫁接成功, graft union 嫁接愈合部
[联] tree 树, branch 枝, orchard 果园, grow 生长, plant 植物, rootstock 砧木, scion 接穗
[记] 可数/动词。skin graft 为医学义；园艺 graft 为动/名

**mow**
英/美 /məʊ/
v. 割（草）
[义] To cut grass with a machine or tool. 用机器或工具割草（mow the lawn 割草坪, mowing season 割草季）。不规则动词。
[形] mow / mowed / mowed或mown / mowing
[例] ① He mows the lawn every Saturday morning. 他每周六早晨割草坪。 ② The field was mown for hay before the rain. 雨前田地已割晒干草。
[搭] mow the lawn 割草坪, mowing season 割草季, freshly mown grass 新割的草, lawn mower 割草机
[联] lawn 草坪, grass 草, cut 割, trim 修剪, garden 花园, hay 干草, field 田
[记] 不规则：mow / mowed / mown。make hay while the sun shines 与割晒干草相关

**trellis**
英/美 /ˈtrelɪs/
n. 棚架
v. 用棚架支撑
[义] A framework of light wooden or metal bars for climbing plants. 供攀缘植物攀爬的轻木或金属格架（rose trellis 玫瑰棚架, trellis against the wall 靠墙棚架）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Jasmine climbed the trellis beside the garden gate. 茉莉爬上花园门旁的棚架。 ② They built a trellis for the grapevines along the fence. 他们沿篱笆为葡萄藤搭了棚架。
[搭] rose trellis 玫瑰棚架, trellis against the wall 靠墙棚架, wooden trellis 木棚架, climb a trellis 爬棚架
[联] vine 藤, climb 攀爬, garden 花园, rose 玫瑰, fence 篱笆, support 支撑, jasmine 茉莉
[记] 可数/动词。trellis 常见于英式村舍花园描述

**trim**
英/美 /trɪm/
v. 修剪；整理
n. 修剪
[义] To cut away the unwanted parts of a bush, hedge, or lawn edge to make it neat. 剪去灌木、树篱或草坪边缘多余部分使其整齐（trim the hedge 修树篱, trim the lawn edges 修草坪边）。动词/可数名词。
[例] ① She trimmed the box hedge into a smooth curve. 她把黄杨树篱修成平滑弧线。 ② Regular trimming keeps the roses healthy. 定期修剪使玫瑰保持健康。
[搭] trim the hedge 修树篱, trim the lawn edges 修草坪边, trim back 剪短, hedge trimming 修篱
[联] prune 修剪, hedge 树篱, cut 剪, neat 整齐的, garden 花园, shrub 灌木, mow 割草
[记] 及物动词。trim 通用；prune 更专指果树/玫瑰等修枝

**biennial**
英/美 /baɪˈeniəl/
n. 二年生植物
adj. 两年一次的；二年生的
[义] A plant that completes its life cycle in two years; happening every two years. 两年完成生命周期的植物；每两年发生（biennial flowers 二年生花卉, biennial plant 二年生植物）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Foxgloves are biennials that bloom in the second year. 毛地黄为二年生，第二年开花。 ② She sowed biennials in trays to transplant later. 她在育苗盘播二年生植物，稍后移栽。
[搭] biennial flowers 二年生花卉, biennial plant 二年生植物, biennial cycle 两年周期
[联] annual 一年生, perennial 多年生, flower 花, seed 种子, garden 花园, bloom 开花, cycle 周期
[记] 可数/形容词。bi-（二）+ ann-（年）；与 biannual（一年两次）不同

**nectar**
英/美 /ˈnektə(r)/
n. 花蜜
[义] A sweet liquid produced by flowers that attracts bees and other pollinators. 花分泌的甜液，吸引蜜蜂等传粉者（nectar and pollen 花蜜与花粉, collect nectar 采蜜）。不可数。
[例] ① Bees returned to the hive heavy with nectar. 蜜蜂满载花蜜回巢。 ② Hummingbirds feed on nectar from tubular flowers. 蜂鸟取食管状花的花蜜。
[搭] nectar and pollen 花蜜与花粉, collect nectar 采蜜, sweet nectar 甘甜花蜜, flower nectar 花蜜
[联] pollen 花粉, bee 蜜蜂, flower 花, butterfly 蝴蝶, sweet 甜的, pollinate 授粉, hive 蜂巢
[记] 不可数。nectar 亦指「琼浆玉液」；希腊神话中为神饮

**frond**
英/美 /frɒnd/
n. 叶；羽状叶
[义] A large divided leaf of a fern or palm. 蕨类或棕榈的大裂叶（palm frond 棕榈叶, fern frond 蕨叶）。可数。
[例] ① Palm fronds shaded the beach chairs. 棕榈叶荫蔽了沙滩椅。 ② She traced the pattern of veins on a fern frond. 她描摹蕨叶上的脉纹。
[搭] palm frond 棕榈叶, fern frond 蕨叶, tropical fronds 热带羽叶, wave a frond 挥棕榈叶
[联] fern 蕨, palm 棕榈, leaf 叶, tropical 热带的, vein 叶脉, shade 荫, plant 植物
[记] 可数：a frond。棕榈 frond 在热带、宗教仪式 imagery 中常见

**lichen**
英/美 /ˈlaɪkən/
n. 地衣
[义] A slow-growing plant-like organism formed from algae and fungus living together, often on rocks or tree bark. 藻与菌共生、生长缓慢、类植物体，常见于岩石或树皮（lichen on rocks 岩石地衣, tree lichen 树生地衣）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Grey lichen covered the old stone wall in the forest. 灰色地衣覆满林中旧石墙。 ② Clean air allows lichen to thrive on tree trunks. 洁净空气使地衣在树干上繁茂。
[搭] lichen on rocks 岩石地衣, tree lichen 树生地衣, grey lichen 灰地衣, lichen growth 地衣生长
[联] moss 苔藓, bark 树皮, rock 岩石, fungus 真菌, algae 藻类, forest 森林, slow 缓慢
[记] 不可数/可数。地衣为空气质量指示生物；与 liken（比拟）同音异义

**thicket**
英/美 /ˈθɪkɪt/
n. 灌木丛；密林
[义] A dense group of bushes or small trees. 灌木或小树密集丛生处（dense thicket 密灌丛, thicket of bushes 灌木丛）。可数。
[例] ① The path disappeared into a thicket of brambles. 小径没入 bramble 灌丛。 ② Birds nested hidden in the riverside thicket. 鸟藏在河边灌丛中筑巢。
[搭] dense thicket 密灌丛, thicket of bushes 灌木丛, push through a thicket 穿过灌丛
[联] bush 灌木, shrub 灌木, forest 森林, dense 密的, wild 野生的, bramble 黑莓丛, hide 隐藏
[记] 可数：a thicket。thick + -et（小）→ 密小丛；文学中常作 concealment 场景

**barley**
英/美 /ˈbɑːli/
n. 大麦
[义] A cereal plant whose grain is used for food, animal feed, and brewing. 谷物作物，其粒用于食、饲与酿造（barley field 大麦田, barley crop 大麦收成, pearl barley 珍珠大麦）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Golden barley rippled before the summer harvest. 夏收前大麦起伏如浪。 ② Farmers rotated barley with legumes to enrich the soil. 农民以大麦与豆类轮作肥土。
[搭] barley field 大麦田, barley crop 大麦收成, pearl barley 珍珠大麦, grow barley 种大麦
[联] wheat 小麦, grain 谷物, crop 作物, harvest 收获, field 田, beer 啤酒, farm 农场
[记] 不可数/可数。barley 与 brewing 关联深；pearl barley 为去壳圆粒

**soybean**
英/美 /ˈsɔɪbiːn/
n. 大豆
[义] A bean plant widely grown for its protein-rich seeds and oil. 广泛种植、籽富蛋白与油的豆类作物（soybean field 大豆田, soybean crop 大豆收成, genetically modified soybeans 转基因大豆）。可数/通常复数 soybeans。
[例] ① Soybeans fix nitrogen in the soil through root nodules. 大豆通过根瘤固氮。 ② The region shifted from corn to soybeans over the decade. 十年间该地区从玉米转向大豆。
[搭] soybean field 大豆田, soybean crop 大豆收成, grow soybeans 种大豆, soybean plant 大豆植株
[联] bean 豆, crop 作物, field 田, protein 蛋白质, oil 油, harvest 收获, legume 豆类
[记] 可数/复数 soybeans。亦拼 soya bean（英式）；加工食品义在食物章

**sugarcane**
英/美 /ˈʃʊɡəkeɪn/
n. 甘蔗
[义] A tall tropical grass whose thick stems contain sugar. 热带 tall 禾本，粗茎含蔗糖（sugarcane field 蔗田, sugarcane harvest 甘蔗采收, sugarcane plantation 甘蔗种植园）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Workers cut sugarcane by hand in the heat. 工人在酷暑中手工砍蔗。 ② Sugarcane rows stretched to the horizon in Louisiana. 路易斯安那蔗行延至天际。
[搭] sugarcane field 蔗田, sugarcane harvest 甘蔗采收, sugarcane plantation 甘蔗种植园, cut sugarcane 砍蔗
[联] sugar 糖, crop 作物, plantation 种植园, tropical 热带的, harvest 收获, field 田, grass 草
[记] 不可数/可数。亦写 sugar cane（两词）；本章取作物/植株义

**tomato**
英/美 /təˈmɑːtəʊ/
n. 番茄；西红柿
[义] A soft red fruit eaten as a vegetable, grown on vines in gardens and fields. 软红果实、作蔬菜食，种于园田藤上（tomato plant 番茄植株, cherry tomato 小番茄, tomato vine 番茄藤）。可数。
[例] ① Tomato plants need support as they grow taller. 番茄愈长愈高需要支架。 ② She staked the tomato vines in the kitchen garden. 她在厨房菜园里给番茄藤搭桩。
[搭] tomato plant 番茄植株, cherry tomato 小番茄, tomato vine 番茄藤, grow tomatoes 种番茄
[联] vine 藤, garden 花园, fruit 果实, red 红的, crop 作物, harvest 收获, seed 种子
[记] 可数：a tomato。植物学上为 fruit；烹饪与食材用法在食物章

**potato**
英/美 /pəˈteɪtəʊ/
n. 马铃薯；土豆
[义] A round brown tuber grown underground, eaten as a staple food. 地下生长的棕褐色块茎，作主食（potato plant 马铃薯植株, potato field 马铃薯田, seed potato 种薯）。可数。
[例] ① Potato plants flowered above the hills of soil. 马铃薯植株在土垄上开花。 ② Late blight destroyed the potato crop in the region. 晚疫病毁掉了该地区马铃薯收成。
[搭] potato plant 马铃薯植株, potato field 马铃薯田, seed potato 种薯, grow potatoes 种马铃薯
[联] root 根, crop 作物, field 田, harvest 收获, tuber 块茎, plant 植株, farm 农场
[记] 可数：a potato。爱尔兰大饥荒与 potato blight 历史关联；烹饪义在食物章

**lettuce**
英/美 /ˈletɪs/
n. 莴苣；生菜
[义] A plant with large green leaves eaten raw in salads. 大叶绿色、常生食拌沙拉的植物（lettuce bed 菜床, head of lettuce 一整棵生菜, leaf lettuce 散叶生菜）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Lettuce bolted quickly once the weather turned hot. 天一热生菜很快抽薹。 ② She planted lettuce between the rows of tomatoes. 她在番茄行间种了生菜。
[搭] lettuce bed 菜床, head of lettuce 一整棵生菜, leaf lettuce 散叶生菜, grow lettuce 种生菜
[联] salad 沙拉, garden 花园, leaf 叶, green 绿的, vegetable 蔬菜, crop 作物, harvest 收获
[记] 不可数/可数。bolt 指「抽薹开花」；食材用法在食物章

**grape**
英/美 /ɡreɪp/
n. 葡萄
[义] A small round soft fruit growing in clusters on vines; used for eating or wine. 藤上成串生长的小圆软果；鲜食或酿酒（grape vine 葡萄藤, table grapes 鲜食葡萄, grape cluster 葡萄串）。可数/通常复数 grapes。
[例] ① Grapes hung heavy on the vines before harvest. 采收前葡萄沉甸甸挂在藤上。 ② The vineyard grows grapes for both wine and juice. 葡萄园既酿酒也榨汁。
[搭] grape vine 葡萄藤, table grapes 鲜食葡萄, grape cluster 葡萄串, wild grapes 野葡萄
[联] vine 藤, vineyard 葡萄园, wine 葡萄酒, fruit 果实, harvest 收获, cluster 串, purple 紫的
[记] 可数/复数 grapes。grapevine 亦指「小道消息网」——比喻义

**fade**
英/美 /feɪd/
v. 凋谢；褪色
[义] To lose colour, freshness, or strength gradually; (of flowers) to wither. 逐渐失去颜色、新鲜或活力；（花）凋谢（fade away 消逝, fading petals 凋瓣, colours fade 褪色）。动词。
[例] ① Cut flowers fade within a week without fresh water. 切花不换水一周即凋。 ② Autumn leaves faded from gold to brown on the lawn. 秋叶在草坪上由金变褐。
[搭] fade away 消逝, fading petals 凋瓣, colours fade 褪色, fade in the sun 日晒褪色
[联] wither 枯萎, wilt 蔫, bloom 开花, petal 花瓣, colour 颜色, autumn 秋天, die 死亡
[记] 不及物/及物。fade 通用；花卉语境指「凋残」。fade out 影视术语「淡出」

**rot**
英/美 /rɒt/
v. 腐烂
n. 腐烂
[义] To decay and break down, especially because of moisture or fungus. 腐败分解，尤因潮湿或真菌（wood rot 木腐, rot on the vine 藤上烂果, root rot 根腐）。动词/不可数名词。
[例] ① Fallen apples rotted under the tree within days. 落苹果几天内在树下腐烂。 ② Overwatering caused the roots to rot in their pots. 浇水过多致根在盆中腐烂。
[搭] wood rot 木腐, rot on the vine 藤上烂果, root rot 根腐, rot away 烂掉
[联] decay 腐烂, mould 霉菌, fungus 真菌, compost 堆肥, wet 湿的, fruit 果实, disease 病害
[记] 动词/不可数。rot 比 decay 更口语、常含贬义；root rot 为园艺常见病害

**pot**
英/美 /pɒt/
n. 花盆
v. 盆栽
[义] A container for growing plants; to put a plant into a pot. 栽培植物的容器；把植物栽入盆（flower pot 花盆, potted plant 盆栽, potting soil 盆栽土）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She repotted the orchid into a larger clay pot. 她把兰花换进更大的陶盆。 ② Herbs grow well in pots on sunny windowsills. 香草在向阳窗台花盆里长得好。
[搭] flower pot 花盆, potted plant 盆栽, potting soil 盆栽土, clay pot 陶盆, repot 换盆
[联] plant 种植, garden 花园, soil 土, windowsill 窗台, succulent 多肉, nursery 苗圃, container 容器
[记] 可数/动词。the pot calling the kettle black 习语与花盆无关；pot 多义极多，依语境区分

**shrubbery**
英/美 /ˈʃrʌbəri/
n. 灌木丛；灌木区
[义] An area planted with shrubs, or shrubs considered as a group. 种植灌木的区域；灌木总称（ornamental shrubbery 观赏灌木区, dense shrubbery 密灌木）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The path wound through ornamental shrubbery to the lake. 小径穿过观赏灌木区通到湖边。 ② Birds nested in the shrubbery beside the garage. 鸟在车库旁灌木丛中筑巢。
[搭] ornamental shrubbery 观赏灌木区, dense shrubbery 密灌木, shrubbery border 灌木边带
[联] shrub 灌木, bush 灌木, hedge 树篱, garden 花园, plant 种植, bird 鸟, border 边带
[记] 不可数/可数。shrub + -ery；英式 garden 描述常见，略正式

**acorn**
英/美 /ˈeɪkɔːn/
n. 橡实；栎子
[义] The nut of an oak tree, shaped like a small cup with a cap. 橡树的坚果，小杯形、带帽（fallen acorns 落橡实, acorn cap 橡实帽, oak acorn 橡实）。可数。
[例] ① Squirrels buried acorns under the oak trees. 松鼠把橡实埋在橡树下。 ② Acorns littered the forest floor in autumn. 秋天橡实铺满林地。
[搭] fallen acorns 落橡实, acorn cap 橡实帽, oak acorn 橡实, collect acorns 捡橡实
[联] oak 橡树, nut 坚果, squirrel 松鼠, forest 森林, seed 种子, tree 树, autumn 秋天
[记] 可数：an acorn。great oaks from little acorns grow 谚语喻「合抱之木生于毫末」

**cone**
英/美 /kəʊn/
n. 球果；锥形物
[义] The hard oval seed container of a pine or fir; a solid shape with a circular base tapering to a point. 松或冷杉的硬质卵形种壳；亦指圆底尖顶的立体形（pine cone 松果, fir cone 冷杉球果, ice cream cone 蛋筒〔另义〕）。可数。
[例] ① Children collected pine cones on the forest walk. 孩子在林中漫步时捡松果。 ② Fir cones hung upright on the branches above us. 冷杉球果直立挂在我们上方的枝头。
[搭] pine cone 松果, fir cone 冷杉球果, pine cone scale 松果鳞片, collect cones 捡球果
[联] pine 松, fir 冷杉, conifer 针叶树, seed 种子, forest 森林, evergreen 常绿, tree 树
[记] 可数。ice cream cone 为「蛋筒」；geometry cone 为「圆锥」——多义

**needle**
英/美 /ˈniːdl/
n. 针叶；针
[义] The long thin pointed leaf of a pine, fir, or other conifer. 松、冷杉等针叶树的细长尖叶（pine needles 松针, fallen needles 落针, needle carpet 针叶层）。可数/通常复数 needles。
[例] ① Pine needles carpeted the path through the woods. 松针铺满林间小径。 ② The air smelled of resin from crushed needles. 被踩碎的松针散发树脂气息。
[搭] pine needles 松针, fallen needles 落针, needle carpet 针叶层, resin-scented needles 含脂松针
[联] pine 松, fir 冷杉, conifer 针叶树, leaf 叶, forest 森林, evergreen 常绿, resin 树脂
[记] 可数/复数 needles。sewing needle（缝针）与 pine needle 同形；语境区分

**camellia**
英/美 /kəˈmiːliə/
n. 山茶；茶花
[义] An evergreen shrub with glossy leaves and rose-like flowers, often pink or red. 叶亮革质、花似玫瑰、常粉红或红的常绿灌木（camellia bush 山茶丛, camellia flower 茶花, winter camellia 冬山茶）。可数。
[例] ① Camellias bloomed in the courtyard through the cool months. 山茶在庭院里熬过凉季持续开花。 ② She clipped a camellia blossom for her lapel. 她剪一朵茶花别在衣领上。
[搭] camellia bush 山茶丛, camellia flower 茶花, winter camellia 冬山茶, camellia oil 山茶油
[联] shrub 灌木, flower 花, evergreen 常绿, glossy 有光泽的, garden 花园, petal 花瓣, pink 粉的
[记] 可数：a camellia。茶树（tea plant）学名亦属 Camellia 属；观赏山茶与茶叶用种不同品种

**azalea**
英/美 /əˈzeɪliə/
n. 杜鹃花；映山红
[义] A bush with brightly coloured funnel-shaped flowers, blooming in spring. 春季开放、花色鲜艳的漏斗形花灌木（azalea bush 杜鹃丛, azalea garden 杜鹃园, pink azaleas 粉杜鹃）。可数。
[例] ① Azaleas lit up the woodland garden each April. 每年四月杜鹃点亮林地花园。 ② The trail passed banks of wild azaleas in bloom. 小径经过成片盛开的野杜鹃。
[搭] azalea bush 杜鹃丛, azalea garden 杜鹃园, pink azaleas 粉杜鹃, wild azaleas 野杜鹃
[联] shrub 灌木, flower 花, spring 春天, bloom 开花, garden 花园, pink 粉的, woodland 林地
[记] 可数：an azalea。杜鹃属与 rhododendron 近缘；园艺中常并提

**lotus**
英/美 /ˈləʊtəs/
n. 莲；荷花
[义] A water plant with large round leaves and pink or white flowers that float on ponds. 水生植物，圆大叶，粉或白花浮于池塘（lotus flower 荷花, lotus leaf 荷叶, lotus pond 荷塘）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Lotus flowers opened at dawn on the quiet pond. 荷花在静池上清晨绽放。 ② Lotus leaves shed water in silver beads after the rain. 雨后荷叶上的水珠成银珠滚落。
[搭] lotus flower 荷花, lotus leaf 荷叶, lotus pond 荷塘, lotus root 莲藕（食物章另见）
[联] lily 百合, pond 池塘, water 水, leaf 叶, flower 花, sacred 神圣的, pink 粉的
[记] 可数/不可数。东亚文化中莲花象征清净；lotus position 指瑜伽坐姿，不同义

**holly**
英/美 /ˈhɒli/
n. 冬青
[义] An evergreen bush or tree with spiny dark green leaves and red berries in winter. 叶深绿带刺、冬结红果的常绿灌木或乔木（holly bush 冬青丛, holly berries 冬青果, Christmas holly 圣诞冬青）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Holly berries brightened the grey winter hedge. 冬青果让灰色冬日树篱添彩。 ② She wove holly sprigs into the door wreath. 她把冬青小枝编进门环花圈。
[搭] holly bush 冬青丛, holly berries 冬青果, Christmas holly 圣诞冬青, holly leaf 冬青叶
[联] evergreen 常绿, berry 浆果, Christmas 圣诞, shrub 灌木, thorn 刺, red 红的, wreath 花环
[记] 不可数/可数。西方圣诞装饰经典植物；浆果对宠物与幼儿有毒，勿食

**begonia**
英/美 /bɪˈɡəʊniə/
n. 秋海棠
[义] A plant with colourful asymmetrical leaves or flowers, popular as a houseplant or in shade gardens. 叶或花色彩鲜艳、不对称，宜室内或荫生花园（begonia plant 秋海棠, tuberous begonia 球根秋海棠, rex begonia 蟆叶秋海棠）。可数。
[例] ① Begonias thrived in the shaded porch planters. 秋海棠在荫蔽门廊花盆里长得旺。 ② She chose wax begonias for the window boxes. 她为窗台花箱选了蜡叶秋海棠。
[搭] begonia plant 秋海棠, tuberous begonia 球根秋海棠, rex begonia 蟆叶秋海棠, shade begonias 耐阴秋海棠
[联] flower 花, leaf 叶, shade 荫, garden 花园, houseplant 室内植物, colourful 鲜艳的, pot 盆
[记] 可数：a begonia。品种极多；耐阴，阳台与室内常见

**dandelion**
英/美 /ˈdændɪlaɪən/
n. 蒲公英
[义] A common wild plant with yellow flowers and fluffy seed heads that children blow. 常见野草，黄花，籽冠蓬松如绒球（dandelion clock 蒲公英绒球, dandelion leaf 蒲公英叶, blow dandelions 吹蒲公英）。可数。
[例] ① Dandelions dotted the unmown grass along the lane. 小路旁未割草上点缀蒲公英。 ② Children blew dandelion clocks and watched seeds drift away. 孩子吹蒲公英绒球，看种子飘走。
[搭] dandelion clock 蒲公英绒球, dandelion leaf 蒲公英叶, blow dandelions 吹蒲公英, wild dandelions 野蒲公英
[联] weed 杂草, wildflower 野花, seed 种子, yellow 黄的, lawn 草坪, grass 草, blow 吹
[记] 可数：a dandelion。dent-de-lion（法语「狮牙」）→ 齿状叶；嫩叶可食，园艺亦当杂草除

**clover**
英/美 /ˈkləʊvə(r)/
n. 三叶草；苜蓿
[义] A small plant with leaves of three round parts, growing in fields and lawns. 叶分三圆瓣、生于田野与草坪的小型植物（four-leaf clover 四叶草, clover field 三叶草田, white clover 白三叶草）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Bees hummed over the clover in the meadow. 蜜蜂在草地三叶草上嗡嗡飞舞。 ② She searched the lawn for a four-leaf clover. 她在草坪上找四叶草。
[搭] four-leaf clover 四叶草, clover field 三叶草田, white clover 白三叶草, red clover 红三叶草
[联] grass 草, meadow 草地, lawn 草坪, bee 蜜蜂, luck 运气, leaf 叶, field 田
[记] 不可数/可数。four-leaf clover 象征好运；牲畜饲料与绿肥作物

**mint**
英/美 /mɪnt/
n. 薄荷
[义] A herb with a fresh strong smell, whose leaves are used for tea and flavouring. 气味清凉强烈的香草，叶用于泡茶与调味（fresh mint 新鲜薄荷, mint leaves 薄荷叶, mint tea 薄荷茶）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Mint spread quickly along the damp garden path. 薄荷沿潮湿花园小径迅速蔓延。 ② She crushed mint leaves into the iced tea. 她把薄荷叶捣进冰茶里。
[搭] fresh mint 新鲜薄荷, mint leaves 薄荷叶, mint tea 薄荷茶, spearmint 留兰香, peppermint 胡椒薄荷
[联] herb 香草, garden 花园, leaf 叶, tea 茶, fragrance 芬芳, fresh 新鲜的, smell 气味
[记] 不可数/可数。mint 蔓延性强，宜盆栽约束；食用调味在食物章可交叉

**stake**
英/美 /steɪk/
n. 桩；支柱
v. 用桩支撑
[义] A strong stick pushed into the ground to support a plant; to support with a stake. 插入土中支撑植株的坚固木棍；打桩支撑（tomato stakes 番茄支架, stake the vines 给藤搭桩）。可数/动词。另义「赌注、利害关系」依语境区分。
[例] ① She tied the tomato vines to wooden stakes. 她把番茄藤绑在木桩上。 ② Young trees were staked until their roots held firm. 幼树打桩支撑直至根系稳固。
[搭] tomato stakes 番茄支架, stake the vines 给藤搭桩, wooden stake 木桩, stake a plant 给植株打桩
[联] tomato 番茄, vine 藤, support 支撑, garden 花园, grow 生长, tie 绑, tree 树
[记] 可数/动词。stake out 另有「监视、划界」义；园艺义及物

**hoe**
英/美 /həʊ/
n. 锄头
v. 锄；松土
[义] A tool with a thin flat blade for breaking up soil and removing weeds; to use this tool. 薄平刃、用于松土除杂草的工具；锄地（garden hoe 园艺锄, hoe the rows 锄垄, hoe weeds 锄草）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He hoed between the bean rows on Saturday morning. 周六早晨他在豆垄间锄地。 ② A sharp hoe makes weeding faster. 锋利的锄头除草更快。
[搭] garden hoe 园艺锄, hoe the rows 锄垄, hoe weeds 锄草, long-handled hoe 长柄锄
[联] weed 杂草, garden 花园, soil 土壤, cultivate 耕作, rake 耙, dig 挖, tool 工具
[记] 可数/动词。hoe 与 row crops 搭配常见；具名工具细节可归物质章，此处取园艺动词义

**rake**
英/美 /reɪk/
n. 耙子
v. 耙；耙平
[义] A garden tool with a long handle and teeth for gathering leaves or smoothing soil; to use it. 长柄带齿、用于集叶或耙平土壤的园艺工具；耙（rake leaves 耙落叶, rake the soil 耙土, lawn rake 草坪耙）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She raked fallen leaves into piles for compost. 她把落叶耙成堆以制堆肥。 ② He raked the seedbed smooth before sowing. 播种前他把苗床耙平。
[搭] rake leaves 耙落叶, rake the soil 耙土, lawn rake 草坪耙, rake into piles 耙成堆
[联] leaf 叶, lawn 草坪, garden 花园, compost 堆肥, soil 土壤, autumn 秋天, gather 收集
[记] 可数/动词。rake in money 等为比喻「大赚」，与园艺义无关

**deadhead**
英/美 /ˈdedhed/
v. 摘除残花
n. 残花；迷魂药（俚，另义）
[义] To remove faded flowers from a plant to encourage more blooms. 摘除凋谢的花以促进继续开花（deadhead roses 给玫瑰摘残花, deadheading 摘残花）。动词/可数名词。
[例] ① Deadheading keeps the roses flowering all summer. 摘残花让玫瑰整个夏天持续开。 ② She spent an hour deadheading the petunias. 她花一小时给矮牵牛摘残花。
[搭] deadhead roses 给玫瑰摘残花, deadheading flowers 摘残花, regular deadheading 定期摘残花
[联] rose 玫瑰, bloom 开花, flower 花, garden 花园, prune 修剪, petal 花瓣, summer 夏天
[记] 动词/名词。园艺义合成词 dead + head；俚语 deadhead 指迷幻摇滚粉丝或毒品，语境完全不同

**tobacco**
英/美 /təˈbækəʊ/
n. 烟草；烟叶
[义] A plant whose leaves are dried and used for smoking or chewing; the dried leaves. 叶经晒干用于吸食或咀嚼的植物；干叶（tobacco plant 烟草植株, tobacco field 烟田, grow tobacco 种烟）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Tobacco rows stretched across the rolling farmland. 烟行绵延起伏的农田。 ② The region once depended on tobacco as a cash crop. 该地区曾依赖烟草作经济作物。
[搭] tobacco plant 烟草植株, tobacco field 烟田, grow tobacco 种烟, tobacco leaf 烟叶, cash crop 经济作物
[联] crop 作物, leaf 叶, farm 农场, field 田, harvest 收获, plant 植株, plantation 种植园
[记] 不可数/可数。本章取作物/植株义；制品与政策词汇不归本章

**coffee**
英/美 /ˈkɒfi/
n. 咖啡；咖啡树
[义] A tropical shrub whose seeds are roasted to make the drink coffee; the drink itself. 热带灌木，籽烘焙后作咖啡饮；饮品本身（coffee plant 咖啡树, coffee plantation 咖啡种植园, coffee beans 咖啡豆）。不可数/可数。本章取植物/种植义。
[例] ① Coffee plants flowered white along the mountain terrace. 咖啡树在山地梯田上开白花。 ② Shade-grown coffee protects forest canopy on some farms. 荫蔽种植咖啡在一些农场保护林冠。
[搭] coffee plant 咖啡树, coffee plantation 咖啡种植园, coffee beans 咖啡豆, grow coffee 种咖啡
[联] plantation 种植园, crop 作物, bean 豆, tropical 热带的, harvest 收获, shrub 灌木, shade 荫
[记] 不可数/可数。饮品与咖啡馆用法在食物章；arabica / robusta 为品种名，日常识读即可

**tea**
英/美 /tiː/
n. 茶；茶树
[义] The dried leaves of the tea plant, used to make a hot drink; the shrub itself. 茶树干燥叶片，用于冲泡；茶树本身（tea plant 茶树, tea plantation 茶园, tea leaves 茶叶）。不可数/可数。本章取植株/种植义。
