[例] ① Tea bushes lined the misty hillside terraces. 茶树排列在雾气缭绕的山坡梯田。 ② Workers plucked young tea leaves by hand each spring. 每年春天工人手工采摘嫩芽。
[搭] tea plant 茶树, tea plantation 茶园, tea leaves 茶叶, grow tea 种茶, tea bush 茶树丛
[联] plantation 种植园, leaf 叶, camellia 山茶属, harvest 采摘, hillside 山坡, crop 作物, green 绿茶
[记] 不可数/可数。茶树学名 Camellia sinensis；饮品义在食物章

**oats**
英/美 /əʊts/
n. 燕麦；燕麦田
[义] A cereal plant grown for its grain, used in food and animal feed. 为谷粒而种的谷物，用于食品与饲料（oat field 燕麦田, oat crop 燕麦收成, rolled oats 燕麦片〔食物章〕）。复数形式；单数 oat 较少单独用。
[例] ① Oats swayed gold in the wind before the August harvest. 八月收获前燕麦在风中金黄摇曳。 ② Farmers planted oats as a cover crop over winter. 农民冬种燕麦作覆盖作物。
[搭] oat field 燕麦田, oat crop 燕麦收成, grow oats 种燕麦, wild oats 野燕麦
[联] grain 谷物, barley 大麦, wheat 小麦, crop 作物, field 田, harvest 收获, cereal 谷类
[记] 通常作复数 oats。sow one's wild oats 习语喻「年轻时胡闹」，与作物义无关

**rye**
英/美 /raɪ/
n. 黑麦
[义] A cereal plant related to wheat, used for bread and whiskey. 与小麦近缘的谷物，用于面包与威士忌（rye field 黑麦田, rye bread 黑麦面包〔食物章〕, rye crop 黑麦收成）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Rye grew well in the cooler northern fields. 黑麦在较凉的北方田地长得好。 ② The farmer rotated rye with clover to rest the soil. 农民以黑麦与三叶草轮作养地。
[搭] rye field 黑麦田, rye crop 黑麦收成, grow rye 种黑麦, rye grain 黑麦粒
[联] wheat 小麦, barley 大麦, grain 谷物, crop 作物, bread 面包, field 田, harvest 收获
[记] 不可数/可数。黑麦面包在北欧与东欧常见；威士忌原料义在食物章可交叉

**flax**
英/美 /flæks/
n. 亚麻；亚麻纤维
[义] A plant with blue flowers, grown for its fibre and seeds. 开蓝花、为纤维与种籽而种的植物（flax field 亚麻田, flax fibre 亚麻纤维, flax seed 亚麻籽）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Flax bloomed pale blue across the summer field. 夏日田野上亚麻开淡蓝小花。 ② Linen is made from fibres of the flax plant. 亚麻布由亚麻植株纤维织成。
[搭] flax field 亚麻田, flax fibre 亚麻纤维, flax seed 亚麻籽, grow flax 种亚麻
[联] fibre 纤维, field 田, flower 花, blue 蓝的, crop 作物, linen 亚麻布, seed 种子
[记] 不可数/可数。linen ← flax；亚麻籽为健康食品，食物章可交叉

**cabbage**
英/美 /ˈkæbɪdʒ/
n. 卷心菜；甘蓝
[义] A round vegetable with thick green or purple leaves, growing close to the ground. 近地生长、厚叶成球、绿或紫色的蔬菜植株（cabbage patch 甘蓝地, cabbage head 甘蓝球, red cabbage 紫甘蓝）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Cabbages swelled in the cool autumn garden beds. 甘蓝在秋日凉床里膨大结球。 ② Cabbage white butterflies laid eggs on the leaves. 菜粉蝶在叶上产卵。
[搭] cabbage patch 甘蓝地, cabbage head 甘蓝球, red cabbage 紫甘蓝, grow cabbage 种甘蓝
[联] leaf 叶, vegetable 蔬菜, garden 花园, crop 作物, green 绿的, harvest 收获, butterfly 蝴蝶
[记] 可数/不可数。本章取植株义；烹饪用法在食物章

**carrot**
英/美 /ˈkærət/
n. 胡萝卜
[义] A long orange root vegetable that grows underground. 地下生长的长形橙色根菜（carrot top 胡萝卜缨, carrot patch 胡萝卜地, baby carrots 小胡萝卜〔食物章〕）。可数。
[例] ① Carrot tops waved above the rows in the kitchen garden. 厨房菜园里胡萝卜缨在垄上摇曳。 ② She pulled fresh carrots after a light rain softened the soil. 小雨润土后她拔出新胡萝卜。
[搭] carrot top 胡萝卜缨, carrot patch 胡萝卜地, grow carrots 种胡萝卜, carrot root 胡萝卜根
[联] root 根, vegetable 蔬菜, orange 橙的, garden 花园, soil 土, harvest 收获, crop 作物
[记] 可数：a carrot。carrot and stick 喻「软硬兼施」，与蔬菜义无关

**pumpkin**
英/美 /ˈpʌmpkɪn/
n. 南瓜
[义] A large round orange fruit that grows on a vine, harvested in autumn. 秋初采收、藤生的大型圆橙果（pumpkin patch 南瓜地, pumpkin vine 南瓜藤, giant pumpkin 巨型南瓜）。可数。
[例] ① Pumpkin vines sprawled across the back corner of the lot. 南瓜藤爬满地块后方角落。 ② Children chose pumpkins from the patch for carving. 孩子从瓜地挑南瓜雕刻。
[搭] pumpkin patch 南瓜地, pumpkin vine 南瓜藤, giant pumpkin 巨型南瓜, grow pumpkins 种南瓜
[联] vine 藤, autumn 秋天, harvest 收获, orange 橙的, garden 花园, fruit 果实, crop 作物
[记] 可数：a pumpkin。万圣节雕刻文化；食材义在食物章

**banana**
英/美 /bəˈnɑːnə/
n. 香蕉；香蕉树
[义] A long curved yellow fruit; the tropical plant that bears it. 长弯形黄色水果；结果的热带植物（banana plant 香蕉植株, banana tree 香蕉树, banana plantation 香蕉种植园）。可数。
[例] ① Banana leaves unfurled after the tropical rain. 热带雨后蕉叶舒展。 ② The plantation shipped bananas north each week. 种植园每周北运香蕉。
[搭] banana plant 香蕉植株, banana tree 香蕉树, banana plantation 香蕉种植园, banana leaf 蕉叶
[联] tropical 热带的, fruit 果实, plantation 种植园, palm-like 似棕榈, leaf 叶, harvest 收获, bunch 串
[记] 可数：a banana。香蕉树实为大型草本，非木本乔木；食材义在食物章

**olive**
英/美 /ˈɒlɪv/
n. 橄榄；橄榄树
[义] A small bitter fruit used for oil; the evergreen Mediterranean tree that bears it. 味涩小果，可榨油；产果的地中海常绿树（olive tree 橄榄树, olive grove 橄榄园, olive branch 橄榄枝〔喻和平〕）。可数。
[例] ① Olive trees silvered the hills above the village. 橄榄树让村庄上方山丘泛银灰。 ② Farmers harvested olives by hand each autumn. 农民每年秋天手工采橄榄。
[搭] olive tree 橄榄树, olive grove 橄榄园, olive branch 橄榄枝, olive oil 橄榄油（食物章）
[联] grove 小树林, tree 树, evergreen 常绿, Mediterranean 地中海, fruit 果实, oil 油, branch 枝
[记] 可数：an olive。extend an olive branch 喻「示好求和」；食用油在食物章

**eucalyptus**
英/美 /ˌjuːkəˈlɪptəs/
n. 桉树；尤加利
[义] A fast-growing evergreen tree native to Australia, with aromatic leaves. 澳大利亚原产、生长快、叶芳香的常绿树（eucalyptus tree 桉树, eucalyptus forest 桉树林, eucalyptus oil 桉叶油）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Eucalyptus scent hung in the air after the rain. 雨后空气中弥漫着桉树香。 ② Koalas feed almost exclusively on eucalyptus leaves. 考拉几乎专食桉树叶。
[搭] eucalyptus tree 桉树, eucalyptus forest 桉树林, eucalyptus oil 桉叶油, eucalyptus grove 桉树林
[联] tree 树, evergreen 常绿, Australia 澳大利亚, leaf 叶, forest 森林, aromatic 芳香的, koala 考拉
[记] 可数/不可数。复数 eucalyptuses 或 eucalypti；许多地区作速生造林树种

**coconut**
英/美 /ˈkəʊkənʌt/
n. 椰子；椰子树
[义] The large hard-shelled fruit of the coconut palm; the palm itself. 椰棕的大硬壳果；椰棕本身（coconut palm 椰棕, coconut tree 椰子树, coconut husk 椰壳）。可数。
[例] ① Coconut palms leaned over the white sand beach. 椰棕俯临白色沙滩。 ② Fallen coconuts littered the ground beneath the trees. 树下满地落椰。
[搭] coconut palm 椰棕, coconut tree 椰子树, coconut husk 椰壳, coconut grove 椰林
[联] palm 棕榈, tropical 热带的, beach 海滩, fruit 果实, island 岛屿, oil 油, husk 外壳
[记] 可数：a coconut。椰棕为热带海岸标志；食材与用品在食物章

**apple**
英/美 /ˈæpl/
n. 苹果；苹果树
[义] A round fruit with red or green skin; the tree that bears it. 圆果、皮红或绿；结果之树（apple tree 苹果树, apple orchard 苹果园, apple blossom 苹果花）。可数。本章取植株/果园义。
[例] ① Apple blossoms scented the orchard in late spring. 晚春苹果园花香四溢。 ② She planted two apple trees behind the garage. 她在车库后种了两棵苹果树。
[搭] apple tree 苹果树, apple orchard 苹果园, apple blossom 苹果花, crab apple 海棠
[联] orchard 果园, tree 树, fruit 果实, blossom 花, harvest 收获, cherry 樱桃, crop 作物
[记] 可数：an apple。an apple a day 谚语喻「常苹果保健康」；食材义在食物章

**lemon**
英/美 /ˈlemən/
n. 柠檬；柠檬树
[义] A yellow oval citrus fruit; the small evergreen tree that bears it. 黄色椭圆柑橘；结果的小型常绿树（lemon tree 柠檬树, lemon grove 柠檬园, lemon blossom 柠檬花）。可数。
[例] ① Lemon trees perfumed the courtyard in bloom. 柠檬树开花时庭院香气弥漫。 ② She picked lemons from the pot on the terrace. 她从露台盆栽上摘柠檬。
[搭] lemon tree 柠檬树, lemon grove 柠檬园, lemon blossom 柠檬花, potted lemon 盆栽柠檬
[联] citrus 柑橘, tree 树, yellow 黄的, grove 小树林, fruit 果实, evergreen 常绿, orchard 果园
[记] 可数：a lemon。lemon 俚语可指「次品、倒霉事」，与果树义不同

**spore**
英/美 /spɔː(r)/
n. 孢子
[义] A tiny cell produced by ferns, fungi, and some other plants, from which a new plant can grow. 蕨类、真菌等产生的微小细胞，可长出新植株（fern spores 蕨孢子, mushroom spores 蘑菇孢子, spore dispersal 孢子传播）。可数。
[例] ① Fern spores are often visible as brown dots under the leaf. 蕨孢子常在叶背呈褐点可见。 ② Wind carries spores across long distances in the forest. 风将孢子带过林中远距离。
[搭] fern spores 蕨孢子, mushroom spores 蘑菇孢子, spore dispersal 孢子传播, release spores 释放孢子
[联] fern 蕨, mushroom 蘑菇, fungus 真菌, seed 种子, reproduce 繁殖, moss 苔藓, dispersal 传播
[记] 可数：a spore。孢子非种子；蕨与真菌繁殖关键词

**resin**
英/美 /ˈrezɪn/
n. 树脂
[义] A sticky substance produced by some trees, especially pines and firs. 某些树木分泌的黏性物质，尤指松杉（pine resin 松脂, resinous smell 树脂气味, tree resin 树树脂）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Resin oozed from cuts in the pine bark. 松树皮切口渗出树脂。 ② The cabin smelled of warm wood and resin after the fire. 生火后木屋弥漫着暖木与树脂气息。
[搭] pine resin 松脂, resinous smell 树脂气味, tree resin 树树脂, sticky resin 黏树脂
[联] pine 松, fir 冷杉, tree 树, bark 树皮, sticky 黏的, smell 气味, needle 针叶
[记] 不可数/可数。resin 用于清漆、香料与药用；amber 为远古树脂化石

**sap**
英/美 /sæp/
n. 树液；汁液
[义] The liquid that carries food through a plant, especially in trees. 在植物体内输送养分的液体，尤指树木（tree sap 树液, maple sap 枫汁, sap rising 树液上升）。不可数。
[例] ① Maple sap flows in spring when nights are cold and days warm. 春夜冷昼暖时枫汁流动。 ② He wiped sticky sap from his hands after pruning pines. 修剪松树后他擦掉手上黏汁。
[搭] tree sap 树液, maple sap 枫汁, sap rising 树液上升, sticky sap 黏汁
[联] maple 枫, tree 树, resin 树脂, spring 春天, liquid 液体, flow 流动, syrup 糖浆
[记] 不可数。sap 俚语可指「精力、元气」或「削弱」（sap one's strength）；maple syrup 来自 sap

**canopy**
英/美 /ˈkænəpi/
n. 树冠层
[义] The upper layer of branches and leaves in a forest; a covering that forms a roof. 森林上层枝叶；形成顶盖的覆盖（forest canopy 林冠, tree canopy 树冠, dense canopy 密冠层）。可数。
[例] ① Sunlight barely reached the forest floor beneath the thick canopy. 密林冠下阳光几乎照不到林地。 ② Monkeys moved through the rainforest canopy at dawn. 猴子黎明时分在雨林冠层活动。
[搭] forest canopy 林冠, tree canopy 树冠, dense canopy 密冠层, canopy layer 冠层
[联] forest 森林, tree 树, rainforest 雨林, foliage 叶丛, shade 荫, layer 层, jungle 丛林
[记] 可数：a canopy。生态与雨林报道高频；亦指床幔、遮阳篷——多义

**understory**
英/美 /ˈʌndəstɔːri/
n. 林下植被；下层林
[义] The layer of smaller plants and young trees that grow beneath the forest canopy. 生长于林冠之下的小植物与幼树层（rainforest understory 雨林下层， dense understory 密林下， understory plants 林下植物）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Ferns and shrubs fill the understory of the old-growth forest. 蕨类与灌木布满老林下层。 ② Light rarely reaches the understory in tropical rainforest. 热带雨林林下极少有光。
[搭] rainforest understory 雨林下层, dense understory 密林下, understory plants 林下植物, forest understory 林下
[联] canopy 林冠, undergrowth 林下灌木, forest 森林, shrub 灌木, fern 蕨, rainforest 雨林, shade 荫
[记] 可数/不可数。亦作 understorey（英式）；比 undergrowth 更强调「林冠下整层结构」

**undergrowth**
英/美 /ˈʌndəɡrəʊθ/
n. 林下灌木；下层植被
[义] A mass of bushes and small plants growing under trees in a wood. 林下灌木与小型植物丛生层（dense undergrowth 密灌下层, push through undergrowth 穿过下层灌丛）。不可数。
[例] ① The path was choked with thorny undergrowth. 小径被多刺下层灌丛堵塞。 ② Deer hid in the undergrowth at the forest edge. 鹿躲在林缘下层植被中。
[搭] dense undergrowth 密灌下层, push through undergrowth 穿过下层灌丛, forest undergrowth 林下植被
[联] forest 森林, bush 灌木, thicket 灌丛, woodland 林地, shade 荫, wildlife 野生动物, path 小径
[记] 不可数。under + growth；与 canopy 相对，构成垂直结构联想

**vein**
英/美 /veɪn/
n. 叶脉；纹理
[义] One of the fine lines on a leaf or petal; a pattern like this. 叶或花瓣上的细纹；类似纹理（leaf veins 叶脉, prominent veins 明显叶脉, veined leaves 有脉纹的叶）。可数。人体「静脉」义在生理章。
[例] ① She held the leaf to the light to see its veins. 她把叶子对光察看叶脉。 ② The veins on the petal formed a delicate net. 花瓣上的脉纹成精致网络。
[搭] leaf veins 叶脉, prominent veins 明显叶脉, veined leaves 有脉纹的叶, vein pattern 脉纹图案
[联] leaf 叶, petal 花瓣, pattern 纹理, stem 茎, plant 植物, green 绿的, light 光
[记] 可数：a vein。botany 与 anatomy 同形；in the same vein 为「同样思路」，比喻义

**bramble**
英/美 /ˈbræmbl/
n. 黑莓丛；荆棘灌木
[义] A wild bush with thorns that produces blackberries. 带刺、结黑莓的野灌木（bramble patch 黑莓丛, bramble thorns 荆棘刺, pick brambles 采黑莓）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Brambles clawed at their sleeves along the overgrown path. 杂草丛生小径上黑莓丛钩住他们袖子。 ② She returned with purple-stained fingers from the brambles. 她从荆棘丛回来，手指染紫。
[搭] bramble patch 黑莓丛, bramble thorns 荆棘刺, pick brambles 采黑莓, wild brambles 野黑莓丛
[联] thorn 刺, berry 浆果, bush 灌木, thicket 灌丛, wild 野生的, blackberry 黑莓, prick 刺
[记] 可数/不可数。bramble 在英式乡野文学中极常见；果实食物义在食物章

**seaweed**
英/美 /ˈsiːwiːd/
n. 海藻；海草
[义] A plant that grows in the sea, often attached to rocks or floating. 在海中生长的植物，常附于岩石或漂浮（seaweed on rocks 岩石海藻, dried seaweed 干海藻, seaweed forest 海藻林）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Seaweed draped the tide pools at low water. 低潮时海藻垂覆潮池。 ② Coastal farms cultivate seaweed for food and fertilizer. 沿海农场养殖海藻作食物与肥料。
[搭] seaweed on rocks 岩石海藻, dried seaweed 干海藻, seaweed forest 海藻林, collect seaweed 采集海藻
[联] algae 藻类, ocean 海洋, kelp 巨藻, rock 岩石, tide 潮汐, marine 海洋的, float 漂浮
[记] 不可数/可数。sea + weed；食用与养殖在沿海文化常见，食材义在食物章

**kelp**
英/美 /kelp/
n. 巨藻；海带
[义] Large brown seaweed that grows in underwater forests in cool seas. 寒带海中形成「海底森林」的大型褐藻（kelp forest 巨藻林, giant kelp 巨型海带, kelp bed 巨藻床）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Kelp forests shelter fish along the Pacific coast. 太平洋沿岸巨藻林为鱼提供庇护。 ② Divers swam through towering kelp off the island. 潜水员在岛外高耸巨藻间穿行。
[搭] kelp forest 巨藻林, giant kelp 巨型海带, kelp bed 巨藻床, underwater kelp 水下巨藻
[联] seaweed 海藻, ocean 海洋, forest 林, marine 海洋的, algae 藻类, coast 海岸, fish 鱼
[记] 不可数/可数。kelp forest 为重要海洋生态系统；碘与食材义在食物章

**seagrass**
英/美 /ˈsiːɡrɑːs/
n. 海草
[义] A flowering plant that grows in shallow sea water, forming underwater meadows. 在浅海水中生长的开花植物，形成水下草甸（seagrass meadow 海草草甸， seagrass bed 海草床， protect seagrass 保护海草）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Seagrass meadows shelter juvenile fish along the coast. 海草草甸庇护沿海幼鱼。 ② Boat anchors can tear up fragile seagrass beds. 船锚可扯碎脆弱的海草床。
[搭] seagrass meadow 海草草甸, seagrass bed 海草床, protect seagrass 保护海草, seagrass habitat 海草生境
[联] seaweed 海藻, kelp 巨藻, marine 海洋的, meadow 草甸, fish 鱼, coast 海岸, habitat 栖息地
[记] 不可数/可数。seagrass 为开花植物；seaweed 多指广义海藻，二者不同

**reed**
英/美 /riːd/
n. 芦苇；芦秆
[义] A tall plant with a hollow stem that grows in wetlands; the stem used for mats or music reeds. 湿地生长、茎中空的高大植物；茎可编席或制哨片（reed bed 芦苇荡, bamboo reed 芦竹〔近缘〕, reeds by the river 河边芦苇）。可数/通常复数 reeds。
[例] ① Reeds rustled along the marsh edge at sunset. 日落时芦苇沿沼泽缘沙沙作响。 ② Birds nested deep in the reed beds away from paths. 鸟在远离小径的芦苇荡深处筑巢。
[搭] reed bed 芦苇荡, reeds by the river 河边芦苇, tall reeds 高芦苇, reed marsh 芦苇沼泽
[联] marsh 沼泽, wetland 湿地, grass 草, river 河, bird 鸟, hollow 中空的, rustle 沙沙声
[记] 可数/复数 reeds。reed 亦指乐器「哨片」；oboe reed 与植物义同形

**rhododendron**
英/美 /ˌrəʊdəˈdendrən/
n. 杜鹃花属；映山红（大树杜鹃类）
[义] A bush or small tree with large clusters of bell-shaped flowers, often pink or purple. 开大簇钟形花、常粉或紫的灌木或小乔木（rhododendron bush 杜鹃丛, alpine rhododendron 高山杜鹃, rhododendron garden 杜鹃园）。可数。
[例] ① Rhododendrons covered the hillside in late spring colour. 晚春杜鹃把山坡染成一片艳色。 ② The lodge was framed by towering rhododendron hedges. 旅舍两侧是高大的杜鹃树篱。
[搭] rhododendron bush 杜鹃丛, alpine rhododendron 高山杜鹃, rhododendron garden 杜鹃园, wild rhododendron 野杜鹃
[联] azalea 杜鹃, shrub 灌木, flower 花, spring 春天, pink 粉的, purple 紫的, woodland 林地
[记] 可数：a rhododendron。与 azalea 近缘，园艺中常并提；部分品种喜酸性土

**petunia**
英/美 /pəˈtjuːniə/
n. 矮牵牛；碧冬茄
[义] A popular garden plant with trumpet-shaped flowers in many colours, blooming all summer. 夏季持续开花、喇叭形花、色多的常见园艺植物（petunia basket 矮牵牛吊篮, trailing petunias 垂吊矮牵牛, petunia bed 矮牵牛花坛）。可数。
[例] ① Petunias spilled over the edges of the hanging baskets. 矮牵牛从吊篮边缘垂泻而下。 ② She deadheaded the petunias to keep them blooming. 她给矮牵牛摘残花以促继续开。
[搭] petunia basket 矮牵牛吊篮, trailing petunias 垂吊矮牵牛, petunia bed 矮牵牛花坛, plant petunias 种矮牵牛
[联] flower 花, garden 花园, annual 一年生, deadhead 摘残花, colourful 鲜艳的, summer 夏天, basket 吊篮
[记] 可数：a petunia。阳台与窗台极常见；易养护、花色多

**geranium**
英/美 /dʒəˈreɪniəm/
n. 天竺葵；老鹳草
[义] A plant with rounded leaves and clusters of red, pink, or white flowers, common in pots and window boxes. 圆叶、簇生红粉白花，常见于盆与窗台花箱（scented geranium 香天竺葵, geranium pot 天竺葵盆, ivy geranium 常春藤叶天竺葵）。可数。
[例] ① Geraniums brightened every windowsill on the street. 街上每个窗台都被天竺葵点亮。 ② She rubbed a scented geranium leaf between her fingers. 她用手指揉捻香天竺葵叶片。
[搭] scented geranium 香天竺葵, geranium pot 天竺葵盆, ivy geranium 常春藤叶天竺葵, red geraniums 红天竺葵
[联] flower 花, pot 盆, garden 花园, leaf 叶, fragrance 芬芳, windowsill 窗台, pink 粉的
[记] 可数：a geranium。园艺「geranium」多指 Pelargonium；true geranium 为另一类宿根草花

**wisteria**
英/美 /wɪˈstɪəriə/
n. 紫藤
[义] A climbing plant with long hanging clusters of purple or white flowers in spring. 春季垂挂长串紫或白花的攀缘植物（wisteria vine 紫藤, wisteria pergola 紫藤花架, Chinese wisteria 中国紫藤）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Wisteria draped the pergola in lavender cascades. 紫藤如淡紫瀑布覆满花架。 ② The courtyard smelled of wisteria after the morning rain. 晨雨后庭院弥漫着紫藤香。
[搭] wisteria vine 紫藤, wisteria pergola 紫藤花架, Chinese wisteria 中国紫藤, wisteria bloom 紫藤花期
[联] vine 藤, flower 花, purple 紫的, climb 攀爬, spring 春天, trellis 棚架, fragrance 芬芳
[记] 可数/不可数。生长强劲，需坚固棚架；东亚庭院经典藤本

**foxglove**
英/美 /ˈfɒksɡlʌv/
n. 毛地黄
[义] A tall plant with a spike of tubular purple or white flowers; highly poisonous. 高大、穗状管形紫或白花；剧毒（foxglove spike 毛地黄穗, digitalis 洋地黄〔药〕, woodland foxglove 林地毛地黄）。可数。
[例] ① Foxgloves rose above the ferns at the wood's edge. 毛地黄挺立在林缘蕨丛之上。 ② Gardeners warned children not to touch the foxglove flowers. 园丁告诫孩子勿触毛地黄花。
[搭] foxglove spike 毛地黄穗, woodland foxglove 林地毛地黄, poisonous foxglove 有毒毛地黄, tall foxgloves 高毛地黄
[联] biennial 二年生, flower 花, poisonous 有毒的, woodland 林地, purple 紫的, tall 高的, fern 蕨
[记] 可数：a foxglove。digitalis 为心脏病药前体；野生与花园均常见，勿误食

**iris**
英/美 /ˈaɪrɪs/
n. 鸢尾；蝴蝶花
[义] A plant with long sword-shaped leaves and large purple, yellow, or white flowers. 叶剑形、开大朵紫黄白花的植物（bearded iris 有髯鸢尾, iris bed 鸢尾花坛, water iris 水生鸢尾）。可数。
[例] ① Irises lined the pond bank in early summer. 初夏鸢尾排列池岸。 ② She divided the iris clumps after they finished blooming. 花谢后她把鸢尾丛分株。
[搭] bearded iris 有髯鸢尾, iris bed 鸢尾花坛, water iris 水生鸢尾, purple irises 紫鸢尾
[联] flower 花, leaf 叶, pond 池塘, purple 紫的, perennial 多年生, garden 花园, bloom 开花
[记] 可数：an iris。iris 亦指「虹膜、彩虹」，依语境区分；梵高的鸢尾画极有名

**basil**
英/美 /ˈbæzl/
n. 罗勒；九层塔
[义] A sweet-smelling herb with green leaves, essential in Mediterranean cooking. 绿叶清香，地中海烹饪必备香草（fresh basil 新鲜罗勒, basil leaves 罗勒叶, Thai basil 泰式罗勒）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Basil wilted quickly in the sun until she watered the pots. 罗勒在阳光下很快打蔫，浇水后才恢复。 ② She pinched the tops off basil plants to encourage bushier growth. 她掐去罗勒顶端以促分枝。
[搭] fresh basil 新鲜罗勒, basil leaves 罗勒叶, Thai basil 泰式罗勒, grow basil 种罗勒, basil plant 罗勒植株
[联] herb 香草, mint 薄荷, leaf 叶, garden 花园, fragrance 芬芳, tomato 番茄, cooking 烹饪
[记] 不可数/可数。与番茄、Mozzarella 经典搭配；食材用法在食物章

**rosemary**
英/美 /ˈrəʊzməri/
n. 迷迭香
[义] An evergreen herb with narrow aromatic leaves, used in cooking and gardens. 窄叶芳香、常绿，用于烹饪与园艺的香草（rosemary bush 迷迭香丛, rosemary sprig 迷迭香小枝, creeping rosemary 匍匐迷迭香）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Rosemary filled the herb bed with needle-like fragrance. 迷迭香以针状清香充满香草圃。 ② She clipped rosemary sprigs to roast with potatoes. 她剪迷迭香小枝与土豆同烤。
[搭] rosemary bush 迷迭香丛, rosemary sprig 迷迭香小枝, creeping rosemary 匍匐迷迭香, grow rosemary 种迷迭香
[联] herb 香草, evergreen 常绿, fragrance 芬芳, garden 花园, leaf 叶, needle 针状, cooking 烹饪
[记] 不可数/可数。耐旱常绿；象征 remembrance（纪念）

**thyme**
英/美 /taɪm/
n. 百里香
[义] A low-growing herb with tiny aromatic leaves, used to flavour meat and stews. 低矮、小叶芳香，用于肉类与炖菜的香草（fresh thyme 新鲜百里香, thyme sprig 百里香枝, lemon thyme 柠檬百里香）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Thyme spread between the paving stones in the sunny courtyard. 百里香在向阳庭院石板缝间蔓延。 ② She tied a bundle of thyme and hung it to dry. 她扎一束百里香悬挂晾干。
[搭] fresh thyme 新鲜百里香, thyme sprig 百里香枝, lemon thyme 柠檬百里香, wild thyme 野百里香
[联] herb 香草, rosemary 迷迭香, sage 鼠尾草, leaf 叶, fragrance 芬芳, garden 花园, low-growing 低矮的
[记] 不可数/可数。thyme 与 time 同音异义；普罗旺斯草本组合常见

**sage**
英/美 /seɪdʒ/
n. 鼠尾草
[义] A herb with soft grey-green leaves and a strong savoury smell. 叶灰绿柔软、气味浓郁的开胃香草（garden sage 园鼠尾草, sage leaves 鼠尾草叶, sage bush 鼠尾草丛）。不可数/可数。另义「智者」为名词，依语境区分。
[例] ① Sage bushes attracted bees in the herb garden. 鼠尾草丛在香草园招蜂。 ② She rubbed sage leaves to release their sharp scent. 她揉搓鼠尾草叶释放浓烈香气。
[搭] garden sage 园鼠尾草, sage leaves 鼠尾草叶, sage bush 鼠尾草丛, grow sage 种鼠尾草
[联] herb 香草, thyme 百里香, rosemary 迷迭香, leaf 叶, grey-green 灰绿的, fragrance 芬芳, garden 花园
[记] 不可数/可数。sage 亦指「智者、贤达」——多义。Thanksgiving 填料常用

**parsley**
英/美 /ˈpɑːsli/
n. 欧芹；香菜〔英式有时混称〕
[义] A herb with curly or flat green leaves, used fresh as garnish and in cooking. 叶卷曲或扁平、翠绿，作装饰与烹饪的香草（flat-leaf parsley 平叶欧芹, curly parsley 卷曲欧芹, parsley patch 欧芹圃）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Parsley self-seeded along the vegetable bed edges. 欧芹在菜床边缘自播繁衍。 ② She snipped parsley over the soup at the table. 她在桌上把欧芹剪入汤里。
[搭] flat-leaf parsley 平叶欧芹, curly parsley 卷曲欧芹, parsley patch 欧芹圃, fresh parsley 新鲜欧芹
[联] herb 香草, leaf 叶, garnish 装饰, garden 花园, green 绿的, cooking 烹饪, cilantro 芫荽叶
[记] 不可数/可数。flat-leaf 与 curly 为常见品种；与 cilantro（芫荽）勿混

**maize**
英/美 /meɪz/
n. 玉米；玉蜀黍
[义] (Especially BrE) The cereal plant known as corn in American English; its grains. 尤英式指美式 corn 所称的玉米作物及其籽粒（maize field 玉米田, maize crop 玉米收成, sweet maize 甜玉米）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Maize stood taller than a person by midsummer. 仲夏玉米长得比人还高。 ② The region rotated maize with beans to enrich the soil. 该地区以玉米与豆类轮作肥土。
[搭] maize field 玉米田, maize crop 玉米收成, sweet maize 甜玉米, grow maize 种玉米
[联] corn 玉米, crop 作物, field 田, harvest 收获, grain 谷物, stalk 秆, farm 农场
[记] 不可数/可数。英式 maize = 美式 corn（玉米）；英式 corn 可泛指谷物，须看语境

**sorghum**
英/美 /ˈsɔːɡəm/
n. 高粱
[义] A drought-tolerant cereal grown for grain and animal feed, especially in hot regions. 耐旱谷物，为粒与饲料而种，尤见于炎热地区（sorghum field 高粱田, sorghum crop 高粱收成, sweet sorghum 甜高粱）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Sorghum survived weeks without rain on the plain. 平原上高粱数周无雨仍存活。 ② Farmers planted sorghum where maize struggled in the heat. 农民在玉米不耐热处改种高粱。
[搭] sorghum field 高粱田, sorghum crop 高粱收成, sweet sorghum 甜高粱, grow sorghum 种高粱
[联] crop 作物, grain 谷物, drought 干旱, field 田, harvest 收获, cereal 谷类, maize 玉米
[记] 不可数/可数。非洲与亚洲重要粮食作物；亦作生物能源原料

**millet**
英/美 /ˈmɪlɪt/
n. 小米；粟
[义] A small-grained cereal crop grown in dry warm regions. 干旱暖区种植的小粒谷类作物（millet field 谷子田, finger millet 稗谷〔粟类品种〕, millet crop 谷子收成）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Millet ripened gold on the terraced hillsides. 谷子在山坡梯田上熟成金黄。 ② Birds flocked to the millet fields at harvest time. 收获时节鸟群飞赴谷子田。
[搭] millet field 谷子田, millet crop 谷子收成, grow millet 种粟, bird millet 饲鸟粟
[联] grain 谷物, crop 作物, cereal 谷类, field 田, harvest 收获, drought 耐旱, bird 鸟
[记] 不可数/可数。亚洲与非洲传统主食；西方亦作鸟食与健康谷物

**onion**
英/美 /ˈʌnjən/
n. 洋葱
[义] A round vegetable with layers and a strong smell, growing underground. 地下生长的圆球状、分层、气味浓烈的蔬菜（onion bulb 洋葱鳞茎, onion patch 洋葱地, spring onion 小葱〔食物章〕）。可数。
[例] ① Onion tops poked through the soil in neat rows. 洋葱叶在整齐垄行间破土而出。 ② She harvested onions when the leaves turned brown and fell. 叶褐下垂时她采收洋葱。
[搭] onion bulb 洋葱鳞茎, onion patch 洋葱地, grow onions 种洋葱, red onion 红洋葱
[联] bulb 鳞茎, root 根, vegetable 蔬菜, garden 花园, crop 作物, harvest 收获, layer 层
[记] 可数：an onion。植株义在本章；切洋葱流泪为厨房常识，烹饪在食物章

**garlic**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːlɪk/
n. 大蒜
[义] A plant with a strong taste and smell, whose cloves grow in a bulb underground. 味烈气浓、蒜瓣于地下鳞茎中生长的植物（garlic bulb 蒜头, garlic patch 大蒜地, garlic scape 蒜薹）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Garlic scapes curled above the spring garden beds. 春天蒜薹在菜床上方弯曲生长。 ② She planted garlic cloves before the first hard frost. 她在初霜前种下蒜瓣。
[搭] garlic bulb 蒜头, garlic patch 大蒜地, garlic scape 蒜薹, grow garlic 种大蒜
[联] bulb 鳞茎, herb 香草, garden 花园, crop 作物, strong 浓烈的, clove 蒜瓣, harvest 收获
[记] 不可数/可数。植株与种植在本章；调味与烹饪在食物章

**bean**
英/美 /biːn/
n. 豆；豆科植物
[义] A seed or pod of various climbing or bushy plants; the plant itself. 多种攀缘或灌木状植物的籽或荚；植株本身（bean plant 豆株, bean pole 豆架, green beans 四季豆〔食物章〕）。可数。
[例] ① Bean vines climbed the poles in the kitchen garden. 厨房菜园里豆藤爬上豆架。 ② She planted bush beans where space was tight. 空间有限处她种矮生豆。
[搭] bean plant 豆株, bean pole 豆架, climb beans 架豆, bush beans 矮生豆, runner beans 架豆
[联] legume 豆类, crop 作物, vine 藤, garden 花园, pod 荚, harvest 收获, stake 桩
[记] 可数：a bean。full of beans 俚语「精力充沛」，与豆植株无关

**pea**
英/美 /piː/
n. 豌豆
[义] A small round green seed eaten as a vegetable; the climbing plant that produces it. 作蔬菜食用的小圆绿籽；产籽的攀缘植物（pea plant 豌豆株, pea trellis 豌豆架, sweet peas 香豌豆〔观赏〕）。可数/通常复数 peas。
[例] ① Pea tendrils curled around the garden netting. 豌豆卷须缠住园网。 ② Sweet peas perfumed the fence each June. 每年六月香豌豆为篱笆添香。
[搭] pea plant 豌豆株, pea trellis 豌豆架, sweet peas 香豌豆, garden peas 菜豌豆, pea shoots 豌豆苗
[联] legume 豆类, vine 藤, pod 荚, garden 花园, green 绿的, crop 作物, flower 花
[记] 可数/复数 peas。like two peas in a pod 喻「一模一样」；sweet pea 观赏品种

**spinach**
英/美 /ˈspɪnɪtʃ/
n. 菠菜
[义] A plant with dark green leaves eaten as a vegetable, rich in iron. 深绿叶作蔬菜食用的植物，富铁（spinach bed 菠菜床, baby spinach 嫩菠菜叶, bolted spinach 抽薹菠菜）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Spinach leaves were large enough to harvest within six weeks. 六周内菠菜叶已大可采收。 ② Heat made the spinach bolt before she could pick it. 天热使菠菜在她采收前抽薹。
[搭] spinach bed 菠菜床, baby spinach 嫩菠菜叶, grow spinach 种菠菜, bolted spinach 抽薹菠菜
[联] leaf 叶, vegetable 蔬菜, garden 花园, green 绿的, crop 作物, harvest 收获, iron 铁
[记] 不可数/可数。bolt 指「抽薹开花」；波派吃菠菜为文化梗，食材在食物章

**broccoli**
英/美 /ˈbrɒkəli/
n. 西兰花；花椰菜近缘
[义] A vegetable plant with thick green flowering heads eaten before the flowers open. 未开花前采收厚绿花头的蔬菜植株（broccoli plant 西兰花株, broccoli head 花球, sprouting broccoli 芦笋西兰花）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Broccoli heads swelled in the cool autumn beds. 西兰花球在秋日凉床中膨大。 ② Caterpillars sometimes chewed holes in the broccoli leaves. 毛虫有时把西兰花叶啃出洞。
[搭] broccoli plant 西兰花株, broccoli head 花球, sprouting broccoli 芦笋西兰花, grow broccoli 种西兰花
[联] cabbage 甘蓝, vegetable 蔬菜, leaf 叶, garden 花园, crop 作物, harvest 收获, flower 花
[记] 不可数/可数。与 cauliflower 近缘；本章取植株义

**cucumber**
英/美 /ˈkjuːkʌmbə(r)/
n. 黄瓜
[义] A long green fruit growing on a climbing plant, eaten raw or pickled. 攀缘植物上所结的长形绿果，生食或腌制（cucumber vine 黄瓜藤, cucumber patch 黄瓜地, greenhouse cucumbers 温室黄瓜）。可数。
[例] ① Cucumber vines sprawled across the raised bed trellis. 黄瓜藤爬满高床棚架。 ② She picked cucumbers before they turned yellow and bitter. 她在黄瓜变黄变苦前采摘。
[搭] cucumber vine 黄瓜藤, cucumber patch 黄瓜地, greenhouse cucumbers 温室黄瓜, grow cucumbers 种黄瓜
[联] vine 藤, garden 花园, fruit 果实, green 绿的, trellis 棚架, harvest 收获, pickle 腌制
[记] 可数：a cucumber。cool as a cucumber 喻「镇定自若」，与植株无关

**pepper**
英/美 /ˈpepə(r)/
n. 辣椒；甜椒；胡椒
[义] A plant bearing hot or sweet fruit used as a vegetable or spice; the fruit itself. 结辣或甜果、作蔬菜或香料的植物及其果实（bell pepper 甜椒, pepper plant 辣椒株, chilli pepper 辣椒）。可数/不可数。black pepper 为香料，依语境区分。
[例] ① Pepper plants reddened on the sunny south wall. 辣椒在向阳南墙转红。 ② She staked pepper plants when the fruit weighed down the branches. 果实压枝时她给辣椒打桩。
[搭] bell pepper 甜椒, pepper plant 辣椒株, chilli pepper 辣椒, green peppers 青椒, hot peppers 辣椒
[联] vegetable 蔬菜, plant 植株, garden 花园, spicy 辣的, red 红的, crop 作物, stake 桩
[记] 可数/不可数。bell pepper / sweet pepper 为甜椒；black pepper 来自 Piper 属，非本义

**uproot**
英/美 /ʌpˈruːt/
v. 连根拔起；根除
[义] To pull a plant out of the ground, including the roots; to remove completely. 将植物连根拔出；彻底铲除（uproot weeds 拔草连根, uproot a tree 拔树, uprooted plants 被拔起的植株）。动词。
[例] ① Storms uprooted several old trees along the riverbank. 暴风雨把河岸几棵老树连根拔起。 ② Volunteers uprooted invasive plants from the meadow. 志愿者从草地根除入侵植物。
[搭] uproot weeds 拔草连根, uproot a tree 拔树, uprooted plants 被拔植株, uproot completely 彻底根除
[联] root 根, weed 杂草, remove 移除, transplant 移栽, storm 暴风雨, invasive 入侵的, dig 挖
[记] 及物动词。up- + root；比喻 uproot a family 指「迫使迁离」

**chestnut**
英/美 /ˈtʃesnʌt/
n. 栗子；栗树
[义] A large edible nut; the tall deciduous tree that bears it. 大型可食坚果；结果的高大落叶树（chestnut tree 栗树, chestnut grove 栗树林, horse chestnut 七叶树〔观赏，果不可食〕）。可数。
[例] ① Chestnuts fell in spiky husks on the forest path. 刺壳栗果落在林间小径上。 ② The old chestnut tree shaded the village square. 老栗树荫蔽村广场。
[搭] chestnut tree 栗树, chestnut grove 栗树林, horse chestnut 七叶树, roast chestnuts 烤栗子（食物章）
[联] tree 树, nut 坚果, forest 森林, autumn 秋天, deciduous 落叶的, oak 橡, harvest 收获
[记] 可数：a chestnut。horse chestnut 为观赏七叶树，果通常不食用；old chestnut 俚语指「老掉牙笑话」

**walnut**
英/美 /ˈwɔːlnʌt/
n. 核桃；胡桃树
[义] A hard-shelled edible nut; the large tree that produces it. 硬壳可食坚果；产此果的大树（walnut tree 核桃树, walnut grove 核桃园, black walnut 黑核桃）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Walnut leaves perfumed the lane in early summer. 初夏核桃叶香满小巷。 ② She gathered fallen walnuts from under the orchard trees. 她从果园树下捡落核桃。
[搭] walnut tree 核桃树, walnut grove 核桃园, black walnut 黑核桃, walnut shell 核桃壳
[联] tree 树, nut 坚果, orchard 果园, wood 木材, harvest 收获, shell 壳, autumn 秋天
[记] 可数/不可数。核桃木为优质家具材；食材义在食物章

**ash**
英/美 /æʃ/
n. 梣树；白蜡树
[义] A tall tree with grey bark and leaves divided into leaflets; its hard pale wood. 皮灰、复叶的高大乔木；其坚硬浅色木材（ash tree 梣树, mountain ash 花楸〔不同种〕, ash wood 梣木）。可数/不可数。ash 亦指「灰烬」，依语境区分。
[例] ① Ash trees lined the lane with tall pale trunks. 梣树以高大浅色树干排列在小巷两侧。 ② Ash dieback disease threatened woodlands across the country. 梣树枯病威胁全国林地。
[搭] ash tree 梣树, ash wood 梣木, ash dieback 梣树枯病, mountain ash 花楸
[联] tree 树, wood 木材, forest 森林, leaf 叶, bark 树皮, disease 病害, deciduous 落叶的
[记] 可数/不可数。ash 与灰烬同形；棒球 bat 常用 ash wood

**stamen**
英/美 /ˈsteɪmən/
n. 雄蕊
[义] The male part of a flower that produces pollen. 花中产生花粉的雄性部分（stamen and pistil 雄蕊与雌蕊, pollen on stamens 雄蕊上的花粉）。可数。
[例] ① Students examined the stamens under a magnifying glass. 学生用放大镜观察雄蕊。 ② Bees brushed against the stamens collecting pollen. 蜜蜂蹭过雄蕊采集花粉。
[搭] stamen and pistil 雄蕊与雌蕊, pollen on stamens 雄蕊花粉, count stamens 数雄蕊
[联] pistil 雌蕊, pollen 花粉, petal 花瓣, flower 花, pollinate 授粉, bee 蜜蜂, botany 植物学
[记] 可数：a stamen。复数 stamens 或 stamina（后者易与「耐力」混淆）

**pistil**
英/美 /ˈpɪstɪl/
n. 雌蕊
[义] The female part of a flower that receives pollen and forms seeds. 花中接受花粉并发育为种子的雌性部分（pistil and stamen 雌蕊与雄蕊, central pistil 中央雌蕊）。可数。
[例] ① The pistil stood at the centre of the lily flower. 雌蕊立于百合花中央。 ② Pollen must reach the pistil for seeds to form. 花粉须抵达雌蕊方能结实。
[搭] pistil and stamen 雌蕊与雄蕊, central pistil 中央雌蕊, pollen reaches the pistil 花粉达雌蕊
[联] stamen 雄蕊, pollen 花粉, seed 种子, flower 花, fruit 果实, pollinate 授粉, lily 百合
[记] 可数：a pistil。与 pistol（手枪）发音相近，拼写不同

**seedbed**
英/美 /ˈsiːdbed/
n. 苗床；温床
[义] A plot of soil prepared for sowing seeds; a place where ideas or trouble grow (fig.). 为播种整备的土床；喻某种事物滋生的温床。可数。
[例] ① She raked the seedbed fine before sowing lettuce. 播生菜前她把苗床耙细。 ② Seedlings filled every tray in the greenhouse seedbed. 温室苗床每盘都长满秧苗。
[搭] prepare a seedbed 整苗床, fine seedbed 细土苗床, greenhouse seedbed 温室苗床, sow in the seedbed 播于苗床
[联] seed 种子, sow 播种, seedling 幼苗, soil 土壤, rake 耙, garden 花园, nursery 苗圃
[记] 可数：a seedbed。比喻 a seedbed of unrest 指「动荡温床」

**crocus**
英/美 /ˈkrəʊkəs/
n. 番红花
[义] A small spring flower growing from a corm, often purple, yellow, or white, appearing early in the year. 由球茎长出的小型春花，常紫黄白，年初即开（spring crocus 春番红花, crocus bulb 番红花球茎, crocus lawn 番红花草坪）。可数。
[例] ① Crocuses dotted the lawn the first warm week of March. 三月第一个暖周，番红花点缀草坪。 ② Bees woke early to visit the crocus cups. 蜂早早醒来造访番红花杯状花。
[搭] spring crocus 春番红花, crocus bulb 番红花球茎, crocus lawn 番红花草坪, purple crocuses 紫番红花
[联] bulb 球茎, spring 春天, flower 花, lawn 草坪, bee 蜜蜂, early 早开的, saffron 藏红花
[记] 可数：a crocus。saffron 来自 Crocus sativus 的花柱，为不同用途品种

**cacao**
英/美 /kəˈkaʊ/
n. 可可树
[义] The tropical tree whose seeds are used to make chocolate and cocoa. 热带树种，其籽用于制作巧克力与可可（cacao tree 可可树, cacao plantation 可可种植园, cacao pods 可可荚）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Cacao pods ripened yellow and red on the shaded plantation. 可可荚在荫蔽种植园转成黄红。 ② Farmers fermented cacao beans before drying them in the sun. 农民将可可豆发酵后日晒干燥。
[搭] cacao tree 可可树, cacao plantation 可可种植园, cacao pods 可可荚, grow cacao 种可可
[联] tropical 热带的, plantation 种植园, pod 荚, bean 豆, chocolate 巧克力, harvest 收获, shade 荫
[记] 不可数/可数。拼写 cacao（树/豆）与 cocoa（粉末饮品）常混；植株义在本章

**narcissus**
英/美 /nɑːˈsɪsəs/
n. 水仙
（复数 narcissi /nɑːˈsɪsaɪ/ 或 narcissuses）
[义] A spring bulb flower with white or yellow petals and a trumpet-shaped centre; the daffodil is one kind. 春季球根花，瓣白或黄、花心喇叭状；黄水仙为其中一种（wild narcissus 野水仙, narcissus bulb 水仙球茎, paperwhite narcissus 纸白水仙）。可数。
[例] ① Narcissi lined the woodland path each March. 每年三月水仙排列林径。 ② She planted narcissus bulbs in drifts under the birch trees. 她把水仙球茎成片种在桦树下。
[搭] wild narcissus 野水仙, narcissus bulb 水仙球茎, spring narcissus 春水仙, narcissus cluster 水仙花丛
[联] daffodil 黄水仙, bulb 球茎, spring 春天, flower 花, crocus 番红花, yellow 黄的, white 白的
[记] 可数；复数 narcissi 或 narcissuses。daffodil 为常见种；希腊神话 Narcissus 喻自恋，与花无关

**hyacinth**
英/美 /ˈhaɪəsɪnθ/
n. 风信子
[义] A fragrant spring flower growing from a bulb, with dense clusters of blue, pink, or white blooms. 春季球根香花，密簇蓝粉或白花（Dutch hyacinth 荷兰风信子, hyacinth bulb 风信子球茎, grape hyacinth 葡萄风信子）。可数。
[例] ① Hyacinths perfumed the windowsill greenhouse in April. 四月风信子让窗台温室香气四溢。 ② Grape hyacinths spread as a blue carpet under the shrubs. 葡萄风信子在灌木下铺成蓝色花毯。
[搭] Dutch hyacinth 荷兰风信子, hyacinth bulb 风信子球茎, grape hyacinth 葡萄风信子, fragrant hyacinths 香风信子
[联] bulb 球茎, spring 春天, flower 花, fragrance 芬芳, crocus 番红花, tulip 郁金香, garden 花园
[记] 可数：a hyacinth。grape hyacinth 为较小品种 Muscari；盆养室内常见

**lilac**
英/美 /ˈlaɪlək/
n. 丁香；紫丁香
[义] A bush or small tree with fragrant purple, pink, or white flower clusters in spring. 春开芳香紫粉或白花簇的灌木或小乔木（lilac bush 丁香丛, lilac hedge 丁香树篱, common lilac 普通丁香）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Lilac scent drifted from the hedge every May morning. 每年五月清晨丁香香从树篱飘来。 ② She cut lilac sprays for the kitchen table. 她剪丁香花枝插餐桌。
[搭] lilac bush 丁香丛, lilac hedge 丁香树篱, common lilac 普通丁香, lilac flowers 丁香花
[联] shrub 灌木, flower 花, spring 春天, fragrance 芬芳, purple 紫的, hedge 树篱, blossom 花朵
[记] 可数/不可数。lilac 亦作淡紫色；北美与欧洲庭院极常见

**honeysuckle**
英/美 /ˈhʌnisʌkl/
n. 忍冬；金银花
[义] A climbing or arching shrub with sweet-scented tubular flowers, often cream or yellow. 攀缘或拱形灌木，管状花芳香，常乳黄或黄色（honeysuckle vine 忍冬藤, wild honeysuckle 野忍冬, Japanese honeysuckle 日本忍冬）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Honeysuckle twined around the porch railings all summer. 忍冬整个夏天缠绕门廊栏杆。 ② Moths visited honeysuckle blooms after sunset. 飞蛾日落后造访忍冬花。
[搭] honeysuckle vine 忍冬藤, wild honeysuckle 野忍冬, Japanese honeysuckle 日本忍冬, honeysuckle scent 忍冬香
[联] vine 藤, fragrance 芬芳, climb 攀爬, shrub 灌木, moth 飞蛾, summer 夏天, flower 花
[记] 不可数/可数。中药「金银花」多指 Lonicera japonica；部分种类入侵性强

**primrose**
英/美 /ˈprɪmrəʊz/
n. 樱草；报春花
[义] A small pale yellow wildflower that blooms in early spring. 早春开放的小型淡黄野花（evening primrose 月见草〔不同属〕, primrose path 享乐之路〔喻〕, wild primroses 野樱草）。可数。
[例] ① Primroses dotted the bank beneath the hedgerow. 樱草点缀树篱下土坡。 ② Children picked primroses on the first warm Sunday of spring. 孩子春天第一个暖周日采樱草。
[搭] wild primroses 野樱草, primrose bank 樱草坡, cowslip 黄花九轮草（近缘）, primrose yellow 樱草黄
[联] wildflower 野花, spring 春天, yellow 黄的, woodland 林地, flower 花, early 早开的, meadow 草地
[记] 可数：a primrose。primrose path 文学喻「享乐导致毁灭之路」；evening primrose 为月见草，不同种

**bluebell**
英/美 /ˈbluːbel/
n. 蓝铃花；野风信子
[义] A woodland flower with drooping blue bell-shaped blooms in spring. 春季林下垂挂蓝色钟形花的植物（English bluebell 英国蓝铃花, bluebell wood 蓝铃花林）。可数/通常复数 bluebells。
[例] ① Bluebells carpeted the oak wood in a haze of violet-blue. 蓝铃花以蓝紫雾毯覆盖橡树林。 ② Visitors came each April to walk the famous bluebell trail. 游客每年四月来走著名的蓝铃花径。
[搭] English bluebell 英国蓝铃花, bluebell wood 蓝铃花林, bluebell carpet 蓝铃花毯, spring bluebells 春蓝铃花
[联] wildflower 野花, woodland 林地, spring 春天, blue 蓝的, flower 花, forest 森林, bulb 球茎
[记] 可数/复数 bluebells。英国蓝铃花林为春季旅游亮点；与苏格兰风铃草名易混

**linden**
英/美 /ˈlɪndən/
n. 椴树；菩提树
[义] A tall deciduous tree with heart-shaped leaves and fragrant yellow flowers. 高大落叶乔木，心形叶、黄花芳香（linden tree 椴树, linden avenue 椴树大道, silver linden 银椴）。可数。
[例] ① Bees swarmed around the linden blossoms in June. 六月蜜蜂簇拥椴树花。 ② Linden shade cooled the park benches on hot afternoons. 炎热午后椴树荫凉公园长椅。
[搭] linden tree 椴树, linden avenue 椴树大道, linden honey 椴树蜜, silver linden 银椴
[联] tree 树, leaf 叶, honey 蜂蜜, bee 蜜蜂, shade 荫, deciduous 落叶的, fragrance 芬芳
[记] 可数：a linden。亦称 lime tree（英式），与 lime 水果无关；菩提树在欧亚文化中有圣树意象

**beech**
英/美 /biːtʃ/
n. 山毛榉；榉树
[义] A large tree with smooth grey bark and small nuts in prickly cases. 皮光滑灰白、小坚果包于刺壳的大树（beech tree 山毛榉, beech forest 山毛榉林, copper beech 紫叶山毛榉）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Beech leaves rustled copper in the autumn wood. 秋林中山毛榉叶沙沙作响、泛铜色。 ② The path wound through a cathedral of tall beech trunks. 小径蜿蜒穿过高耸山毛榉干构成的「大教堂」。
[搭] beech tree 山毛榉, beech forest 山毛榉林, copper beech 紫叶山毛榉, beech nut 山毛榉果
[联] tree 树, forest 森林, leaf 叶, bark 树皮, deciduous 落叶的, nut 坚果, autumn 秋天
[记] 可数/不可数。山毛榉材致密，为家具与地板常用；not out of the wood 习语与 beech 无关

**sycamore**
英/美 /ˈsɪkəmɔː(r)/
n. 悬铃木；美国梧桐
[义] A large tree with broad lobed leaves and winged seeds that spin as they fall. 大叶掌状裂、种子带翅旋转飘落的大树（sycamore tree 悬铃木, sycamore avenue 悬铃木大道, London plane 伦敦梧桐〔近缘〕）。可数。
[例] ① Sycamore keys helicoptered down onto the pavement. 悬铃木翅果旋落人行道。 ② The old sycamore shaded the village cricket ground. 老悬铃木荫蔽村里板球场。
[搭] sycamore tree 悬铃木, sycamore avenue 悬铃木大道, sycamore key 悬铃木翅果, London plane 伦敦梧桐
[联] tree 树, leaf 叶, seed 种子, shade 荫, avenue 大道, park 公园, autumn 秋天
[记] 可数：a sycamore。英式 sycamore 有时指槭属；美式多指 Platanus；依地区识读

**yew**
英/美 /juː/
n. 红豆杉；紫杉
[义] An evergreen tree or shrub with dark needles and red berry-like cups, often used in churchyards. 常绿乔木或灌木，暗色针叶、红色假果杯状，常见于教堂墓地（yew tree 紫杉, yew hedge 紫杉树篱, ancient yew 古紫杉）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Ancient yews stood in the churchyard older than the building. 教堂墓地古紫杉比建筑更老。 ② She clipped the yew hedge into crisp geometric shapes. 她把紫杉树篱剪成利落几何形。
[搭] yew tree 紫杉, yew hedge 紫杉树篱, ancient yew 古紫杉, yew berries 紫杉假果
[联] evergreen 常绿, tree 树, hedge 树篱, churchyard 教堂墓地, needle 针叶, poisonous 有毒的, trim 修剪
[记] 可数/不可数。紫杉全株有毒（除假种皮外）；弓材历史上用紫杉木

**hawthorn**
英/美 /ˈhɔːθɔːn/
n. 山楂
[义] A thorny bush or small tree with white blossom in spring and red berries in autumn. 多刺灌木或小乔木，春开白花、秋结红果（hawthorn hedge 山楂树篱, hawthorn blossom 山楂花, may blossom 五月花）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Hawthorn hedges bloomed white along the field boundaries. 山楂树篱沿田界绽白。 ② Birds fed on hawthorn berries through the winter. 鸟整个冬天以山楂果为食。
[搭] hawthorn hedge 山楂树篱, hawthorn blossom 山楂花, may blossom 五月花, hawthorn berries 山楂果
[联] hedge 树篱, thorn 刺, berry 浆果, blossom 花, spring 春天, bird 鸟, shrub 灌木
[记] 可数/不可数。英国传统树篱植物；may 指山楂春花；浆果可制果酱，食物章可交叉

**cauliflower**
英/美 /ˈkɒlɪflaʊə(r)/
n. 花椰菜；菜花
[义] A vegetable plant with a large white flowering head eaten before the flowers open. 未开花前采收大白花球食用的蔬菜植株（cauliflower plant 花椰菜株, cauliflower head 花球, purple cauliflower 紫菜花）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Cauliflower heads needed blanching ties to stay white in the sun. 花椰菜花球需绑叶遮光以保白。 ② Frost sweetened the autumn cauliflower in the kitchen garden. 霜冻让厨房菜园秋菜花更甜。
[搭] cauliflower plant 花椰菜株, cauliflower head 花球, purple cauliflower 紫菜花, grow cauliflower 种花椰菜
[联] broccoli 西兰花, cabbage 甘蓝, vegetable 蔬菜, garden 花园, crop 作物, leaf 叶, harvest 收获
[记] 可数/不可数。cauli-（卷心菜）+ flower；与 broccoli 近缘，栽培类似

**celery**
英/美 /ˈseləri/
n. 芹菜
[义] A vegetable with long crisp stalks and leaves, growing in moist soil. 长脆茎叶、喜湿土生长的蔬菜（celery stalk 芹菜茎, celery bed 芹菜床, leaf celery 芹叶）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Celery rows needed steady watering through the dry spell. 干旱期芹菜垄需持续浇水。 ② She blanched celery by mounding soil around the stalks. 她培土围茎使芹菜变白。
[搭] celery stalk 芹菜茎, celery bed 芹菜床, leaf celery 芹叶, grow celery 种芹菜
[联] stalk 茎, vegetable 蔬菜, garden 花园, crisp 脆的, water 浇水, crop 作物, leaf 叶
[记] 不可数/可数。stalk 为食用部位；食材与烹饪在食物章

**radish**
英/美 /ˈrædɪʃ/
n. 萝卜；小萝卜
[义] A small fast-growing root vegetable with a peppery taste, often red or white. 生长快、味辣的小型根菜，常红或白（radish patch 萝卜地, spring radishes 春萝卜, daikon 大根萝卜）。可数。
[例] ① Radishes were ready to pull within three weeks of sowing. 萝卜播后三周即可拔收。 ② She sowed radishes between slower crops as markers. 她在生长慢的作物行间播萝卜作标记。
[搭] radish patch 萝卜地, spring radishes 春萝卜, grow radishes 种萝卜, red radishes 红萝卜
[联] root 根, vegetable 蔬菜, garden 花园, crop 作物, sow 播种, fast-growing 速生的, peppery 辛辣的
[记] 可数：a radish。daikon 为大根萝卜品种；生食沙拉常见

**turnip**
英/美 /ˈtɜːnɪp/
n. 芜菁
[义] A round white or yellow root vegetable grown in cool weather. 凉季种植的圆白或黄根菜（turnip greens 芜菁叶, turnip field 芜菁田, swede 瑞典芜菁〔近缘〕）。可数。
[例] ① Turnips swelled after the autumn rains softened the soil. 秋雨润土后芜菁膨大。 ② Farmers grazed sheep on turnip fields in winter. 农民冬天让羊在芜菁田放牧。
[搭] turnip greens 芜菁叶, turnip field 芜菁田, grow turnips 种芜菁, swede 瑞典芜菁
[联] root 根, vegetable 蔬菜, crop 作物, field 田, leaf 叶, autumn 秋天, farm 农场
[记] 可数：a turnip。turnip greens 为叶菜；与 rutabaga/swede 近缘

**beet**
英/美 /biːt/
n. 甜菜
[义] A plant with a thick edible root, including sugar beet and beetroot varieties. 厚根可食的植物，含糖用甜菜与食用甜菜根（sugar beet 糖用甜菜, beetroot 甜菜根, beet field 甜菜田）。可数。
[例] ① Sugar beet rows stretched across the flat northern fields. 糖用甜菜行铺满北方平原田地。 ② She harvested beetroots when the shoulders showed above the soil. 根茎露头时她采收甜菜根。
[搭] sugar beet 糖用甜菜, beetroot 甜菜根, beet field 甜菜田, grow beets 种甜菜
[联] root 根, crop 作物, field 田, sugar 糖, vegetable 蔬菜, harvest 收获, red 红的
[记] 可数：a beet。beetroot 英式常指食用红甜菜；糖用甜菜为重要工业作物

**dill**
英/美 /dɪl/
n. 莳萝
[义] An herb with feathery leaves and seeds used for pickling and flavouring. 羽状叶、籽用于腌制与调味的香草（dill weed 莳萝叶, dill seeds 莳萝籽, dill plant 莳萝植株）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Dill volunteered between the cucumber hills each year. 莳萝每年自行生在黄瓜垄间。 ② She tied fresh dill into bundles for the pickle jars. 她把新鲜莳萝扎束入泡菜罐。
[搭] dill weed 莳萝叶, dill seeds 莳萝籽, dill plant 莳萝植株, fresh dill 新鲜莳萝
[联] herb 香草, cucumber 黄瓜, pickle 腌制, leaf 叶, seed 种子, garden 花园, fragrance 芬芳
[记] 不可数/可数。腌黄瓜经典配料；与 fennel 叶形近，勿混

**coriander**
英/美 /ˌkɒriˈændə(r)/
n. 香菜；芫荽
[义] An herb whose leaves (cilantro) and seeds are used in cooking. 叶（cilantro）与籽均用于烹饪的香草（fresh coriander 新鲜香菜, coriander leaves 香菜叶, coriander seeds 芫荽子）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Coriander bolted quickly once the days lengthened in May. 五月日长后香菜很快抽薹。 ② She sowed coriander in pots on the kitchen windowsill. 她在厨房窗台盆栽香菜。
[搭] fresh coriander 新鲜香菜, coriander leaves 香菜叶, coriander seeds 芫荽子, grow coriander 种香菜
[联] herb 香草, cilantro 香菜叶, leaf 叶, seed 种子, garden 花园, cooking 烹饪, parsley 欧芹
[记] 不可数/可数。美式叶用常称 cilantro；籽称 coriander seeds

**hemp**
英/美 /hemp/
n. 大麻
[义] A tall plant grown for its strong fibre and seeds; some varieties are low in the drug THC. 为强韧纤维与种籽而种的高大植物；部分品种 THC 含量低（hemp fibre 麻纤维, hemp field 麻田, industrial hemp 工业大麻）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Hemp fields swayed like a green sea before harvest. 收获前麻田如绿海摇曳。 ② The farm trialled industrial hemp for sustainable textiles. 农场试种工业大麻作可持续纺织原料。
[搭] hemp fibre 麻纤维, hemp field 麻田, industrial hemp 工业大麻, grow hemp 种大麻
[联] fibre 纤维, crop 作物, field 田, plant 植株, tall 高的, textile 纺织, seed 种子
[记] 不可数/可数。industrial hemp 与毒品用大麻同种不同品系；法律与政策语境须谨慎

**saffron**
英/美 /ˈsæfrən/
n. 藏红花
[义] A spice made from the dried stigmas of a crocus flower; the plant or its orange colour. 由番红花柱头干燥制成的香料；植株或橙黄色（saffron crocus 藏红花, saffron threads 藏红花丝, saffron field 藏红花田）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Farmers harvested saffron threads by hand at dawn. 农民黎明手工采收藏红花丝。 ② Saffron crocuses bloomed for only a few days each autumn. 藏红花番红每年秋天只开数日。
[搭] saffron crocus 藏红花, saffron threads 藏红花丝, saffron field 藏红花田, harvest saffron 采藏红花
[联] crocus 番红花, spice 香料, flower 花, expensive 昂贵的, orange 橙黄的, harvest 采收, autumn 秋天
[记] 不可数/可数。世界最贵香料之一；西班牙 paella 与伊朗种植文化相关

**tendril**
英/美 /ˈtendrəl/
n. 卷须
[义] A thin curling stem by which a climbing plant attaches to supports. 攀缘植物攀附支架用的细长卷曲茎（grape tendril 葡萄卷须, pea tendril 豌豆卷须, curling tendrils 卷曲卷须）。可数。
[例] ① Pea tendrils reached for the garden twine overhead. 豌豆卷须伸向头顶的园绳。 ② Grape tendrils wrapped around the wire trellis. 葡萄卷须缠绕金属棚架。
[搭] grape tendril 葡萄卷须, pea tendril 豌豆卷须, curling tendrils 卷曲卷须, tendril attachment 卷须附着
[联] vine 藤, climb 攀爬, pea 豌豆, grape 葡萄, stem 茎, support 支撑, curl 卷曲
[记] 可数：a tendril。卷须接触支撑物会卷曲缠绕；观赏与食用藤本均常见

**nettle**
英/美 /ˈnetl/
n. 荨麻
[义] A wild plant with stinging hairs on the leaves that cause a burning sensation. 叶上有刺毛、触碰灼痛的野草（stinging nettle 刺荨麻, nettle patch 荨麻丛, nettle sting 荨麻蜇痛）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Nettles stung his ankles where the path narrowed. 小径变窄处荨麻蜇他脚踝。 ② She wore gloves to pull nettles from the herb bed edge. 她戴手套从香草圃边拔荨麻。
[搭] stinging nettle 刺荨麻, nettle patch 荨麻丛, nettle sting 荨麻蜇痛, nettle soup 荨麻汤（食物章）
[联] weed 杂草, sting 蜇痛, wild 野生的, leaf 叶, herb 香草, painful 痛的, patch 丛
[记] 可数/不可数。嫩叶焯后可作野菜；grasp the nettle 习语喻「迎难而上」

**thistle**
英/美 /ˈθɪsl/
n. 蓟
[义] A wild plant with prickly leaves and often purple flower heads. 叶多刺、常开紫花的野生植物（Scottish thistle 苏格兰蓟, thistle down 蓟絮, milk thistle 水飞蓟）。可数。
[例] ① Thistles colonised the abandoned field behind the barn. 蓟占据谷仓后废弃田地。 ② Goldfinches fed on thistle seeds in late summer. 夏末金翅雀以蓟籽为食。
[搭] Scottish thistle 苏格兰蓟, thistle down 蓟絮, milk thistle 水飞蓟, thistle head 蓟花头
[联] prickly 多刺的, wildflower 野花, purple 紫的, weed 杂草, seed 种子, bird 鸟, Scotland 苏格兰
[记] 可数：a thistle。苏格兰国花；thistle down 轻絮可飘飞

**propagate**
英/美 /ˈprɒpəɡeɪt/
v. 繁殖；传播
[义] To produce new plants from a parent plant by cuttings, seeds, or division. 以扦插、播种或分株等方式繁殖新株；传播（propagate cuttings 扦插繁殖, propagate by seed 种子繁殖, propagate succulents 繁殖多肉）。动词。
[例] ① She propagated rosemary from stem cuttings in damp sand. 她在湿沙中以茎插繁殖迷迭香。 ② The nursery propagates native species for restoration projects. 苗圃为修复项目繁殖本地物种。
[形] propagation /ˌprɒpəˈɡeɪʃn/ 繁殖（不可数）
[搭] propagate cuttings 扦插繁殖, propagate by seed 种子繁殖, propagate succulents 繁殖多肉, vegetative propagation 营养繁殖
[联] grow 生长, cutting 扦插, seed 种子, plant 种植, nursery 苗圃, garden 花园, reproduce 繁殖
[记] 及物/不及物。propagation 为名词；亦泛指信息传播，依语境区分

**espalier**
英/美 /ɪˈspæliə(r)/
n. 贴墙整形栽培
v. 贴墙整枝栽培
[义] A fruit tree trained to grow flat against a wall or on wires; to train a tree in this way. 贴墙或沿铁丝网平面生长的果树；如此整枝栽培（espalier apple 贴墙苹果, espalier frame 贴墙架, fan espalier 扇形贴墙）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Espaliered pears warmed against the south-facing brick wall. 贴墙梨树贴南墙砖墙受暖。 ② He espaliered young apple trees along the garden fence. 他沿花园篱笆贴墙整枝幼苹果树。
[搭] espalier apple 贴墙苹果, espalier frame 贴墙架, fan espalier 扇形贴墙, train as espalier 贴墙整枝
[联] apple 苹果, pear 梨, prune 修剪, wall 墙, fruit 果实, garden 花园, trellis 棚架
[记] 可数/动词。法式与英式果园传统技法；省空间、利采光

**thin**
英/美 /θɪn/
v. 间苗；使稀疏
adj. 薄的；稀疏的
[义] To remove some seedlings or plants so the rest have room to grow. 拔除部分秧苗或植株，使其余有生长空间（thin seedlings 间苗, thin to six inches 间至六英寸间距, thin carrots 间胡萝卜）。动词/形容词。
[例] ① She thinned the carrot rows when the leaves touched. 胡萝卜叶相触时她间苗。 ② Thin overcrowded lettuce so heads can form. 间去过密生菜以利结球。
[搭] thin seedlings 间苗, thin to six inches 间至六英寸, thin carrots 间胡萝卜, thin out 间疏
[联] seedling 幼苗, sow 播种, garden 花园, grow 生长, spacing 间距, crop 作物, harvest 收获
[记] 动词/形容词。thin out 与 thin 园艺义同；形容词 thin 用法极广，依语境区分

**harden**
英/美 /ˈhɑːdn/
v. 使硬化；炼苗
[义] To accustom young plants to outdoor conditions before transplanting. 移栽前让幼株适应户外环境（harden off seedlings 炼苗, harden plants outdoors 户外炼苗）。动词。
[例] ① She hardened off tomato seedlings on the porch for a week. 她在门廊炼番茄秧一周。 ② Plants must be hardened before planting in the open garden. 露地栽种前须炼苗。
[搭] harden off seedlings 炼苗, harden plants outdoors 户外炼苗, harden before transplanting 移栽前炼苗
[联] seedling 幼苗, transplant 移栽, greenhouse 温室, frost 霜冻, outdoor 户外, garden 花园, gradual 逐渐的
[记] 及物/不及物。harden off 为园艺固定搭配；一般义「变硬」依语境区分

**raspberry**
英/美 /ˈrɑːzbəri/
n. 覆盆子
[义] A soft red or black berry; the prickly cane that bears it. 柔软红或黑浆果；结果的多刺茎（raspberry cane 覆盆子茎, raspberry patch 覆盆子丛, wild raspberries 野覆盆子）。可数/通常复数 raspberries。
[例] ① Raspberry canes arched over the garden path in July. 七月覆盆子茎拱过花园小径。 ② She tied raspberry canes to the wire supports each spring. 每年春天她把覆盆子茎绑到铁丝支架上。
[搭] raspberry cane 覆盆子茎, raspberry patch 覆盆子丛, wild raspberries 野覆盆子, grow raspberries 种覆盆子
[联] berry 浆果, bramble 黑莓丛, cane 茎, garden 花园, thorn 刺, fruit 果实, harvest 收获
[记] 可数/复数 raspberries。blow a raspberry 俚语「呸声」，与浆果无关

**cranberry**
英/美 /ˈkrænbəri/
n. 蔓越莓
[义] A small red sour berry; the low trailing shrub that produces it, often in bogs. 小型红色酸浆果；常生于沼泽的低矮匍匐灌木（cranberry bog 蔓越莓沼泽, cranberry harvest 蔓越莓采收, wild cranberries 野蔓越莓）。可数/通常复数 cranberries。
[例] ① Cranberry bogs turned deep red during the autumn harvest. 秋收时蔓越莓沼泽一片深红。 ② Farmers flooded the bog to float cranberries for picking. 农民淹没沼泽使蔓越莓浮起以便采摘。
[搭] cranberry bog 蔓越莓沼泽, cranberry harvest 蔓越莓采收, wild cranberries 野蔓越莓, grow cranberries 种蔓越莓
[联] berry 浆果, bog 泥沼, wetland 湿地, harvest 收获, shrub 灌木, acidic 酸性的, North America 北美
[记] 可数/复数 cranberries。北美感恩节常见；食材义在食物章

**blackberry**
英/美 /ˈblækbəri/
n. 黑莓
[义] A soft dark berry; the thorny bramble bush that bears it. 柔软深色浆果；结果的多刺黑莓灌丛（blackberry bush 黑莓丛, blackberry patch 黑莓地, wild blackberries 野黑莓）。可数/通常复数 blackberries。
[例] ① Wild blackberries ripened along the hedgerow in August. 八月篱边野黑莓成熟。 ② She wore gloves to pick blackberries from the thorny canes. 她戴手套从多刺茎上摘黑莓。
[搭] blackberry bush 黑莓丛, blackberry patch 黑莓地, wild blackberries 野黑莓, pick blackberries 采黑莓
[联] berry 浆果, bramble 黑莓丛, thorn 刺, cane 茎, hedge 树篱, raspberry 覆盆子, harvest 收获
[记] 可数/复数 blackberries。与 raspberry 近缘；blackberry 手机品牌另义

**gooseberry**
英/美 /ˈɡuːzbəri/
n. 醋栗；鹅莓
[义] A small round green, yellow, or red berry with a tart taste; the thorny bush that bears it. 圆形绿、黄或红、味酸的浆果；结果的多刺灌木（gooseberry bush 醋栗丛, green gooseberries 青醋栗, wild gooseberry 野醋栗）。可数/通常复数 gooseberries。
[例] ① Gooseberry bushes lined the cottage garden wall. 村舍花园墙边一排醋栗丛。 ② She stewed green gooseberries with sugar for a pie. 她用糖炖青醋栗做派。
[搭] gooseberry bush 醋栗丛, green gooseberries 青醋栗, wild gooseberry 野醋栗, grow gooseberries 种醋栗
[联] berry 浆果, bush 灌木, thorn 刺, garden 花园, tart 酸的, harvest 收获, currant 醋栗属
[记] 可数/复数 gooseberries。英式 play gooseberry 指「当电灯泡」——习语与植物无关

**currant**
英/美 /ˈkʌrənt/
n. 醋栗；无核小葡萄干
[义] A small dried grape used in baking; also a shrub bearing small berries related to gooseberries. 烘焙用的小粒无核葡萄干；亦指结小型浆果、与醋栗近缘的灌木（black currant 黑醋栗, red currant 红醋栗, dried currants 无核小葡萄干）。可数/通常复数 currants。
[例] ① Red currants hung in clusters on the garden bush. 红醋栗成串挂在园丛上。 ② The recipe calls for currants and sultanas in the dough. 配方要求在面团里加无核小葡萄干与苏丹娜。
[搭] black currant 黑醋栗, red currant 红醋栗, dried currants 无核小葡萄干, currant bush 醋栗丛
[联] gooseberry 醋栗, berry 浆果, bush 灌木, raisin 葡萄干, garden 花园, jam 果酱
[记] 可数/复数 currants。勿与 current（当前的/水流）混淆；葡萄干义与灌木义同形

**hazelnut**
英/美 /ˈheɪzlnʌt/
n. 榛子；榛树
[义] The edible nut of the hazel tree; the tree itself. 榛树的可食坚果；榛树本身（roasted hazelnuts 烤榛子, hazelnut tree 榛树, hazelnut spread 榛子酱）。可数。
[例] ① Squirrels buried hazelnuts beneath the oak trees. 松鼠在橡树下埋榛子。 ② Hazelnut orchards cover the hills in autumn. 秋来山坡上满是榛园。
[搭] roasted hazelnuts 烤榛子, hazelnut tree 榛树, hazelnut spread 榛子酱, wild hazelnut 野榛
[联] nut 坚果, tree 树, forest 森林, harvest 收获, chocolate 巧克力, oak 橡树
[记] 可数：a hazelnut / hazelnut trees。亦作 filbert（品种名）；与 hazel（榛树/淡褐色）相关

**quinoa**
英/美 /ˈkiːnwɑː/
n. 藜麦
[义] A South American plant whose seeds are eaten as a grain; the seeds themselves. 南美植物，其籽作谷物食用（quinoa field 藜麦田, quinoa harvest 藜麦收获, grow quinoa 种藜麦）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Quinoa thrives on the high, dry plateaus of the Andes. 藜麦在安第斯高原干冷台地上生长旺盛。 ② Farmers rotated quinoa with potatoes on the slopes. 农民在坡地轮作藜麦与土豆。
[搭] quinoa field 藜麦田, quinoa harvest 藜麦收获, grow quinoa 种藜麦, Andean quinoa 安第斯藜麦
[联] crop 作物, grain 谷物, harvest 收获, field 田, Andes 安第斯, drought 干旱, protein 蛋白质
[记] 不可数/可数。发音常 /ˈkiːnə/ 或 /kɪˈnəʊə/；作谷物时食材义亦在食物章

**strawberry**
英/美 /ˈstrɔːbəri/
n. 草莓
[义] A sweet red fruit with seeds on the surface; the low plant that produces it. 表面有籽的甜红果；低矮的结果植株（strawberry plant 草莓株, strawberry patch 草莓地, wild strawberry 野草莓）。可数/通常复数 strawberries。
[例] ① Strawberry runners spread across the raised bed each summer. 草莓匍匐茎每年夏天爬满高床。 ② She mulched strawberry plants with straw before winter. 冬前她用稻草为草莓覆盖保温。
[搭] strawberry plant 草莓株, strawberry patch 草莓地, wild strawberry 野草莓, grow strawberries 种草莓
[联] berry 浆果, fruit 果实, garden 花园, runner 匍匐茎, red 红的, mulch 覆盖, harvest 收获
[记] 可数/复数 strawberries。straw 非现代栽培必用；食材义在食物章

**blueberry**
英/美 /ˈbluːbəri/
n. 蓝莓
[义] A small dark blue edible berry; the shrub that produces it, native to North America. 深蓝色可食小浆果；产果之灌木，北美原产（blueberry bush 蓝莓丛, blueberry patch 蓝莓地, wild blueberry 野蓝莓）。可数/通常复数 blueberries。
[例] ① Blueberry bushes turned red and gold before the leaves fell. 蓝莓丛落叶前叶色转红金。 ② Birds stripped the blueberry patch before we could harvest. 我们采收前鸟已把蓝莓地啄光。
[搭] blueberry bush 蓝莓丛, blueberry patch 蓝莓地, wild blueberry 野蓝莓, grow blueberries 种蓝莓, highbush blueberry 高丛蓝莓
[联] berry 浆果, shrub 灌木, fruit 果实, garden 花园, harvest 收获, cranberry 蔓越莓, strawberry 草莓
[记] 可数/复数 blueberries。highbush 与 lowbush 为常见栽培类型；superfood 报道中高频

**plum**
英/美 /plʌm/
n. 李子；李树
[义] A soft oval fruit with smooth skin; the tree that bears it. 光滑皮的软椭圆果；结果之树（plum tree 李树, plum orchard 李园, wild plum 野李）。可数。本章取植株/果园义。
[例] ① Plum blossom scented the orchard on the first warm days. 李园在最初暖日花香四溢。 ② She thinned young plums so the branches would not break. 她疏果以防枝条压断。

[例] ① Peach trees flowered pink before the leaves unfolded. 桃树叶展前先开粉花。 ② Late frost killed the peach blossom that year. 那年晚霜冻坏了桃花。
