英/美 /ˈmæŋɡəʊ/
n. 芒果；芒果树
[义] A large sweet tropical fruit; the evergreen tree that produces it. 大型甜热带果；结果的常绿树（mango tree 芒果树, mango orchard 芒果园, mango blossom 芒果花）。可数。
[例] ① Mango blossoms perfumed the orchard before the rains. 雨季前芒果花香气弥漫果园。 ② Green mangoes hung heavy on the low branches. 青芒果低垂压枝。
[搭] mango tree 芒果树, mango orchard 芒果园, mango blossom 芒果花, ripe mangoes 熟芒果
[联] tropical 热带的, fruit 果实, tree 树, orchard 果园, evergreen 常绿, harvest 收获, sweet 甜的
[记] 可数：a mango。印度与东南亚「水果之王」；食材义在食物章

**pineapple**
英/美 /ˈpaɪnæpl/
n. 菠萝；凤梨
[义] A large tropical fruit with tough skin and sweet yellow flesh; the plant that produces it. 厚皮、果肉甜黄的大型热带果；产果之植株（pineapple plant 菠萝植株, pineapple field 菠萝田, pineapple crown 菠萝冠芽）。可数。
[例] ① Pineapple fields stretched across the coastal plain. 菠萝田延绵于沿海平原。 ② Farmers planted new pineapples from crown cuttings. 农民用冠芽扦插新株。
[搭] pineapple plant 菠萝植株, pineapple field 菠萝田, pineapple crown 菠萝冠芽, grow pineapples 种菠萝, tropical plantation 热带种植园
[联] tropical 热带的, fruit 果实, plantation 种植园, harvest 收获, sweet 甜的, mango 芒果, field 田
[记] 可数：a pineapple。名虽带 apple，与苹果无关；夏威夷与东南亚为主要产区

**avocado**
英/美 /ˌævəˈkɑːdəʊ/
n. 鳄梨；牛油果
[义] A pear-shaped fruit with creamy green flesh; the tree native to Central America. 梨形、果肉乳绿的果实；中美洲原产树（avocado tree 鳄梨树, avocado orchard 鳄梨园, Hass avocado 哈斯鳄梨）。可数。
[例] ① Avocado trees needed shelter from frost on cold nights. 寒夜鳄梨树需防霜。 ② The orchard grafted Hass avocados onto hardy rootstock. 果园将 Hass 鳄梨嫁接到耐寒砧木上。
[搭] avocado tree 鳄梨树, avocado orchard 鳄梨园, Hass avocado 哈斯鳄梨, grow avocados 种鳄梨
[联] tree 树, fruit 果实, tropical 热带的, orchard 果园, graft 嫁接, rootstock 砧木, harvest 收获
[记] 可数：an avocado。botanically 为大浆果；全球消费增长极快

**papaya**
英/美 /pəˈpaɪə/
n. 木瓜；番木瓜
[义] A large tropical fruit with orange flesh; the fast-growing tree that bears it. 大型热带果、果肉橙色；生长快的结果树（papaya tree 木瓜树, papaya plantation 木瓜种植园, green papaya 青木瓜）。可数。
[例] ① Papaya trunks stood bare after workers picked the fruit. 工人摘果后木瓜树干光秃。 ② She grew papayas in pots until they outgrew the greenhouse. 她在盆里种木瓜直至长超温室。
[搭] papaya tree 木瓜树, papaya plantation 木瓜种植园, green papaya 青木瓜, grow papaya 种木瓜
[联] tropical 热带的, fruit 果实, tree 树, plantation 种植园, fast-growing 速生的, orange 橙的, harvest 收获
[记] 可数：a papaya。树干中空、无分枝；青木瓜作蔬菜，食物章可交叉

**rootstock**
英/美 /ˈruːtstɒk/
n. 砧木
[义] The root and lower stem onto which another plant is grafted. 供另一植株嫁接其上的根与下部茎（hardy rootstock 耐寒砧木, graft onto rootstock 嫁接到砧木, rootstock selection 砧木选择）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The nursery sold apple scions and matching rootstock. 苗圃出售苹果接穗与配套砧木。 ② Dwarf rootstock kept the trees small for the home garden. 矮化砧木使树适合家庭花园。
[搭] hardy rootstock 耐寒砧木, graft onto rootstock 嫁接到砧木, rootstock selection 砧木选择, dwarf rootstock 矮化砧木
[联] graft 嫁接, scion 接穗, root 根, tree 树, nursery 苗圃, apple 苹果, dwarf 矮化的
[记] 可数/不可数。砧木决定抗性、树势与寿命；果树栽培核心词

**scion**
英/美 /ˈsaɪən/
n. 接穗；嫩枝
[义] A young shoot or twig cut for grafting onto a rootstock. 为嫁接到砧木而剪下的幼枝或嫩梢（graft a scion 嫁接接穗, scion wood 接穗条, apple scion 苹果接穗）。可数。
[例] ① She wrapped the scion to the rootstock with grafting tape. 她用嫁接带把接穗绑在砧木上。 ② Only disease-free scion wood was used in the orchard. 果园仅使用无病毒接穗条。
[搭] graft a scion 嫁接接穗, scion wood 接穗条, apple scion 苹果接穗, bud scion 芽接穗
[联] graft 嫁接, rootstock 砧木, branch 枝, twig 嫩枝, nursery 苗圃, tree 树, apple 苹果
[记] 可数：a scion。scion 亦喻「世家子弟」，与园艺义同形

**legume**
英/美 /ˈleɡjuːm/
n. 豆科植物；荚果
[义] A plant of the pea family, often with pods and nitrogen-fixing roots; its seed pod. 豆科作物，常具荚果与固氮根；其荚果（legume crop 豆科作物, nitrogen-fixing legumes 固氮豆科, legume family 豆科）。可数。
[例] ① Farmers planted legumes to restore nitrogen to tired soil. 农民种豆科作物为疲土补氮。 ② Beans and peas are common garden legumes. 菜豆与豌豆是常见园用豆科植物。
[搭] legume crop 豆科作物, nitrogen-fixing legumes 固氮豆科, legume family 豆科, grow legumes 种豆科作物
[联] bean 豆, pea 豌豆, pod 荚, soybean 大豆, nitrogen 氮, crop 作物, soil 土壤
[记] 可数：a legume。豆科与根瘤菌共生固氮；轮作农业重要

**pod**
英/美 /pɒd/
n. 荚果；豆荚
[义] A long thin case on a plant that holds seeds, especially peas and beans. 植物上包种子的长薄壳，尤指豌豆、豆类（pea pod 豌豆荚, bean pod 豆荚, split pods 裂荚）。可数。
[例] ① Pea pods swelled fat on the vines in early summer. 初夏豌豆荚在藤上鼓胀。 ② She shelled pods on the porch steps after harvesting. 采收后她在门廊台阶上剥荚。
[搭] pea pod 豌豆荚, bean pod 豆荚, split pods 裂荚, empty pods 空荚
[联] pea 豌豆, bean 豆, seed 种子, legume 豆科, harvest 收获, shell 剥荚, garden 花园
[记] 可数：a pod。pod 亦指「太空舱、豆荚形物体」——多义

**husk**
英/美 /hʌsk/
n. 外壳
[义] The dry outer covering of seeds or fruits, especially corn. 籽或果的干外皮，尤指玉米（corn husk 玉米苞叶, rice husk 稻壳, remove the husk 去壳）。可数/通常复数 husks。
[例] ① Corn husks dried on the fence after harvest. 收获后玉米苞叶在篱笆上晒干。 ② She saved husks for wrapping tamales. 她留苞叶包墨西哥玉米粽（食物章）。
[搭] corn husk 玉米苞叶, rice husk 稻壳, remove the husk 去壳, dry husks 干壳
[联] corn 玉米, seed 种子, shell 壳, harvest 收获, dry 干的, grain 谷物, wrap 包裹
[记] 可数/复数 husks。husk 动词指「剥壳」；coconut husk 为椰壳纤维

**aerate**
英/美 /ˈeəreɪt/
v. 通气；松土透气
[义] To allow air to reach the soil or roots, especially by making holes in a lawn. 使空气到达土壤或根部，尤指在草坪打孔（aerate the lawn 给草坪通气, aerate soil 土壤通气, aerated compost 通气堆肥）。动词。
[例] ① He aerated the lawn before overseeding in autumn. 秋天补播前他给草坪通气。 ② Compost needs turning to aerate the pile. 堆肥需翻堆以通气。
[搭] aerate the lawn 给草坪通气, aerate soil 土壤通气, aerated compost 通气堆肥, core aeration 取芯通气
[联] soil 土壤, lawn 草坪, compost 堆肥, root 根, oxygen 氧气, garden 花园, water 水
[记] 及物动词。aerate 与「充气（饮料）」同词；园艺义指改善土壤孔隙

**hops**
英/美 /hɒps/
n. 啤酒花
[义] The dried flowers of a climbing plant, used to flavour beer. 攀缘植物干燥花，用于啤酒调味（hop vine 啤酒花藤, hop field 啤酒花田, brewer's hops 酿酒啤酒花）。通常复数 hops。
[例] ① Hop vines climbed twenty feet up the trellis wires. 啤酒花藤沿棚架铁丝爬上二十英尺。 ② The valley was famous for fragrant hop fields. 该山谷以芳香啤酒花田闻名。
[搭] hop vine 啤酒花藤, hop field 啤酒花田, brewer's hops 酿酒啤酒花, grow hops 种啤酒花
[联] vine 藤, flower 花, beer 啤酒, climb 攀爬, harvest 收获, field 田, fragrance 芬芳
[记] 通常复数 hops。单数 hop 指单朵花或跳跃；啤酒酿造核心原料

**chicory**
英/美 /ˈtʃɪkəri/
n. 菊苣
[义] A plant with blue flowers whose root is roasted as a coffee substitute; leaves used in salads. 蓝花植物，根烘焙作咖啡代用品；叶作沙拉（chicory root 菊苣根, wild chicory 野菊苣, Belgian endive 比利时菊苣〔栽培变种〕）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Chicory bloomed blue along the gravel road in July. 七月菊苣在砾石路旁开蓝花。 ② Farmers dug chicory roots after the first frost. 初霜后农民挖收菊苣根。
[搭] chicory root 菊苣根, wild chicory 野菊苣, Belgian endive 比利时菊苣, roast chicory 烘焙菊苣
[联] root 根, flower 花, blue 蓝的, coffee 咖啡, salad 沙拉, wildflower 野花, harvest 收获
[记] 不可数/可数。新奥尔良咖啡常混菊苣；叶用品种亦称 endive、witloof（菊苣球）

**snapdragon**
英/美 /ˈsnæpdræɡən/
n. 金鱼草
[义] A garden plant with tall spikes of colourful flowers whose mouths open when squeezed. 高大穗状彩色花、挤压时「张口」的园艺植物（snapdragon spike 金鱼草穗, dwarf snapdragons 矮生金鱼草, antirrhinum 金鱼草属）。可数。
[例] ① Snapdragons lined the cottage garden path in mixed colours. 金鱼草以杂色排列村舍花园小径。 ② Children squeezed snapdragon flowers to make them snap. 孩子捏金鱼草花令其「叭」一声开合。
[搭] snapdragon spike 金鱼草穗, dwarf snapdragons 矮生金鱼草, mixed snapdragons 杂色金鱼草, grow snapdragons 种金鱼草
[联] flower 花, garden 花园, colourful 鲜艳的, spike 穗, perennial 多年生, child 孩子, cottage 村舍
[记] 可数：a snapdragon。学名 Antirrhinum；花形似龙口，故名

**zinnia**
英/美 /ˈzɪniə/
n. 百日草
[义] A garden flower with bright daisy-like blooms, popular in summer borders. 夏日花境常见的明亮雏菊状花（zinnia bed 百日草花坛, giant zinnias 巨型百日草, cut zinnias 切花百日草）。可数。
[例] ① Zinnias blazed orange and magenta until the first frost. 百日草橙红洋红怒放直至初霜。 ② Butterflies visited zinnias in the sunny front bed. 蝴蝶造访向阳前排百日草。
[搭] zinnia bed 百日草花坛, giant zinnias 巨型百日草, cut zinnias 切花百日草, grow zinnias 种百日草
[联] flower 花, garden 花园, summer 夏天, butterfly 蝴蝶, colourful 鲜艳的, annual 一年生, bloom 开花
[记] 可数：a zinnia。耐热耐旱，美国夏季花园极常见

**hosta**
英/美 /ˈhɒstə/
n. 玉簪
[义] A shade-loving garden plant with broad leaves and spikes of white or lavender flowers. 耐阴园艺植物，阔叶、穗状白或淡紫花（hosta plant 玉簪, hosta leaves 玉簪叶, blue hostas 蓝叶玉簪）。可数。
[例] ① Hostas thrived in the damp shade beneath the maple trees. 玉簪在枫树荫下湿处长得旺。 ② Slugs sometimes chewed holes in hosta leaves overnight. 蛞蝓有时一夜啃出玉簪叶上的洞。
[搭] hosta plant 玉簪, hosta leaves 玉簪叶, blue hostas 蓝叶玉簪, shade hostas 耐阴玉簪
[联] shade 荫, leaf 叶, garden 花园, perennial 多年生, slug 蛞蝓, flower 花, woodland 林地
[记] 可数：a hosta。北方阴生花园标配；叶片品种差异极大

**astilbe**
英/美 /əˈstɪlb/
n. 落新妇
[义] A shade-tolerant plant with feathery plumes of pink, red, or white flowers. 耐阴、羽状粉红白花序的园艺植物（astilbe plume 落新妇花穗, false spirea 假绣线菊, astilbe border 落新妇花境）。可数。
[例] ① Astilbes lit up the shady border with soft pink plumes. 落新妇以柔粉花穗点亮荫蔽花境。 ② She planted astilbes where grass refused to grow. 她在草长不好的荫处种落新妇。
[搭] astilbe plume 落新妇花穗, astilbe border 落新妇花境, pink astilbes 粉落新妇, shade astilbes 耐阴落新妇
[联] shade 荫, flower 花, garden 花园, perennial 多年生, pink 粉的, feathery 羽状的, border 花境
[记] 可数：an astilbe。亦称 false spirea；湿荫环境表现最佳

**salvia**
英/美 /ˈsælviə/
n. 鼠尾草属
[义] A plant of the sage family, often with spikes of red, purple, or blue flowers attractive to bees. 唇形科鼠尾草属植物，常穗状红紫蓝花、招蜂（red salvia 红鼠尾草, perennial salvia 多年生鼠尾草, culinary sage 食用鼠尾草〔近缘〕）。可数。
[例] ① Salvias bloomed long after many summer flowers had faded. 许多夏花凋谢后鼠尾草仍久开。 ② Hummingbirds visited red salvias on the patio. 蜂鸟造访露台上的红鼠尾草。
[搭] red salvia 红鼠尾草, perennial salvia 多年生鼠尾草, salvia spike 鼠尾草穗, grow salvias 种鼠尾草
[联] sage 鼠尾草, flower 花, bee 蜜蜂, garden 花园, purple 紫的, spike 穗, hummingbird 蜂鸟
[记] 可数：a salvia。食用鼠尾草（Salvia officinalis）为其中一种；观赏品种繁多

**poppy**
英/美 /ˈpɒpi/
n. 罂粟
[义] A plant with delicate papery flowers, often red or orange; some species are the source of opium. 瓣薄如纸的花，常红或橙；某些种为鸦片来源（field poppy 野罂粟, poppy seed 罂粟籽, oriental poppy 东方罂粟）。可数。
[例] ① Poppies swayed among the wheat after the rains. 雨后罂粟在麦间摇曳。 ② She grew bright oriental poppies at the front of the border. 她在花境前排种东方罂粟。
[搭] field poppy 野罂粟, poppy seed 罂粟籽, oriental poppy 东方罂粟, red poppies 红罂粟
[联] flower 花, red 红的, seed 种子, field 田, wheat 小麦, wildflower 野花, papery 薄如纸的
[记] 可数：a poppy。阵亡将士纪念日（Remembrance Day）佩戴罂粟花纪念阵亡；食用罂粟籽与鸦片罂粟不同种

**cosmos**
英/美 /ˈkɒzmɒs/
n. 波斯菊
[义] A tall garden flower with daisy-like blooms in pink, white, or purple, blooming late into autumn. 高大园艺花，雏菊状粉白紫花，秋末仍开（cosmos flower 波斯菊, cosmos bed 波斯菊床, chocolate cosmos 巧克力色波斯菊）。可数。
[例] ① Cosmos leaned over the fence in a tangle of slender stems. 波斯菊细茎交错、倾过篱笆。 ② Butterflies fed on cosmos until the first frost. 初霜前蝴蝶以波斯菊为食。
[搭] cosmos flower 波斯菊, cosmos bed 波斯菊床, chocolate cosmos 巧克力色波斯菊, grow cosmos 种波斯菊
[联] flower 花, butterfly 蝴蝶, autumn 秋天, daisy 雏菊状, garden 花园, tall 高的, annual 一年生
[记] 可数：a cosmos。与宇宙 cosmos 同形异义；自播能力强

**phlox**
英/美 /flɒks/
n. 福禄考
[义] A garden plant with clusters of fragrant pink, purple, or white flowers. 簇生芳香粉紫白花的园艺植物（garden phlox 园福禄考, creeping phlox 匍匐福禄考, phlox border 福禄考花境）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Phlox perfumed the evening air on the cottage porch. 村舍门廊上福禄考让傍晚空气香气弥漫。 ② Creeping phlox covered the rocky slope in spring pink. 匍匐福禄考以春粉覆盖岩坡。
[搭] garden phlox 园福禄考, creeping phlox 匍匐福禄考, phlox border 福禄考花境, fragrant phlox 香福禄考
[联] flower 花, fragrance 芬芳, garden 花园, pink 粉的, perennial 多年生, cluster 花簇, spring 春天
[记] 不可数/可数。creeping phlox 铺地；garden phlox 高杆夏花

**impatiens**
英/美 /ɪmˈpeɪʃənz/
n. 凤仙花
[义] A shade-loving plant with flat colourful flowers, widely used in bedding and pots. 耐阴、扁瓣彩色花，广泛用于花坛与盆栽（busy Lizzie 凤仙花〔俗名〕, impatiens bed 凤仙花床, double impatiens 重瓣凤仙）。可数/通常复数。
[例] ① Impatiens filled the shady planters under the porch roof. 凤仙花铺满门廊屋顶下的荫蔽花盆。 ② She replaced impatiens after mildew hit the beds. 霉病侵袭后她换种了凤仙花。
[搭] busy Lizzie 凤仙花, impatiens bed 凤仙花床, double impatiens 重瓣凤仙, shade impatiens 耐阴凤仙
[联] shade 荫, flower 花, garden 花园, pot 盆, colourful 鲜艳的, bedding 花坛, annual 一年生
[记] 可数/复数。busy Lizzie 为英式俗名；近年部分品种受霜霉病（downy mildew）影响

**rhizome**
英/美 /ˈraɪzəʊm/
n. 根茎
[义] A horizontal underground stem that sends out roots and shoots, as in ginger, iris, or bamboo. 地下横向茎，出根与枝，如姜、鸢尾、竹（ginger rhizome 姜根茎, iris rhizomes 鸢尾根茎, spreading rhizomes 蔓延根茎）。可数。
[例] ① Bamboo spread through aggressive underground rhizomes. 竹通过地下蔓延根茎扩散。 ② She divided iris clumps by cutting the rhizomes apart. 她切分鸢尾根茎以分株。
[搭] ginger rhizome 姜根茎, iris rhizomes 鸢尾根茎, spreading rhizomes 蔓延根茎, rhizome division 根茎分株
[联] root 根, stem 茎, ginger 姜, iris 鸢尾, bamboo 竹, underground 地下的, spread 蔓延
[记] 可数：a rhizome。rhiz-（根）+ -ome；与 tuber、bulb 区分：rhizome 为茎

**tuber**
英/美 /ˈtjuːbə(r)/
n. 块茎
[义] A swollen underground stem or root used for food storage, as in potato or sweet potato. 膨大地下茎或根，贮养，如马铃薯、甘薯（potato tuber 马铃薯块茎, tuber crop 块茎作物, tuber formation 块茎形成）。可数。
[例] ① Potato tubers formed as stolons swelled underground. 匍匐茎在地下膨大形成马铃薯块茎。 ② Farmers stored tubers in cool dark sheds through winter. 农民冬藏块茎于凉暗棚。
[搭] potato tuber 马铃薯块茎, tuber crop 块茎作物, tuber formation 块茎形成, edible tubers 食用块茎
[联] potato 马铃薯, root 根, stem 茎, crop 作物, harvest 收获, underground 地下的, storage 贮藏
[记] 可数：a tuber。与 bulb（鳞茎）、rhizome（根茎）区分；块茎作物为重要粮食类

**herbaceous**
英/美 /hɜːˈbeɪʃəs/
adj. 草本的
[义] (Of plants) soft and green, not woody; dying back to the ground in winter. （植物）柔软非木质；冬枯于地（herbaceous border 草本花境, herbaceous plants 草本植物, herbaceous perennial 草本多年生）。只作形容词。
[例] ① The herbaceous border burst into colour each June. 草本花境每年六月色彩爆发。 ② Herbaceous plants died back, leaving bare soil in winter. 草本植物冬枯，留裸土。
[搭] herbaceous border 草本花境, herbaceous plants 草本植物, herbaceous perennial 草本多年生, herbaceous layer 草本层
[联] perennial 多年生, shrub 灌木, woody 木质的, garden 花园, border 花境, soft 柔软的, die back 冬枯
[记] 只作形容词。与 woody（木本）相对；herbaceous border 为英式花园经典概念

**oregano**
英/美 /əˈreɡənəʊ/
n. 牛至
[义] A Mediterranean herb with small aromatic leaves, used especially in Italian cooking. 地中海香草，小叶芳香，尤用于意大利菜（fresh oregano 新鲜牛至, oregano plant 牛至植株, wild oregano 野牛至）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Oregano sprawled over the sunny stone wall of the herb garden. 牛至蔓延于香草园向阳石墙。 ② She dried oregano bundles from the summer harvest. 她将夏收牛至扎束晾干。
[搭] fresh oregano 新鲜牛至, oregano plant 牛至植株, wild oregano 野牛至, dried oregano 干牛至
[联] herb 香草, marjoram 马郁兰, Mediterranean 地中海, leaf 叶, cooking 烹饪, fragrance 芬芳, garden 花园
[记] 不可数/可数。与 marjoram 近缘易混；披萨与意式料理标志香料

**marjoram**
英/美 /ˈmɑːdʒərəm/
n. 马郁兰
[义] A sweet-scented herb related to oregano, used in cooking and teas. 甜香香草，与牛至近缘，用于烹饪与茶饮（sweet marjoram 甜马郁兰, pot marjoram 盆栽马郁兰, fresh marjoram 新鲜马郁兰）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Marjoram filled the greenhouse with a gentle sweet scent. 马郁兰让温室充满柔甜香气。 ② She added fresh marjoram to the bean stew. 她在豆炖菜里加新鲜马郁兰。
[搭] sweet marjoram 甜马郁兰, pot marjoram 盆栽马郁兰, fresh marjoram 新鲜马郁兰, dried marjoram 干马郁兰
[联] oregano 牛至, herb 香草, thyme 百里香, cooking 烹饪, fragrance 芬芳, garden 花园, sweet 甜的
[记] 不可数/可数。sweet marjoram 与 oregano 风味近但较温和

**yarrow**
英/美 /ˈjærəʊ/
n. 蓍草
[义] A hardy wildflower with flat clusters of tiny white or pink flowers and feathery leaves. 耐寒野花，扁平簇生细小白或粉花、羽状叶（wild yarrow 野蓍草, yarrow flowers 蓍草花, common yarrow 欧蓍草）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Yarrow grew among the grasses on the dry hillside. 蓍草在干燥山坡草间生长。 ② Herbalists once used yarrow leaves in traditional remedies. 草药师曾以蓍草叶入传统方剂。
[搭] wild yarrow 野蓍草, yarrow flowers 蓍草花, common yarrow 欧蓍草, feathery leaves 羽状叶
[联] wildflower 野花, herb 药草, meadow 草地, hardy 耐寒的, cluster 花簇, leaf 叶, traditional 传统的
[记] 不可数/可数。欧蓍草；传说阿喀琉斯用其止血——勿另写专科属名 junk 条

**rhubarb**
英/美 /ˈruːbɑːb/
n. 大黄
[义] A plant with thick red stalks eaten as fruit and large poisonous leaves. 厚红茎作水果食、大叶有毒的植物（rhubarb crown 大黄株丛, rhubarb patch 大黄地, forced rhubarb 催软大黄）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Rhubarb stalks were ready to pull once they reached a foot long. 大黄茎长至一英尺即可拔收。 ② Never eat rhubarb leaves—they contain oxalic acid. 切勿食大黄叶——含草酸。
[搭] rhubarb crown 大黄株丛, rhubarb patch 大黄地, forced rhubarb 催软大黄, rhubarb stalk 大黄茎
[联] stalk 茎, leaf 叶, poisonous 有毒的, garden 花园, spring 春天, pie 派, harvest 收获
[记] 不可数/可数。茎作大黄派；叶有毒。forced rhubarb 为暗室催软茎

**watermelon**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtəmelən/
n. 西瓜
[义] A large oval fruit with green skin and sweet red flesh; the trailing plant that produces it. 大型椭圆绿皮甜红瓤果；匍匐结果的植株（watermelon vine 西瓜藤, watermelon patch 西瓜地, seedless watermelon 无籽西瓜）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Watermelon vines sprawled across the sandy field in July. 七月西瓜藤爬满沙质田地。 ② Heat and sun swelled the melons to harvest size. 暑阳把瓜催至可收大小。
[搭] watermelon vine 西瓜藤, watermelon patch 西瓜地, seedless watermelon 无籽西瓜, grow watermelons 种西瓜
[联] melon 瓜, vine 藤, fruit 果实, summer 夏天, field 田, sweet 甜的, harvest 收获
[记] 可数/不可数。本章取植株/种植义；切块食用在食物章

**melon**
英/美 /ˈmelən/
n. 甜瓜；瓜类
[义] A sweet juicy fruit of several types, including cantaloupe and honeydew; the plant that bears it. 多种类型的甜多汁水果，含哈密瓜、蜜瓜；结果植株（melon patch 瓜地, melon vine 瓜藤, ripe melons 熟瓜）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Melon flowers opened in the morning and closed by afternoon. 甜瓜花晨开午合。 ② She thinned melon fruits to two per vine for size. 她疏果至每藤两颗以求个大。
[搭] melon patch 瓜地, melon vine 瓜藤, ripe melons 熟瓜, cantaloupe 哈密瓜, honeydew 蜜瓜
[联] fruit 果实, vine 藤, watermelon 西瓜, summer 夏天, sweet 甜的, harvest 收获, field 田
[记] 可数/不可数。melon 总称；具体品种名在食物章可交叉

**elder**
英/美 /ˈeldə(r)/
n. 接骨木
[义] A shrub or small tree with clusters of white flowers and dark purple berries. 灌木或小乔木，簇生白花与深紫浆果（elder tree 接骨木, elder flowers 接骨木花, elderberries 接骨木果）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Elder flowers foamed white along the lane in late spring. 晚春接骨木花白如沫排列小路旁。 ② Birds stripped elderberries from the branches in autumn. 秋天鸟啄光枝上接骨木果。
[搭] elder tree 接骨木, elder flowers 接骨木花, elderberries 接骨木果, wild elder 野接骨木
[联] shrub 灌木, berry 浆果, flower 花, white 白的, bird 鸟, hedge 树篱, syrup 糖浆
[记] 可数/不可数。接骨木花可制糖浆与酒；浆果需煮熟食用，食物章可交叉

**dogwood**
英/美 /ˈdɒgwʊd/
n. 山茱萸
[义] A tree or shrub with showy white or pink bracts that look like petals in spring. 春开醒目白或粉苞片、似花瓣的乔木或灌木（flowering dogwood 山茱萸, dogwood blossom 山茱萸花, pink dogwood 粉花山茱萸）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Dogwood blossoms lit the understory each April. 每年四月山茱萸花点亮林下。 ② The trail passed beneath flowering dogwoods in pink and white. 小径穿过粉白开花的山茱萸。
[搭] flowering dogwood 山茱萸, dogwood blossom 山茱萸花, pink dogwood 粉花山茱萸, dogwood tree 山茱萸树
[联] tree 树, blossom 花, spring 春天, white 白的, pink 粉的, woodland 林地, shrub 灌木
[记] 可数/不可数。所见「花瓣」实为苞片（bracts）；美国东南部春季标志树种

**juniper**
英/美 /ˈdʒuːnɪpə(r)/
n. 杜松
[义] An evergreen shrub or tree with needle-like or scale-like leaves and berry-like cones. 常绿灌木或乔木，针状或鳞状叶、浆果状球果（juniper bush 杜松丛, juniper berries 杜松子, common juniper 普通杜松）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Juniper covered the dry ridge in low grey-green mats. 杜松以灰绿低毯覆盖干燥山脊。 ② Gin is flavoured with juniper berries. 金酒以杜松子调味。
[搭] juniper bush 杜松丛, juniper berries 杜松子, common juniper 普通杜松, juniper hedge 杜松树篱
[联] evergreen 常绿, shrub 灌木, berry 浆果, cone 球果, gin 金酒, needle 针叶, dry 干旱的
[记] 可数/不可数。杜松子为金酒核心风味；浆果亦作料理香料

**goldenrod**
英/美 /ˈɡəʊldənrɒd/
n. 一枝黄花
[义] A wild plant with tall spikes of small yellow flowers in late summer. 晚夏高穗状小黄花的野生植物（goldenrod field 一枝黄花田, wild goldenrod 野一枝黄花, goldenrod pollen 一枝黄花花粉）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Goldenrod turned the meadow gold before autumn. 秋前一枝黄花把草地染金。 ② Bees crowded goldenrod blooms on warm September days. 九月暖日蜜蜂拥满一枝黄花。
[搭] goldenrod field 一枝黄花田, wild goldenrod 野一枝黄花, goldenrod pollen 一枝黄花花粉, late-summer goldenrod 晚夏一枝黄花
[联] wildflower 野花, yellow 黄的, meadow 草地, bee 蜜蜂, autumn 秋天, pollen 花粉, tall 高的
[记] 不可数/可数。常被误指为 hay fever 主因（实为 ragweed）；生态上重要蜜源

**milkweed**
英/美 /ˈmɪkwiːd/
n. 马利筋
[义] A North American plant with milky sap and pods of silky seeds; essential food for monarch caterpillars. 北美植物，乳状汁液、丝絮种荚；帝王蝶幼虫必需食源（common milkweed 普通马利筋, milkweed pod 马利筋荚, monarch milkweed 帝王蝶马利筋）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Milkweed pods split open, releasing seeds on silky parachutes. 马利筋荚裂，丝絮伞携种飞出。 ② Gardeners planted milkweed to support monarch butterflies. 园丁种马利筋以支持帝王蝶。
[搭] common milkweed 普通马利筋, milkweed pod 马利筋荚, monarch milkweed 帝王蝶马利筋, plant milkweed 种马利筋
[联] monarch 帝王蝶, butterfly 蝴蝶, pod 荚, seed 种子, wildflower 野花, sap 汁液, caterpillar 毛虫
[记] 不可数/可数。monarch 迁徙依赖马利筋；全株乳胶有毒，勿误食

**bracken**
英/美 /ˈbrækən/
n. 欧蕨；大型蕨
[义] A large coarse fern common on hillsides and woodland edges. 常见于山坡与林缘的大型粗蕨（bracken hillside 欧蕨山坡, bracken fronds 欧蕨羽叶, bracken cover 欧蕨覆盖）。不可数。
[例] ① Bracken spread across the abandoned pasture each summer. 欧蕨每年夏天蔓延废弃牧场。 ② Walkers brushed through tall bracken fronds on the path. 行人拨过高欧蕨羽叶过小径。
[搭] bracken hillside 欧蕨山坡, bracken fronds 欧蕨羽叶, bracken cover 欧蕨覆盖, bracken clearing 清理欧蕨
[联] fern 蕨, frond 羽叶, hillside 山坡, woodland 林地, spread 蔓延, coarse 粗大的, path 小径
[记] 不可数。欧蕨可占据弱势林地；部分品种幼嫩卷芽可食，须识种

**corm**
英/美 /kɔːm/
n. 球茎（短缩茎）
[义] A solid swollen underground stem base, as in crocus or gladiolus. 实心膨大地下茎基，如番红花、唐菖蒲（crocus corm 番红球球茎, corm planting 球茎种植, store corms 贮藏球茎）。可数。
[例] ① She planted crocus corms in autumn for spring colour. 她秋植番红球球茎以换春色。 ② Corms multiplied underground over several seasons. 球茎数季在地下增殖。
[搭] crocus corm 番红球球茎, corm planting 球茎种植, store corms 贮藏球茎, gladiolus corms 唐菖蒲球茎
[联] crocus 番红花, bulb 鳞茎, rhizome 根茎, underground 地下的, spring 春天, plant 种植, storage 贮藏
[记] 可数：a corm。与 bulb、rhizome、tuber 并为四类地下器官；唐菖蒲常用 corm 繁殖

**sepal**
英/美 /ˈsiːpl/
n. 萼片
[义] Any of the green leaf-like parts that protect a flower bud. 保护花蕾的绿色叶状部分之一（green sepals 绿萼片, sepal and petal 萼片与花瓣, calyx of sepals 萼片组成的花萼）。可数/通常复数 sepals。
[例] ① Sepals opened to reveal the petals inside. 萼片展开露出内部花瓣。 ② She counted sepals and petals on the classroom specimen. 她在课堂标本上数萼片与花瓣。
[搭] green sepals 绿萼片, sepal and petal 萼片与花瓣, outer sepals 外萼片, sepal colour 萼片色
[联] petal 花瓣, bud 花蕾, flower 花, stamen 雄蕊, pistil 雌蕊, green 绿的, botany 植物学
[记] 可数/复数 sepals。sepals 总称 calyx（花萼）；与 petals 成对记忆

**watercress**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtəkres/
n. 西洋菜
[义] A peppery green plant growing in or near running water, eaten in salads. 生长于流水或近水处、辛辣绿叶、作沙拉食用的植物（wild watercress 野西洋菜, watercress bed 西洋菜床, fresh watercress 新鲜西洋菜）。不可数。
[例] ① Watercress grew in the clear stream behind the farm. 农场后清溪中长西洋菜。 ② She harvested watercress with scissors above the waterline. 她在水线以上用剪刀采收西洋菜。
[搭] wild watercress 野西洋菜, watercress bed 西洋菜床, fresh watercress 新鲜西洋菜, peppery leaves 辛辣叶
[联] herb 香草, salad 沙拉, stream 溪流, water 水, leaf 叶, peppery 辛辣的, wild 野生的
[记] 不可数。需洁净流水种植；营养密度高，食材义在食物章

**arugula**
英/美 /əˈruːɡjələ/
n. 芝麻菜
[义] (AmE) A salad green with peppery leaves; (BrE) rocket. 美式指辛辣叶沙拉菜；英式称 rocket（arugula bed 芝麻菜床, baby arugula 嫩芝麻菜, wild arugula 野芝麻菜）。不可数。
[例] ① Arugula bolted quickly once the weather turned hot. 天热后芝麻菜很快抽薹。 ② She sowed arugula between rows of slower tomatoes. 她在生长较慢的番茄行间播芝麻菜。
[搭] arugula bed 芝麻菜床, baby arugula 嫩芝麻菜, wild arugula 野芝麻菜, grow arugula 种芝麻菜
[联] salad 沙拉, leaf 叶, peppery 辛辣的, garden 花园, rocket 芝麻菜（英式）, bolt 抽薹, green 绿叶
[记] 不可数。美式 arugula = 英式 rocket；意大利料理常见

**radicchio**
英/美 /rəˈdɪkiəʊ/
n. 菊苣（红叶）
[义] A red-leaved variety of chicory, often grilled or used in salads. 红叶菊苣品种，常烤食或作沙拉（radicchio head 菊苣球, grilled radicchio 烤菊苣, radicchio bed 菊苣床）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Radicchio formed tight red heads in the cool autumn beds. 凉秋畦里菊苣结成紧实红球。 ② She quartered radicchio for the winter salad bowl. 她把菊苣切四块入冬沙拉碗。
[搭] radicchio head 菊苣球, grilled radicchio 烤菊苣, radicchio bed 菊苣床, grow radicchio 种菊苣
[联] chicory 菊苣, leaf 叶, salad 沙拉, red 红的, garden 花园, autumn 秋天, vegetable 蔬菜
[记] 不可数/可数。与 endive、chicory 同科；苦甜并存，常见于意式料理

**endive**
英/美 /ˈendaɪv/
n. 苦菊；菊苣叶
[义] A salad plant with bitter curly leaves; also forced blanched heads (Belgian endive). 苦卷叶沙拉菜；亦指催白球状头（比利时菊苣）（curly endive 卷叶苦菊, Belgian endive 比利时菊苣, endive bed 苦菊床）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Endive leaves added bitterness to the mixed green salad. 苦菊叶为杂色沙拉增苦。 ② Belgian endive was forced in dark sheds for pale crisp heads. 比利时菊苣在暗棚催白成淡黄脆球。
[搭] curly endive 卷叶苦菊, Belgian endive 比利时菊苣, endive bed 苦菊床, forced endive 催白菊苣
[联] chicory 菊苣, radicchio 红叶菊苣, salad 沙拉, leaf 叶, bitter 苦的, garden 花园, blanch 催白
[记] 不可数/可数。curly endive 与 escarole 近；Belgian endive 即 witloof（催白菊苣球）

**Brussels sprout**
英/美 /ˌbrʌslz ˈspraʊt/
n. 抱子甘蓝
[义] A small cabbage-like vegetable that grows in clusters along a tall stalk. 沿高茎簇生的小型甘蓝类蔬菜（Brussels sprout stalk 抱子甘蓝茎, sprout tops 顶芽, baby sprouts 小抱子甘蓝）。可数/通常复数 Brussels sprouts。
[例] ① Brussels sprouts ripened from the bottom of the stalk upward. 抱子甘蓝自茎底向上成熟。 ② Frost sweetened the sprouts left on the plants in December. 十二月留株的抱子甘蓝经霜更甜。
[搭] Brussels sprout stalk 抱子甘蓝茎, sprout tops 顶芽, baby sprouts 小抱子甘蓝, grow Brussels sprouts 种抱子甘蓝
[联] cabbage 甘蓝, kale 羽衣甘蓝, stalk 茎, vegetable 蔬菜, garden 花园, frost 霜, harvest 收获
[记] 可数/复数。名虽带 Brussels，泛指该蔬菜；顶芽 sprout tops 亦可食

**parsnip**
英/美 /ˈpɑːsnɪp/
n. 欧洲防风
[义] A long pale root vegetable related to the carrot, sweeter after frost. 与胡萝卜近缘的长淡色根菜，经霜更甜（parsnip root 防风根, parsnip bed 防风床, wild parsnip 野防风）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Parsnips were left in the ground until after the first hard frost. 防风留在地里直至初霜后。 ② She dug parsnip roots as long as her forearm. 她挖出与前臂等长的防风根。
[搭] parsnip root 防风根, parsnip bed 防风床, wild parsnip 野防风, grow parsnips 种防风
[联] carrot 胡萝卜, root 根, vegetable 蔬菜, frost 霜, garden 花园, harvest 收获, sweet 甜的
[记] 可数/不可数。wild parsnip 接触汁液可致光敏性皮炎，勿与食用种混淆

**rutabaga**
英/美 /ˌruːtəˈbeɪɡə/
n. 芜菁甘蓝
[义] (AmE) A large yellow root vegetable; (BrE) swede. 美式指大型黄根菜；英式 swede（rutabaga root 芜菁甘蓝根, rutabaga field 芜菁甘蓝田）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Rutabagas stored well in the root cellar through winter. 芜菁甘蓝在根窖冬藏良好。 ② She planted rutabagas where the heavy clay had defeated carrots. 她在重黏土难种胡萝卜处改种芜菁甘蓝。
[搭] rutabaga root 芜菁甘蓝根, rutabaga field 芜菁甘蓝田, grow rutabagas 种芜菁甘蓝, swede 芜菁甘蓝（英式）
[联] turnip 芜菁, root 根, vegetable 蔬菜, crop 作物, storage 贮藏, frost 霜, field 田
[记] 可数/不可数。美式 rutabaga = 英式 swede；与 turnip 近缘但更大更黄

**squash**
英/美 /skwɒʃ/
n. 南瓜类；西葫芦类
[义] A plant of the gourd family with edible fruit; the fruit itself in gardening contexts. 葫芦科可食果实的植株；园艺语境中亦指其果实（summer squash 夏南瓜, winter squash 冬南瓜, squash vine 南瓜藤）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Squash vines sprawled across the compost-rich mound. 南瓜藤爬满富堆肥土丘。 ② Powdery mildew sometimes hit squash leaves in humid August. 八月潮湿时南瓜叶有时染白粉病。
[搭] summer squash 夏南瓜, winter squash 冬南瓜, squash vine 南瓜藤, squash blossom 南瓜花
[联] pumpkin 南瓜, zucchini 西葫芦, vine 藤, gourd 葫芦, garden 花园, harvest 收获, mildew 霉病
[记] 不可数/可数。summer squash 嫩果连皮食；winter squash 硬皮贮藏；与西葫芦同属南瓜属

**mustard**
英/美 /ˈmʌstəd/
n. 芥菜
[义] A plant with yellow flowers whose seeds are ground for the condiment; also the young leaves eaten as greens. 开黄花、籽磨作调味酱的植物；嫩叶亦作绿叶菜（mustard greens 芥菜叶, field mustard 野芥, mustard seed 芥菜籽）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Mustard yellowed the field edges in early spring. 早春野芥把田边染黄。 ② She picked mustard greens before the plants bolted. 抽薹前她采收芥菜叶。
[搭] mustard greens 芥菜叶, field mustard 野芥, mustard seed 芥菜籽, grow mustard 种芥菜
[联] seed 种子, yellow 黄的, field 田, green 绿叶, crop 作物, condiment 调味品, bolt 抽薹
[记] 不可数/可数。本章取植株/种植义；酱状 mustard 在食物章

**bay**
英/美 /beɪ/
n. 月桂
[义] An evergreen tree or shrub whose aromatic leaves are used in cooking (bay laurel). 常绿乔木或灌木，芳香叶用于烹饪（月桂）（bay tree 月桂树, bay leaves 月桂叶, sweet bay 甜月桂）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① A potted bay tree stood by the kitchen door all winter. 盆栽月桂整冬立厨房门旁。 ② She picked fresh bay leaves before the first frost. 初霜前她摘新鲜月桂叶。
[搭] bay tree 月桂树, bay leaves 月桂叶, sweet bay 甜月桂, potted bay 盆栽月桂
[联] evergreen 常绿, herb 香草, leaf 叶, shrub 灌木, cooking 烹饪, aromatic 芳香的, laurel 月桂
[记] 可数/不可数。bay leaves 干叶久煮增香；勿与 cherry bay 等观赏种混谈烹饪用法

**pear**
英/美 /peə(r)/
n. 梨；梨树
[义] A sweet fruit that is narrow at the top and wider at the bottom; the tree that bears it. 上窄下宽的甜果；结果之树（pear tree 梨树, pear orchard 梨园, pear blossom 梨花）。可数。
[例] ① Pear blossoms drifted white across the orchard in April. 四月梨花白飘满园。 ② She thinned young pears to two per cluster for size. 她疏果至每簇两颗以求个大。
[搭] pear tree 梨树, pear orchard 梨园, pear blossom 梨花, Asian pear 亚洲梨
[联] orchard 果园, tree 树, fruit 果实, blossom 花, apple 苹果, harvest 收获
[记] 可数：a pear。梨与苹果同科；食材义在食物章

**orange**
英/美 /ˈɒrɪndʒ/
n. 橙；橙树
[义] A round citrus fruit with thick skin; the evergreen tree that produces it. 厚皮圆形柑橘；产果之常绿树（orange tree 橙树, orange grove 橙园, orange blossom 橙花）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Orange trees lined the irrigation channels in the valley. 橙树排列于山谷灌溉渠旁。 ② The scent of orange blossom filled the grove in March. 三月橙园弥漫橙花香气。
[搭] orange tree 橙树, orange grove 橙园, orange blossom 橙花, bitter orange 苦橙
[联] citrus 柑橘, grove 果园, tree 树, fruit 果实, blossom 花, evergreen 常绿, harvest 收获
[记] 可数/不可数。orange blossom 亦指橙花婚礼传统；具体品种（navel, blood orange）食物章可交叉

**almond**
英/美 /ˈɑːmənd/
n. 扁桃；扁桃树
[义] A hard-shelled oval nut; the tree that produces it, blooming early in spring. 硬壳椭圆形坚果；早春开花的果树（almond tree 扁桃树, almond orchard 扁桃园, almond blossom 扁桃花）。可数。
[例] ① Almond orchards turned the hills pink each February. 每年二月扁桃园把山染粉。 ② Bees worked the almond blossoms during a brief spring window. 蜜蜂在短暂春窗期采扁桃花。
[搭] almond tree 扁桃树, almond orchard 扁桃园, almond blossom 扁桃花, bitter almond 苦扁桃
[联] nut 坚果, tree 树, orchard 果园, blossom 花, bee 蜜蜂, spring 春天, harvest 收获
[记] 可数：an almond。加州扁桃种植规模极大；本章取树/种植义

**gladiolus**
英/美 /ˌɡlædiˈəʊləs/
n. 唐菖蒲
[义] A garden plant with tall spikes of colourful flowers, grown from corms. 以球茎栽培、高穗彩色花的园艺植物（gladiolus spike 唐菖蒲穗, gladiolus corm 唐菖蒲球茎, cut gladioli 切花唐菖蒲）。可数/通常复数 gladioli。
[例] ① Gladioli lined the fence in rows of red and yellow spikes. 唐菖蒲红黄花穗成排立篱笆旁。 ② She planted gladiolus corms after the last frost. 末霜后她种下唐菖蒲球茎。
[搭] gladiolus spike 唐菖蒲穗, gladiolus corm 唐菖蒲球茎, cut gladioli 切花唐菖蒲, grow gladioli 种唐菖蒲
[联] corm 球茎, flower 花, spike 穗, garden 花园, colourful 鲜艳的, summer 夏天, bulb 球茎
[记] 可数/复数 gladioli。单数 gladiolus；与 corm 条目交叉记忆

**sedum**
英/美 /ˈsiːdəm/
n. 景天
[义] A succulent plant with fleshy leaves, often used in rock gardens and green roofs. 多肉叶植物，常用于岩石园与绿屋顶（sedum roof 景天屋顶, stonecrop 景天〔俗名〕, creeping sedum 匍匐景天）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Sedum spread in mats over the dry stone wall. 景天在干石墙上铺成毯。 ② The green roof was planted with hardy sedum varieties. 绿屋顶种耐寒景天品种。
[搭] sedum roof 景天屋顶, stonecrop 景天, creeping sedum 匍匐景天, sedum mat 景天毯
[联] succulent 多肉, leaf 叶, rock garden 岩石园, drought 干旱, hardy 耐寒的, roof 屋顶, garden 花园
[记] 不可数/可数。亦称 stonecrop；低维护、耐旱，生态屋顶常见

**ginkgo**
英/美 /ˈɡɪŋkəʊ/
n. 银杏
[义] A tall tree with fan-shaped leaves that turn gold in autumn; a living fossil species. 扇形叶、秋叶金黄的乔木；活化石树种（ginkgo tree 银杏树, ginkgo leaf 银杏叶, maidenhair tree 银杏〔别名〕）。可数。
[例] ① Ginkgo leaves carpeted the sidewalk in late November. 十一月下旬银杏叶铺满人行道。 ② City planners planted ginkgos for their pollution tolerance. 城市规划者因抗污染而种银杏。
[搭] ginkgo tree 银杏树, ginkgo leaf 银杏叶, maidenhair tree 银杏, ginkgo biloba 银杏（学名）
[联] tree 树, leaf 叶, autumn 秋天, fossil 化石, fan-shaped 扇形的, urban 城市的, gold 金黄
[记] 可数：a ginkgo。雌性树落果气味强烈；ginkgo biloba 为常用学名

**boxwood**
英/美 /ˈbɒkswʊd/
n. 黄杨
[义] A slow-growing evergreen shrub with small leaves, used for hedges and topiary. 慢生常绿小灌木，用于树篱与造型修剪（boxwood hedge 黄杨树篱, box hedge 黄杨绿篱, topiary boxwood 造型黄杨）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Boxwood hedges framed the formal garden paths. 黄杨树篱框住规整园径。 ② She clipped boxwood into low spheres along the border. 她把边沿黄杨剪成低球。
[搭] boxwood hedge 黄杨树篱, box hedge 黄杨绿篱, topiary boxwood 造型黄杨, clip boxwood 修剪黄杨
[联] hedge 树篱, evergreen 常绿, shrub 灌木, topiary 造型修剪, garden 花园, formal 规整的, clip 修剪
[记] 不可数/可数。box 亦指黄杨木材；温带规整式花园经典绿篱材料

**clematis**
英/美 /ˈklemətɪs/
n. 铁线莲
[义] A climbing plant with large colourful flowers, often trained on trellises and fences. 攀缘、大朵彩色花，常引于棚架与篱笆（clematis vine 铁线莲藤, climbing clematis 攀缘铁线莲, clematis trellis 铁线莲棚架）。可数。
[例] ① Clematis climbed the arbor in waves of purple bloom. 铁线莲紫浪般爬上花架。 ② She pruned clematis hard after the first spring flowers faded. 春花谢后她强剪铁线莲。
[搭] clematis vine 铁线莲藤, climbing clematis 攀缘铁线莲, clematis trellis 铁线莲棚架, prune clematis 修剪铁线莲
[联] vine 藤, climb 攀爬, trellis 棚架, flower 花, garden 花园, purple 紫的, prune 修剪
[记] 可数：a clematis。不同组品种修剪时机不同；园艺界常称「攀缘女王」

**snowdrop**
英/美 /ˈsnəʊdrɒp/
n. 雪花莲
[义] A small white flower that blooms in late winter or early spring, often through snow. 晚冬或早春开花的小型白花，常破雪而出（snowdrop bulb 雪花莲鳞茎, wild snowdrops 野雪花莲, snowdrop carpet 雪花莲地毯）。可数/通常复数 snowdrops。
[例] ① Snowdrops pushed through the frozen soil in February. 二月雪花莲破土而出。 ② A carpet of snowdrops lined the woodland path. 林下小径铺成雪花莲地毯。
[搭] snowdrop bulb 雪花莲鳞茎, wild snowdrops 野雪花莲, snowdrop carpet 雪花莲地毯, plant snowdrops 种雪花莲
[联] bulb 鳞茎, spring 春天, white 白的, woodland 林地, winter 冬天, flower 花, crocus 番红花
[记] 可数/复数 snowdrops。与 crocus、daffodil 并为早春标志；学名为 Galanthus，正文不写专科 junk

**mistletoe**
英/美 /ˈmɪsltəʊ/
n. 槲寄生
[义] A plant that grows on trees and stays green in winter; used in Christmas decorations. 寄生于树上、冬常绿的植物；用于圣诞装饰（mistletoe sprig 槲寄生小枝, mistletoe ball 槲寄生球, oak mistletoe 栎槲寄生）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Mistletoe clumped in the crown of the old apple tree. 老苹果树树冠上簇生槲寄生。 ② They hung mistletoe above the doorway for the holidays. 节日里他们把槲寄生悬于门楣。
[搭] mistletoe sprig 槲寄生小枝, mistletoe ball 槲寄生球, oak mistletoe 栎槲寄生, hang mistletoe 挂槲寄生
[联] parasite 寄生, evergreen 常绿, Christmas 圣诞, tree 树, sprig 小枝, tradition 传统, berry 浆果
[记] 不可数/可数。圣诞槲寄生下接吻习俗；半寄生，吸取宿主树液

**ragweed**
英/美 /ˈræɡwiːd/
n. 豚草
[义] A North American weed whose pollen is a major cause of hay fever. 北美杂草，花粉为花粉症主要致敏原之一（common ragweed 普通豚草, ragweed pollen 豚草花粉, giant ragweed 巨型豚草）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Ragweed thrived in disturbed soil along the road verge. 豚草在路缘扰动土中疯长。 ② Allergy sufferers dread ragweed season in late summer. 过敏者在晚夏惧怕豚草季。
[搭] common ragweed 普通豚草, ragweed pollen 豚草花粉, giant ragweed 巨型豚草, ragweed season 豚草季
[联] pollen 花粉, weed 杂草, allergy 过敏, hay fever 花粉症, goldenrod 一枝黄花, meadow 草地, summer 夏天
[记] 不可数/可数。常被误指 goldenrod 致敏；豚草才是北美花粉症主因

**turf**
英/美 /tɜːf/
n. 草皮
[义] Grass and the earth it grows in, cut for lawns or sports fields; also the surface layer of grass. 连土割下的草皮，用于草坪或运动场；亦指草表层（lay turf 铺草皮, turf grass 草坪草, roll of turf 草皮卷）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① They rolled fresh turf across the bare backyard in spring. 春天他们把新草皮铺过裸后院。 ② The soccer field was rebuilt with drainage and new turf. 足球场翻修排水并换新草皮。
[搭] lay turf 铺草皮, turf grass 草坪草, roll of turf 草皮卷, artificial turf 人造草皮
[联] grass 草, lawn 草坪, sod 草皮, garden 花园, field 场地, roll 卷, green 绿的
[记] 不可数/可数。turf 口语亦指「地盘、势力范围」——与植物义同形

**hay**
英/美 /heɪ/
n. 干草
[义] Grass that has been cut and dried for animal feed. 割下晒干的草，作牲畜饲料（make hay 晒制干草, hay bale 干草捆, hay field 干草田）。不可数。
[例] ① Farmers cut hay when the forecast promised three dry days. 预报连晴三天时农民割草晒干草。 ② The barn smelled of sweet hay and warm animals. 谷仓里有甜干草与暖牲畜的气味。
[搭] make hay 晒制干草, hay bale 干草捆, hay field 干草田, hay fever 花粉症（另义）
[联] grass 草, field 田, barn 谷仓, bale 捆, harvest 收获, meadow 草地, livestock 牲畜
[记] 不可数。make hay while the sun shines 谚「趁热打铁」；hay fever 与干草本身无必然因果

**straw**
英/美 /strɔː/
n. 秸秆；麦秆
[义] The dry stems of cereal plants after the grain has been harvested; used for bedding and mulch. 谷物收粒后干燥茎秆；用作垫料与覆盖（straw bale 秸秆捆, straw mulch 秸秆覆盖, wheat straw 麦秆）。不可数。
[例] ① Straw was spread between the strawberry rows to keep fruit clean. 草莓行间铺秸秆以保持果实清洁。 ② She mulched the garlic bed with a thick layer of straw. 她用厚秸秆覆盖大蒜床。
[搭] straw bale 秸秆捆, straw mulch 秸秆覆盖, wheat straw 麦秆, straw bedding 秸秆垫料
[联] wheat 小麦, grain 谷物, harvest 收获, mulch 覆盖, bale 捆, bedding 垫料, field 田
[记] 不可数。straw 亦指「吸管、稻草人」——与秸秆义同形；本章取农业秸秆义
